# hennery — P1: the PWA and device Settings

- **Plan:** P1; 2026-10-26 (identifier/date assigned by the fleet parent).
- **Design:** [Phone, Web Push and PWA](../specs/2026-10-14-phone-design.md), §§3–5, 6.1, 7–8, 10 and 12.
- **Base:** `37fc7488`, including design PR #135, W1, C1, distribution design #141 and D1 #146. The D1 protocol/schema/generated TypeScript additions are retained. The rebase completed without conflicts; both D1 and P1 README entries remain.
- **Built commit:** `dbbd3b51`; tree `a42ebafaae870041c11a65ba2c0705561c7976ec`. The implementation below is extracted verbatim from its eight task commits. Code wins over task prose.
- **Review:** Codex gpt-6-astra, on the maintainer's behalf, reviewed `6f686151..96a065fc`: **REJECT**, no Critical, four Important and one Minor. The parent subsequently **CONFIRMED all five fixes** and reported CI **15/15 at `88ac41e2`** on draft PR #142. The rebase onto D1 and local rechecks below do not claim new CI or release approval. Cache isolation/digests, activation, contact-only PATCH, payload privacy and CSP passed the earlier review.

## Execution status (2026-10-15)

Built on `plan/phone-p1`, one tests-only commit seen RED followed by one feature commit per task. The five review fixes were folded into their owning task commits using non-interactive fixup/autosquash with signing disabled, and independently confirmed. The branch is now rebased onto `37fc7488`; the embedded blocks and eight expected trees reflect this base. Historical full native/replay evidence and the new generator/web/browser rechecks are distinguished below. Stopped for the parent's review, with no deployment.

## Scope

Four ordered tasks. P1 owns the root manifest and generated PNG icons; digest-verified static-shell install and natural updates; bounded worker registration and offline UI; IndexedDB registration generations and fenced rotation; explicit step-up/permission enable, renewal, disable; push display; capability-gated push/login/hat/contact Settings and mobile sign-out. Reuse existing Rust-owned DTOs, subscriptions, session binding and hat policies. No collector migration.

**Out:** P2 owns notification click navigation/readiness, pending deep links and bounded question reads, mobile answer refinements, gateway invalidation relay and vendor/installed-iOS phone-loop acceptance. The worker displays notices but does not implement click navigation here. Full passkey/public-URL/diagnostics Settings remains 4d's follow-up. No deployment before the early-tester gate or parent's authorization. The open lock-screen question does not block P1; the default remains opening a card and answering in the app.

## Decisions this plan makes where the design permits a choice

1. Use natural activation only: show Update available, ask the operator to close all windows and reopen. No Update now button, skipWaiting, reload or clients.claim. This preserves edits without introducing the P2 readiness handshake.
2. Generate generic PNG icons as code, not remote artwork. Their h stays inside the maskable safe circle; there is no owner/hat identity.
3. Install into a fresh UUID cache attempt. A completion marker is written last; selection requires its matching build id. Every response has a build-time SHA-256, expected MIME, same origin, successful status and no redirect. A failed install deletes only that attempt; retry cannot overwrite an active cache. A worker restart cannot select partial staging writes.
4. One IndexedDB record per origin registration, with generation, enabled intent, confirmed row/endpoint/key, at most one bounded rotation and a short lease. External effects also hold one origin-wide Web Lock, shared by windows/workers, never stolen on lease expiry. No IndexedDB transaction spans a browser/network await. Disable records intent immediately, then waits for external ownership and checks its token before DELETE, unsubscribe and notification close. Repeated disables share a cleanup owner. Enable captures intent before queuing; a later disable cancels it even after the lease expires. Reconciliation uses the same lock, so it cannot mistake an active renewal gap for loss. Foreground row reads carry their pre-request generation.
5. Explicit enable is two taps when confirmation is needed: finish passkey-first step-up, then request permission directly in the new tap. Each HTTP attempt has its own ten-second deadline, cleared before human confirmation; its retry checks ownership again. Default requests retain synchronous fetch startup and caller cancellation. Only a successful POST confirms identity. Renew/rotate preserves the original login binding; missing rows require explicit enable. Worker permission inspection stays outside the critical section; after it resolves, the worker rechecks generation/id/endpoint and owns any subscribe and stale-result cleanup.
6. Disable/logout retains a disabled tombstone, scrubs capability/rotation material and closes notifications. Server and browser failures remain distinct. Confirmed logout clears drafts/images/sends, unmounts question/settings stores and broadcasts to same-origin windows; expiry preserves drafts. Cleanup failure is shown separately after server sign-out; a later successful cleanup clears a timeout warning, including a delayed timeout report.

## Maintainer review fixes

| Finding | Deterministic production-function regression | Fix owner |
|---|---|---|
| Important 1 — stale disable effects | Hold DELETE beyond 12 s, queue a second-window enable, then release; also take over after subscription/notification reads and before each destructive step. Queued pre-disable intent stays cancelled after expiry. | Task 3: Web Lock plus owner token checks; queued intent generation |
| Important 2 — renewal gap | Hold subscribe after unsubscribe, reconcile from another window, advance beyond the lease, then release. Renewal and reconciliation both finish with the confirmed replacement. | Task 3: reconciliation shares external ownership |
| Important 3 — worker resubscribe | Pause permission, complete disable, resume; separately deliver a late subscribe after completed cleanup and preserve a newer same-endpoint binding. Hold a rotation RPC across lease expiry. | Task 4: recheck after permission, own subscribe/RPC, clean stale results and release on exceptions |
| Important 4 — human time in deadline | Return step-up-required, wait 15 s for confirmation, then retry with a fresh live signal; cancel during confirmation and check no retried POST. Retry still times out after its own 10 s. | Task 3: per-attempt transport deadlines and pre-send owner hook |
| Minor 5 — false logout warning | Overlap two real cleanup calls; hold cleanup beyond the outer deadline; independently delay the timeout report until after success. | Task 3: coalesced cleanup plus success-aware warning correction |

The first review regressions were observed RED against the production functions: 8 failed / 31 passed in `review-red.log`; coalescing/late cleanup added 3 failed / 25 passed in `review-extra-red.log`. Additional timeout ordering, queued intent and default-fetch timing regressions were each observed RED, then GREEN and BITE. The first worker cleanup probes missed because the page later removed the replacement; a completed-cleanup test now proves worker cleanup independently. No test, type or rule was weakened. Two old stale-completion tests now restore a newer generation directly because live operations serialize; their preservation/removal assertions remain.

Web Locks are exposed on both window and worker navigators and retain ownership for the callback lifetime; see [W3C Web Locks](https://www.w3.org/TR/web-locks/). [WebKit's Safari 15.4 release](https://webkit.org/blog/12445/new-webkit-features-in-safari-15-4/) documents support before the design's iOS baseline. Missing lock support disables notification enable and fails external effects closed; mobile sign-out remains available.

## Global Constraints

- Same strict script CSP plus explicit worker-src/manifest-src; a restrictive worker response CSP. No inline script, eval, CDN or blob worker.
- Static allowlist only. API/auth, gateway proxy, attachments/logos and non-GET requests are not intercepted. Controlled app routes get one installed build's shell. No transcript, question, answer, credentials or notification body enters CacheStorage or the push journal.
- Never auto-POST subscriptions on launch. Remembered id, browser subscription, permission and loaded VAPID key must agree; this_device alone does not identify a physical device.
- Rotation uses ordinary same-origin credentials/Origin, a ten-second fetch deadline, twelve-second journal lease, an origin Web Lock for live external effects, and bounded retry work. 401 retains sign-in repair, 404/other permanent refusal stops repair, lost success/404 requires explicit repair. No worker permission prompt or permanent timer.
- Per-hat policies remain global across devices, independently muted/generic/details. Fixed example preview only. Contact PATCH sends contact alone, never unchanged public_url.
- All commands run in plain `nix develop -c` from their worktree. Scratch HOME/XDG/config directories isolate test/tool writes; targets are never shared between worktrees. No GitHub action by the child.

## Review Focus

Review cache completion/selection, every digest/MIME/origin check, natural activation and eviction; browser-operation leases, endpoint chain/lost response, disable/renew/enable races and stale row-list generations; real permission gesture and server step-up retry; unsubscribe/logout partial failures; per-hat privacy and generated DTO reuse. Review the two-boundary push mock honestly: emitted worker/PushManager logic is covered, vendor transport and installed iOS are not claimed. The coherent-overlay and DEFERRED snapshot notes remain P2's: P1 touches no session-view reads.

**Reading the steps:** Create makes a new file; Replace the whole overwrites it. Each four-backtick code block is the exact complete file, with its final newline. Apply blocks in order. The tree after each step must equal the named tests/feature commit. Extraction must report a positive block count before tree matching means anything. The replay reads these blocks, not git patches or the original source files.

Run the targeted RED command after Step 1. After Step 2 run its targeted tests and the full gate: fmt, both clippies, workspace tests, both real generators, frozen web install, typecheck/test/build; run Playwright after the finished browser task. Use the repo's foreground long-run log/session protocol. Record actual exits and counts. Push the assigned branch after each finished task; the parent handles reviews/PR/CI.

Create `web/src/pwa/shell.test.ts`:

````typescript
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
import { appRoute, installShell, selectShell, shellResponse, type StaticEntry } from './shell'

// The same Cache API calls as the worker, with separate cache instances.
function storage() {
  const data = new Map<string, Map<string, Response>>()
  const api = {
    open: vi.fn(async (name: string) => {
      if (!data.has(name)) data.set(name, new Map())
      const rows = data.get(name)!
      return { put: async (key: string, r: Response) => { rows.set(key, r) },
        match: async (key: string) => rows.get(key)?.clone() }
    }),
    keys: async () => [...data.keys()],
    delete: vi.fn(async (name: string) => data.delete(name)),
  }
  return { api: api as unknown as CacheStorage, data }
}
const entry: StaticEntry = { url: '/index.html', mime: 'text/html', digest: 'good' }
const deps = { origin: 'https://app.example', digest: async () => 'good' }
const response = () => new Response('shell', { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'text/html', 'Content-Security-Policy': "script-src 'self'" } })

describe('verified static shell', () => {
  it('selects only a completed cache for this build, preserving response headers', async () => {
    const { api } = storage()
    const fetcher = vi.fn(async () => response()) as unknown as typeof fetch
    await installShell(api, fetcher, [entry], 'v1', 'attempt1', deps)
    expect(fetcher).toHaveBeenCalledWith('https://app.example/index.html', expect.objectContaining({ credentials: 'omit', redirect: 'error', cache: 'no-store' }))
    const cache = await selectShell(api, 'v1')
    expect(cache).not.toBeNull()
    const r = await shellResponse(api, 'v1', '/sessions/a', true, [entry])
    expect(await r?.text()).toBe('shell')
    expect(r?.headers.get('Content-Security-Policy')).toBe("script-src 'self'")
    expect(await selectShell(api, 'v2')).toBeNull()
    await api.open('hennery-shell-v2-partial')
    expect(await selectShell(api, 'v2')).toBeNull()
  })
  it.each(['digest', 'mime', 'status', 'redirect', 'foreign', 'fetch'])('rejects %s without touching the active cache', async (fault) => {
    const { api, data } = storage()
    await installShell(api, async () => response(), [entry], 'v1', 'active', deps)
    const fetcher = async () => {
      if (fault === 'fetch') throw new Error('offline')
      const r = fault === 'status' ? new Response('missing', { status: 404 }) : response()
      if (fault === 'mime') r.headers.set('Content-Type', 'text/plain')
      if (fault === 'redirect') Object.defineProperty(r, 'redirected', { value: true })
      if (fault === 'foreign') Object.defineProperty(r, 'url', { value: 'https://evil.example/index.html' })
      return r
    }
    await expect(installShell(api, fetcher, [entry], 'v1', 'retry', { ...deps, digest: async () => fault === 'digest' ? 'bad' : 'good' })).rejects.toThrow()
    expect([...data.keys()]).toEqual(['hennery-shell-v1-active'])
    expect(await selectShell(api, 'v1')).not.toBeNull()
  })
  it('rejects a mixed build and drops every partial write', async () => {
    const { api, data } = storage()
    let call = 0
    await expect(installShell(api, async () => response(), [entry, { ...entry, url: '/theme.js' }], 'v1', 'mix', { ...deps, digest: async () => ++call === 1 ? 'good' : 'bad' })).rejects.toThrow()
    expect(data.size).toBe(0)
  })
  it('serves only exact static URLs and controlled routes, never APIs or queries', async () => {
    const { api } = storage()
    await installShell(api, async () => response(), [entry], 'v1', 'active', deps)
    for (const path of ['/api/auth/login', '/api/sessions', '/mcp/proxy', '/setup', '/login', '/oauth/callback', '/index.html?x=1', '/logos/a', '/unknown']) {
      expect(await shellResponse(api, 'v1', path, true, [entry])).toBeNull()
    }
    expect(await shellResponse(api, 'v1', '/index.html', false, [entry])).not.toBeNull()
    expect(await shellResponse(api, 'v1', '/sessions/a?pending=p', true, [entry])).not.toBeNull()
  })
  it.each(['/sessions', '/sessions/s-1', '/new', '/hosts', '/hats', '/settings', '/mcp'])('recognizes app route %s', (path) => expect(appRoute(path)).toBe(true))
  it.each(['/api', '/login', '/setup', '/mcp/', '/mcp/mount', '/sessions/a/b', '/sessions/%2f', '/sessions/..', '//sessions'])('bypasses %s', (path) => expect(appRoute(path)).toBe(false))
})
````

Replace the whole of `crates/hennery-kernel/src/capabilities.rs`:

````rust
//! `GET /api/capabilities` (kernel spec §8; plan 4b): which mode this
//! collector runs in, and which features it has switched on. The web UI
//! shows the views of its mode (frontend spec §2) and hides a screen whose
//! feature is off.
//!
//! Behind the session cookie: the shell asks for it first, so a 401 here is
//! what sends a signed-out browser to the login screen. The login and setup
//! screens never ask, so they render in either mode.

use crate::operator::Operator;
use axum::routing::get;
use axum::{Json, Router};
use hennery_proto::rest::{CapabilitiesResponse, DeploymentMode};
use std::sync::Arc;

/// The one place the mode is decided. Always the whole cockpit until the
/// standalone gateway (plan 8g) adds its switch here.
pub fn mode() -> DeploymentMode {
    DeploymentMode::Full
}

/// The one place features are switched on: each gateway part adds its name
/// when its API lands (`mcp_connections`, `mcp_stdio`, `mcp_oauth`,
/// `mcp_clients`). Plan 8a's connections API (`/api/mcp/connections`) is
/// served, alongside stdio servers and plan 8f's OAuth (`…/authorize`, the callback, the
/// pre-registered client, the probe).
pub fn features() -> Vec<String> {
    vec![
        "mcp_connections".to_owned(),
        "mcp_stdio".to_owned(),
        "mcp_oauth".to_owned(),
        "web_push".to_owned(),
    ]
}

/// What `GET /api/capabilities` answers.
pub fn current() -> CapabilitiesResponse {
    CapabilitiesResponse {
        mode: mode(),
        features: features(),
    }
}

/// The route, behind the browser rules and the session cookie.
pub fn router(operator: Arc<Operator>) -> Router {
    crate::auth::operator_only(
        Router::new().route("/api/capabilities", get(|| async { Json(current()) })),
        operator,
    )
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn the_collector_is_the_whole_cockpit_with_the_gateways_connections() {
        assert_eq!(
            serde_json::to_value(current()).unwrap(),
            serde_json::json!({"mode": "full", "features": ["mcp_connections", "mcp_stdio", "mcp_oauth", "web_push"]})
        );
    }

    /// The other mode, as the web UI reads it (`lib/views.ts`).
    #[test]
    fn the_gateway_mode_is_named_gateway() {
        assert_eq!(
            serde_json::to_value(DeploymentMode::Gateway).unwrap(),
            serde_json::json!("gateway")
        );
    }
}
````

Replace the whole of `crates/hennery-kernel/src/csp.rs`:

````rust
//! Kernel spec §7.2: every HTML response carries the
//! `Content-Security-Policy`. One layer over the whole router sets it, so a
//! page cannot be added without it.

use axum::http::{HeaderValue, header};
use axum::response::Response;

/// Kernel spec §7.2's policy. No page has an inline script, so it names no
/// hash: the web UI's theme bootstrap is a file of its own (`/theme.js`).
/// Everything else comes from this origin: scripts, styles, fonts, API
/// calls and form targets (plan 4b).
pub const POLICY: &str = "script-src 'self'; worker-src 'self'; manifest-src 'self'; default-src 'self'; connect-src 'self'; img-src 'self' data: blob:; \
     object-src 'none'; frame-ancestors 'none'; base-uri 'none'; form-action 'self'";

/// The policy of a hat's logo (plan 4d-B2; the security review's A9): it
/// is served from this origin, so opened as a page it must still run
/// nothing, load nothing and be framed nowhere, whatever it holds.
pub const LOGO_POLICY: &str = "default-src 'none'; sandbox; frame-ancestors 'none'";

/// The worker can execute and fetch only from its own origin.
pub const WORKER_POLICY: &str = "default-src 'none'; script-src 'self'; connect-src 'self'";

/// Set `POLICY` on a `text/html` response. Use as
/// `axum::middleware::map_response(csp::on_html)`, outermost.
pub async fn on_html(mut response: Response) -> Response {
    let is_html = response
        .headers()
        .get(header::CONTENT_TYPE)
        .and_then(|t| t.to_str().ok())
        .is_some_and(|t| t.trim_start().to_ascii_lowercase().starts_with("text/html"));
    if is_html {
        response
            .headers_mut()
            .insert(header::CONTENT_SECURITY_POLICY, HeaderValue::from_static(POLICY));
    }
    response
}
````

Replace the whole of `crates/hennery-kernel/src/web.rs`:

````rust
//! The web UI, embedded at build time (`build.rs`; kernel spec §7): the
//! router's fallback, so it answers only paths no route claims.
//!
//! - `/api` and `/api/…`, and `/mcp/…` (the gateway's proxy, umbrella §8):
//!   a JSON 404, whatever the method. Never the app.
//! - Any method but `GET` and `HEAD`: a JSON 404.
//! - A file of the build: the file. Hashed files (`/assets/…`) are cached
//!   for good; the rest revalidate with their ETag.
//! - A missing file under `/assets/`: a plain 404, so a stale chunk fails
//!   rather than running the page as a script.
//! - Anything else: `index.html`, whose router shows the route.
//!
//! The page holds no data, so the fallback sits outside the browser rules
//! and the cookie: a link opened from another site loads the app, and the
//! app's API calls are where those rules apply. Every answer is
//! `nosniff`; every file and page is `no-referrer`, `/index.html` asked for
//! by name included, and `/setup`'s `no-store` (kernel spec §3.1). The `Content-Security-Policy` comes from
//! `csp::on_html` over the whole router.

use crate::auth_api::error;
use axum::http::{HeaderMap, HeaderValue, Method, StatusCode, Uri, header};
use axum::response::{IntoResponse, Response};

/// One file of the build, as `build.rs` embeds it.
struct Asset {
    path: &'static str,
    body: &'static [u8],
    content_type: &'static str,
    etag: &'static str,
}

include!(concat!(env!("OUT_DIR"), "/web_assets.rs"));

/// How long a hashed file may be cached: its name changes with its bytes.
const IMMUTABLE: &str = "public, max-age=31536000, immutable";

/// The router's fallback.
pub async fn serve(method: Method, uri: Uri, headers: HeaderMap) -> Response {
    let path = uri.path();
    if is_reserved(path) || (method != Method::GET && method != Method::HEAD) {
        let mut response = error(StatusCode::NOT_FOUND, "not_found", "no such route");
        response
            .headers_mut()
            .insert(header::X_CONTENT_TYPE_OPTIONS, HeaderValue::from_static("nosniff"));
        return response;
    }
    if let Some(asset) = find(path) {
        let cache = if path.starts_with("/assets/") {
            IMMUTABLE
        } else {
            "no-cache"
        };
        return file(asset, cache, &headers);
    }
    if path.starts_with("/assets/") || matches!(path, "/sw.js" | "/manifest.webmanifest") {
        return (
            StatusCode::NOT_FOUND,
            [(header::X_CONTENT_TYPE_OPTIONS, "nosniff")],
            "not found",
        )
            .into_response();
    }
    let index = find("/index.html").expect("the build has an index.html");
    if is_setup(path) {
        file(index, "no-store", &HeaderMap::new())
    } else {
        file(index, "no-cache", &headers)
    }
}

/// Paths the app never answers: the API, and the gateway's proxy.
fn is_reserved(path: &str) -> bool {
    path == "/api" || path.starts_with("/api/") || path.starts_with("/mcp/")
}

/// The page a setup link opens: never cached (kernel spec §3.1).
fn is_setup(path: &str) -> bool {
    path == "/setup" || path.starts_with("/setup/")
}

fn find(path: &str) -> Option<&'static Asset> {
    FILES.iter().find(|a| a.path == path)
}

fn file(asset: &'static Asset, cache: &'static str, request: &HeaderMap) -> Response {
    let fresh = cache == "no-cache"
        && request
            .get(header::IF_NONE_MATCH)
            .and_then(|v| v.to_str().ok())
            .is_some_and(|tags| tags.split(',').any(|t| t.trim() == asset.etag || t.trim() == "*"));
    // A 304 names no type: the cached copy keeps its own.
    let mut response = if fresh {
        StatusCode::NOT_MODIFIED.into_response()
    } else {
        ([(header::CONTENT_TYPE, asset.content_type)], asset.body).into_response()
    };
    let headers = response.headers_mut();
    headers.insert(header::CACHE_CONTROL, HeaderValue::from_static(cache));
    headers.insert(header::X_CONTENT_TYPE_OPTIONS, HeaderValue::from_static("nosniff"));
    headers.insert(header::REFERRER_POLICY, HeaderValue::from_static("no-referrer"));
    if asset.path == "/sw.js" {
        headers.insert(
            header::CONTENT_SECURITY_POLICY,
            HeaderValue::from_static(crate::csp::WORKER_POLICY),
        );
    }
    if cache != "no-store" {
        headers.insert(header::ETAG, HeaderValue::from_static(asset.etag));
    }
    response
}

/// Whether the real UI is embedded, not the placeholder.
pub fn ui_embedded() -> bool {
    EMBEDDED
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn the_api_and_the_gateways_proxy_are_reserved() {
        for path in ["/api", "/api/", "/api/nope", "/mcp/slug", "/mcp/"] {
            assert!(is_reserved(path), "{path}");
        }
        for path in ["/", "/apis", "/mcp", "/sessions/s-1", "/setup"] {
            assert!(!is_reserved(path), "{path}");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_build_has_an_index() {
        let index = find("/index.html").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(index.content_type, "text/html; charset=utf-8");
        assert!(index.etag.starts_with('"') && index.etag.len() == 18, "{}", index.etag);
    }
}
````

````rust
//! `GET /api/capabilities` over HTTP (kernel spec §8; plan 4b). Its cookie
//! and browser rules are pinned by `auth.rs`'s route table.

use hennery_kernel::hosts::Hosts;
use hennery_kernel::operator::Operator;
use hennery_proto::rest::{CapabilitiesResponse, DeploymentMode};
use hennery_sessions::AppState;
use hennery_sessions::store::Store;

/// The signed-in owner learns the mode and the features switched on, and
/// nothing else: today the whole cockpit, with the gateway's connections.
#[tokio::test]
async fn the_owner_learns_the_mode_and_the_features() {
    let listener = tokio::net::TcpListener::bind("127.0.0.1:0").await.unwrap();
    let addr = listener.local_addr().unwrap();
    let state = AppState::new(
        Store::open_in_memory().unwrap(),
        Hosts::open_in_memory().unwrap(),
        Operator::open_in_memory().unwrap(),
    );
    let client = hennery_testkit::operator_client(&state.operator);
    tokio::spawn(hennery_sessions::serve(listener, state));
    let resp = client
        .get(format!("http://{addr}/api/capabilities"))
        .send()
        .await
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(resp.status(), 200);
    let body: serde_json::Value = resp.json().await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        body,
        serde_json::json!({"mode": "full", "features": ["mcp_connections", "mcp_stdio", "mcp_oauth", "web_push"]})
    );
    let typed: CapabilitiesResponse = serde_json::from_value(body).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(typed.mode, DeploymentMode::Full);
}
````

Replace the whole of `crates/hennery-testkit/tests/web.rs`:

````rust
//! The embedded web UI over HTTP (kernel spec §3.1, §7, §7.2; plan 4b): the
//! router's fallback serves the app for every path no route claims, never
//! for the API or the gateway's proxy, with the cache and security headers
//! each answer needs. These tests hold for both builds: the real UI (CI,
//! `HENNERY_WEB_REQUIRE=1`) and the placeholder (no `web/dist`).

use hennery_kernel::hosts::Hosts;
use hennery_kernel::operator::Operator;
use hennery_proto::rest::ApiError;
use hennery_sessions::AppState;
use hennery_sessions::store::Store;
use std::net::SocketAddr;

/// What only the placeholder page carries (`build.rs`).
const PLACEHOLDER_MARKER: &str = "hennery-web-ui-not-embedded";

async fn start() -> SocketAddr {
    let listener = tokio::net::TcpListener::bind("127.0.0.1:0").await.unwrap();
    let addr = listener.local_addr().unwrap();
    let state = AppState::new(
        Store::open_in_memory().unwrap(),
        Hosts::open_in_memory().unwrap(),
        Operator::open_in_memory().unwrap(),
    );
    tokio::spawn(hennery_sessions::serve(listener, state));
    addr
}

fn header(resp: &reqwest::Response, name: &str) -> Option<String> {
    resp.headers().get(name).map(|v| v.to_str().unwrap().to_string())
}

async fn get(addr: SocketAddr, path: &str) -> reqwest::Response {
    reqwest::get(format!("http://{addr}{path}")).await.unwrap()
}

/// Every route the app shows (frontend spec §2), and any other path, is
/// `index.html`: the same page, uncached but revalidated, sending no
/// `Referer`, under the `Content-Security-Policy`.
#[tokio::test]
async fn every_other_path_is_the_app() {
    let addr = start().await;
    let index = get(addr, "/").await.text().await.unwrap();
    for path in [
        "/",
        "/login?next=%2Fhosts",
        "/sessions",
        "/sessions/s-1",
        "/new",
        "/hosts",
        "/mcp",
        "/hats",
        "/settings",
        "/no/such/page",
        "/setup.js",
        "/index.html",
    ] {
        let resp = get(addr, path).await;
        assert_eq!(resp.status(), 200, "{path}");
        assert_eq!(
            header(&resp, "content-type").as_deref(),
            Some("text/html; charset=utf-8"),
            "{path}"
        );
        assert_eq!(header(&resp, "cache-control").as_deref(), Some("no-cache"), "{path}");
        assert_eq!(
            header(&resp, "referrer-policy").as_deref(),
            Some("no-referrer"),
            "{path}"
        );
        assert_eq!(
            header(&resp, "x-content-type-options").as_deref(),
            Some("nosniff"),
            "{path}"
        );
        assert_eq!(
            header(&resp, "content-security-policy").as_deref(),
            Some(hennery_kernel::csp::POLICY),
            "{path}"
        );
        assert!(header(&resp, "etag").is_some(), "{path}");
        assert_eq!(resp.text().await.unwrap(), index, "{path}");
    }
}

/// The API and the gateway's proxy are never the app: an unknown path
/// there is a JSON 404, whatever the method. A method other than `GET` or
/// `HEAD` anywhere else is one too.
#[tokio::test]
async fn the_api_and_the_proxy_are_never_the_app() {
    let addr = start().await;
    let client = reqwest::Client::new();
    for (method, path) in [
        ("GET", "/api"),
        ("GET", "/api/"),
        ("GET", "/api/no-such-route"),
        ("GET", "/api/sessions/s-1/no-such-route"),
        ("POST", "/api/no-such-route"),
        ("GET", "/mcp/some-server"),
        ("POST", "/mcp/some-server"),
        ("POST", "/sessions"),
        ("PUT", "/hosts"),
        ("DELETE", "/"),
    ] {
        let resp = client
            .request(method.parse().unwrap(), format!("http://{addr}{path}"))
            .send()
            .await
            .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(resp.status(), 404, "{method} {path}");
        assert!(
            header(&resp, "content-type").is_some_and(|t| t.starts_with("application/json")),
            "{method} {path}"
        );
        assert_eq!(
            header(&resp, "x-content-type-options").as_deref(),
            Some("nosniff"),
            "{method} {path}"
        );
        assert_eq!(
            resp.json::<ApiError>().await.unwrap().code,
            "not_found",
            "{method} {path}"
        );
    }
}

/// The app holds no data, so it is outside the browser rules: a link
/// opened from another site (mail, chat) loads it. `HEAD` answers as `GET`
/// does, without the body.
#[tokio::test]
async fn a_link_from_another_site_loads_the_app() {
    let addr = start().await;
    let client = reqwest::Client::new();
    let resp = client
        .get(format!("http://{addr}/sessions/s-1"))
        .header("sec-fetch-site", "cross-site")
        .header("origin", "https://elsewhere.example")
        .send()
        .await
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(resp.status(), 200);
    let head = client.head(format!("http://{addr}/hosts")).send().await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(head.status(), 200);
    assert_eq!(header(&head, "cache-control").as_deref(), Some("no-cache"));
    assert!(head.bytes().await.unwrap().is_empty());
}

/// Kernel spec §3.1: the page a setup link opens is never cached and sends
/// no `Referer` onwards. The token stays in the fragment, which no request
/// carries.
#[tokio::test]
async fn the_setup_page_is_never_cached_nor_referred() {
    let addr = start().await;
    for path in ["/setup", "/setup/"] {
        let resp = get(addr, path).await;
        assert_eq!(resp.status(), 200, "{path}");
        assert_eq!(header(&resp, "cache-control").as_deref(), Some("no-store"), "{path}");
        assert_eq!(
            header(&resp, "referrer-policy").as_deref(),
            Some("no-referrer"),
            "{path}"
        );
        assert_eq!(
            header(&resp, "x-content-type-options").as_deref(),
            Some("nosniff"),
            "{path}"
        );
        assert_eq!(
            header(&resp, "content-security-policy").as_deref(),
            Some(hennery_kernel::csp::POLICY),
            "{path}"
        );
        assert_eq!(header(&resp, "etag"), None, "{path}");
    }
}

/// `index.html` revalidates: its ETag, or `*`, answers 304, with no body
/// and no content type (the cached copy keeps its own).
#[tokio::test]
async fn the_page_revalidates_with_its_etag() {
    let addr = start().await;
    let first = get(addr, "/hosts").await;
    let etag = header(&first, "etag").unwrap();
    let client = reqwest::Client::new();
    for tags in [etag.clone(), format!("\"0000\", {etag}"), "*".to_string()] {
        let again = client
            .get(format!("http://{addr}/sessions"))
            .header("if-none-match", &tags)
            .send()
            .await
            .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(again.status(), 304, "{tags}");
        assert_eq!(header(&again, "etag").as_deref(), Some(etag.as_str()), "{tags}");
        assert_eq!(header(&again, "content-type"), None, "{tags}");
        assert!(again.bytes().await.unwrap().is_empty(), "{tags}");
    }
    let stale = client
        .get(format!("http://{addr}/sessions"))
        .header("if-none-match", "\"0000\"")
        .send()
        .await
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(stale.status(), 200);
}

/// The setup page is never revalidated: a cached copy would outlive the
/// token, so even its own ETag or `*` gets the whole page, with no ETag.
#[tokio::test]
async fn the_setup_page_is_never_revalidated() {
    let addr = start().await;
    let etag = header(&get(addr, "/hosts").await, "etag").unwrap();
    let client = reqwest::Client::new();
    for tags in [etag, "*".to_string()] {
        let resp = client
            .get(format!("http://{addr}/setup"))
            .header("if-none-match", &tags)
            .send()
            .await
            .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(resp.status(), 200, "{tags}");
        assert_eq!(header(&resp, "etag"), None, "{tags}");
        assert!(!resp.bytes().await.unwrap().is_empty(), "{tags}");
    }
}

/// `/theme.js` (the real UI only) is not hashed, so it revalidates rather
/// than being cached for good: a new build's theme reaches the browser.
#[tokio::test]
async fn the_theme_script_revalidates() {
    if !hennery_kernel::web::ui_embedded() {
        return;
    }
    let addr = start().await;
    let resp = get(addr, "/theme.js").await;
    assert_eq!(resp.status(), 200);
    assert_eq!(header(&resp, "cache-control").as_deref(), Some("no-cache"));
    assert!(header(&resp, "etag").is_some());
    assert_eq!(
        header(&resp, "content-type").as_deref(),
        Some("text/javascript; charset=utf-8")
    );
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn pwa_files_are_real_assets_or_404_and_the_worker_has_its_own_policy() {
    let addr = start().await;
    let client = reqwest::Client::new();
    for (path, mime) in [
        ("/sw.js", "text/javascript; charset=utf-8"),
        ("/manifest.webmanifest", "application/manifest+json"),
    ] {
        let first = get(addr, path).await;
        if !hennery_kernel::web::ui_embedded() {
            assert_eq!(first.status(), 404);
            continue;
        }
        assert_eq!(first.status(), 200);
        assert_eq!(header(&first, "content-type").as_deref(), Some(mime));
        assert_eq!(header(&first, "cache-control").as_deref(), Some("no-cache"));
        if path == "/sw.js" {
            assert_eq!(
                header(&first, "content-security-policy").as_deref(),
                Some(hennery_kernel::csp::WORKER_POLICY)
            );
        }
        let etag = header(&first, "etag").unwrap();
        let second = client
            .get(format!("http://{addr}{path}"))
            .header("if-none-match", etag)
            .send()
            .await
            .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(second.status(), 304);
    }
    let html = get(addr, "/").await;
    let policy = header(&html, "content-security-policy").unwrap();
    assert!(policy.contains("worker-src 'self'") && policy.contains("manifest-src 'self'"));
    assert!(!policy.contains("unsafe-inline") && !policy.contains("unsafe-eval"));
}

/// A missing hashed file is a plain 404, never the page: a browser that
/// asks for a stale chunk must not run HTML as a script. The real build's
/// own hashed files are cached for good.
#[tokio::test]
async fn hashed_files_are_immutable_and_a_missing_one_is_a_plain_404() {
    let addr = start().await;
    let missing = get(addr, "/assets/index-00000000.js").await;
    assert_eq!(missing.status(), 404);
    assert!(header(&missing, "content-type").is_some_and(|t| t.starts_with("text/plain")));
    assert_eq!(header(&missing, "x-content-type-options").as_deref(), Some("nosniff"));

    let index = get(addr, "/").await.text().await.unwrap();
    let assets: Vec<&str> = index.split('"').filter(|part| part.starts_with("/assets/")).collect();
    assert_eq!(assets.is_empty(), !hennery_kernel::web::ui_embedded(), "{index}");
    for path in assets {
        let resp = get(addr, path).await;
        assert_eq!(resp.status(), 200, "{path}");
        assert_eq!(
            header(&resp, "cache-control").as_deref(),
            Some("public, max-age=31536000, immutable"),
            "{path}"
        );
        assert_eq!(
            header(&resp, "x-content-type-options").as_deref(),
            Some("nosniff"),
            "{path}"
        );
        let kind = header(&resp, "content-type").unwrap();
        assert!(
            (path.ends_with(".js") && kind == "text/javascript; charset=utf-8")
                || (path.ends_with(".css") && kind == "text/css; charset=utf-8"),
            "{path}: {kind}"
        );
    }
}

/// The page runs no inline script and holds no inline style (kernel spec
/// §7.2: `script-src 'self'`, `default-src 'self'`). The placeholder holds
/// no script and no form, takes no secret, and is the only page with the
/// marker release builds grep for.
#[tokio::test]
async fn the_page_runs_no_inline_script() {
    let addr = start().await;
    let index = get(addr, "/").await.text().await.unwrap();
    for tag in index.split("<script").skip(1) {
        let open = &tag[..tag.find('>').unwrap()];
        assert!(open.contains("src=\"/"), "an inline script: <script{open}>");
    }
    assert!(!index.contains("<style"), "{index}");
    if hennery_kernel::web::ui_embedded() {
        assert!(!index.contains(PLACEHOLDER_MARKER));
        assert!(index.contains("<script src=\"/theme.js\"></script>"), "{index}");
    } else {
        assert!(index.contains(PLACEHOLDER_MARKER));
        assert!(!index.contains("<script") && !index.contains("<form"), "{index}");
        assert!(index.contains("hennery admin setup-url") && index.contains("/api/setup"));
    }
}

/// A build told to require the UI (CI, release builds) embeds it.
#[test]
fn a_build_that_requires_the_ui_embeds_it() {
    if option_env!("HENNERY_WEB_REQUIRE") == Some("1") {
        assert!(hennery_kernel::web::ui_embedded());
    }
}
````

Create `web/pwa-build.ts`:

````typescript
// Runs after Vite has emitted every chunk. The worker isn't part of its own
// precache. No network-generated URL or query parameter enters this list.
import { createHash } from 'node:crypto'
import { readFileSync, readdirSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs'
import { resolve } from 'node:path'
import { deflateSync } from 'node:zlib'
import { build, type Plugin } from 'vite'

const hash = (bytes: string | Uint8Array) => createHash('sha256').update(bytes).digest('hex')
function png(size: number): Buffer {
  const crc = (bytes: Buffer) => {
    let c = 0xffffffff
    for (const b of bytes) { c ^= b; for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) c = (c >>> 1) ^ ((c & 1) ? 0xedb88320 : 0) }
    return (c ^ 0xffffffff) >>> 0
  }
  const chunk = (kind: string, data: Buffer) => {
    const body = Buffer.concat([Buffer.from(kind), data]), length = Buffer.alloc(4), check = Buffer.alloc(4)
    length.writeUInt32BE(data.length); check.writeUInt32BE(crc(body))
    return Buffer.concat([length, body, check])
  }
  const header = Buffer.alloc(13)
  header.writeUInt32BE(size); header.writeUInt32BE(size, 4); header[8] = 8; header[9] = 6
  const pixels = Buffer.alloc((size * 4 + 1) * size)
  // A simple h inside the central 60%, safe within a maskable icon's circle.
  for (let y = 0; y < size; y++) for (let x = 0; x < size; x++) {
    const u = x / size, v = y / size
    const mark = (u > .30 && u < .40 && v > .25 && v < .75)
      || (u > .40 && u < .65 && v > .46 && v < .56)
      || (u > .55 && u < .65 && v > .50 && v < .75)
    const offset = y * (size * 4 + 1) + 1 + x * 4
    pixels.set(mark ? [245, 245, 240, 255] : [30, 39, 35, 255], offset)
  }
  return Buffer.concat([Buffer.from([137,80,78,71,13,10,26,10]), chunk('IHDR', header), chunk('IDAT', deflateSync(pixels)), chunk('IEND', Buffer.alloc(0))])
}

export function pwaBuild(): Plugin {
  let root = '', out = ''
  return {
    name: 'hennery-pwa', apply: 'build',
    configResolved(config) { root = config.root; out = resolve(root, config.build.outDir) },
    async closeBundle() {
      for (const size of [180, 192, 512]) writeFileSync(resolve(out, `icon-${size}.png`), png(size))
      writeFileSync(resolve(out, 'icon-maskable.png'), png(512))
      const mime: Record<string, string> = { html: 'text/html', js: 'text/javascript', css: 'text/css', svg: 'image/svg+xml', png: 'image/png', webmanifest: 'application/manifest+json', woff2: 'font/woff2' }
      const entries: { url: string; mime: string; digest: string }[] = []
      const visit = (dir: string, prefix: string) => {
        for (const file of readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true })) {
          if (file.isDirectory()) visit(resolve(dir, file.name), `${prefix}${file.name}/`)
          else if (mime[file.name.split('.').at(-1)!] && file.name !== 'sw.js') entries.push({ url: `${prefix}${file.name}`, mime: mime[file.name.split('.').at(-1)!], digest: hash(readFileSync(resolve(dir, file.name))) })
        }
      }
      visit(out, '/')
      entries.sort((a, b) => a.url.localeCompare(b.url))
      await build({ configFile: false, root, define: { __PRECACHE__: JSON.stringify(entries), __BUILD_ID__: JSON.stringify(hash(JSON.stringify(entries))) },
        build: { outDir: out, emptyOutDir: false, target: 'es2022', lib: { entry: resolve(root, 'worker.js'), name: 'henneryWorker', formats: ['iife'], fileName: () => 'sw.js' } } })
    },
  }
}
````

Replace the whole of `web/src/pwa/shell.test.ts`:

````typescript
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
import { appRoute, installShell, selectShell, shellResponse, type StaticEntry } from './shell'

// The same Cache API calls as the worker, with separate cache instances.
function storage() {
  const data = new Map<string, Map<string, Response>>()
  const api = {
    open: vi.fn(async (name: string) => {
      if (!data.has(name)) data.set(name, new Map())
      const rows = data.get(name)!
      return { put: async (key: string, r: Response) => { rows.set(key, r) },
        match: async (key: string) => rows.get(key)?.clone() }
    }),
    keys: async () => [...data.keys()],
    delete: vi.fn(async (name: string) => data.delete(name)),
  }
  return { api: api as unknown as CacheStorage, data }
}
const entry: StaticEntry = { url: '/index.html', mime: 'text/html', digest: 'good' }
const deps = { origin: 'https://app.example', digest: async () => 'good' }
const response = () => new Response('shell', { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'text/html', 'Content-Security-Policy': "script-src 'self'" } })

describe('verified static shell', () => {
  it('selects only a completed cache for this build, preserving response headers', async () => {
    const { api } = storage()
    const fetcher = vi.fn(async () => response()) as unknown as typeof fetch
    await installShell(api, fetcher, [entry], 'v1', 'attempt1', deps)
    expect(fetcher).toHaveBeenCalledWith('https://app.example/index.html', expect.objectContaining({ credentials: 'omit', redirect: 'error', cache: 'no-store' }))
    const cache = await selectShell(api, 'v1')
    expect(cache).not.toBeNull()
    const r = await shellResponse(api, 'v1', '/sessions/a', true, [entry])
    expect(await r?.text()).toBe('shell')
    expect(r?.headers.get('Content-Security-Policy')).toBe("script-src 'self'")
    expect(await selectShell(api, 'v2')).toBeNull()
    await api.open('hennery-shell-v2-partial')
    expect(await selectShell(api, 'v2')).toBeNull()
  })
  it.each(['digest', 'mime', 'status', 'redirect', 'foreign', 'fetch'])('rejects %s without touching the active cache', async (fault) => {
    const { api, data } = storage()
    await installShell(api, async () => response(), [entry], 'v1', 'active', deps)
    const fetcher = async () => {
      if (fault === 'fetch') throw new Error('offline')
      const r = fault === 'status' ? new Response('missing', { status: 404, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'text/html' } }) : response()
      if (fault === 'mime') r.headers.set('Content-Type', 'text/plain')
      if (fault === 'redirect') Object.defineProperty(r, 'redirected', { value: true })
      if (fault === 'foreign') Object.defineProperty(r, 'url', { value: 'https://evil.example/index.html' })
      return r
    }
    await expect(installShell(api, fetcher, [entry], 'v1', 'retry', { ...deps, digest: async () => fault === 'digest' ? 'bad' : 'good' })).rejects.toThrow()
    expect([...data.keys()]).toEqual(['hennery-shell-v1-active'])
    expect(await selectShell(api, 'v1')).not.toBeNull()
  })
  it('rejects a mixed build and drops every partial write', async () => {
    const { api, data } = storage()
    let call = 0
    await expect(installShell(api, async () => response(), [entry, { ...entry, url: '/theme.js' }], 'v1', 'mix', { ...deps, digest: async () => ++call === 1 ? 'good' : 'bad' })).rejects.toThrow()
    expect(data.size).toBe(0)
  })
  it('does not overwrite an existing attempt even when retrying the same build', async () => {
    const { api } = storage()
    await installShell(api, async () => response(), [entry], 'v1', 'active', deps)
    await expect(installShell(api, async () => response(), [entry], 'v1', 'active', deps)).rejects.toThrow('already exists')
    expect(await (await (await selectShell(api, 'v1'))?.match('/index.html'))?.text()).toBe('shell')
  })
  it('checks the namespace as well as completion, and ignores unexpected cached URLs', async () => {
    const { api } = storage()
    const foreign = await api.open('hennery-shell-v2-other')
    await foreign.put('/__hennery_shell_complete__', new Response('v1'))
    await foreign.put('/index.html', response())
    expect(await selectShell(api, 'v1')).toBeNull()
    await installShell(api, async () => response(), [entry], 'v1', 'active', deps)
    const cache = (await selectShell(api, 'v1'))!
    await cache.put('/api/secret', new Response('secret'))
    expect(await shellResponse(api, 'v1', '/api/secret', false, [entry])).toBeNull()
  })
  it('serves only exact static URLs and controlled routes, never APIs or queries', async () => {
    const { api } = storage()
    await installShell(api, async () => response(), [entry], 'v1', 'active', deps)
    for (const path of ['/api/auth/login', '/api/sessions', '/mcp/proxy', '/setup', '/login', '/oauth/callback', '/index.html?x=1', '/logos/a', '/unknown']) {
      expect(await shellResponse(api, 'v1', path, true, [entry])).toBeNull()
    }
    expect(await shellResponse(api, 'v1', '/index.html', false, [entry])).not.toBeNull()
    expect(await shellResponse(api, 'v1', '/sessions/a?pending=p', true, [entry])).not.toBeNull()
  })
  it.each(['/sessions', '/sessions/s-1', '/new', '/hosts', '/hats', '/settings', '/mcp'])('recognizes app route %s', (path) => expect(appRoute(path)).toBe(true))
  it.each(['/api', '/login', '/setup', '/mcp/', '/mcp/mount', '/sessions/a/b', '/sessions/%2f', '/sessions/..', '//sessions'])('bypasses %s', (path) => expect(appRoute(path)).toBe(false))
})
````

Create `web/src/pwa/shell.ts`:

````typescript
// Static-only worker cache. A unique attempt is never reused; a final marker
// makes a whole cache selectable across worker restarts. Writes are not atomic.
export interface StaticEntry { url: string; mime: string; digest: string }
export const SHELL_PREFIX = 'hennery-shell-'
const COMPLETE = '/__hennery_shell_complete__'

export async function sha256(bytes: ArrayBuffer): Promise<string> {
  return [...new Uint8Array(await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', bytes))].map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('')
}

export async function installShell(
  storage: CacheStorage, fetcher: typeof fetch, entries: StaticEntry[], build: string, attempt: string,
  deps = { origin: location.origin, digest: sha256 },
): Promise<void> {
  const name = `${SHELL_PREFIX}${build}-${attempt}`
  // Fail rather than touch a cache that may belong to a running worker.
  if ((await storage.keys()).includes(name)) throw new Error('cache attempt already exists')
  try {
    const cache = await storage.open(name)
    for (const entry of entries) {
      const url = new URL(entry.url, deps.origin)
      const r = await fetcher(url.href, { credentials: 'omit', redirect: 'error', cache: 'no-store' })
      if (!r.ok || r.redirected || (r.url && new URL(r.url).origin !== deps.origin)
        || r.headers.get('content-type')?.split(';')[0].trim() !== entry.mime
        || await deps.digest(await r.clone().arrayBuffer()) !== entry.digest) throw new Error('shell verification failed')
      await cache.put(entry.url, r)
    }
    await cache.put(COMPLETE, new Response(build))
  } catch (err) {
    await storage.delete(name)
    throw err
  }
}

export async function selectShell(storage: CacheStorage, build: string): Promise<Cache | null> {
  for (const name of await storage.keys()) {
    if (!name.startsWith(`${SHELL_PREFIX}${build}-`)) continue
    const cache = await storage.open(name)
    if (await (await cache.match(COMPLETE))?.text() === build) return cache
  }
  return null
}

export function appRoute(path: string): boolean {
  return ['/sessions', '/new', '/hosts', '/hats', '/settings', '/mcp'].includes(path)
    || /^\/sessions\/[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,128}$/.test(path)
}

export async function shellResponse(storage: CacheStorage, build: string, path: string, navigation: boolean, entries: StaticEntry[]): Promise<Response | null> {
  const key = navigation && appRoute(path.split('?')[0]) ? '/index.html' : path
  if (!entries.some((e) => e.url === key)) return null
  return (await (await selectShell(storage, build))?.match(key)) ?? null
}
````

````typescript
/// <reference types="vitest/config" />
import { defineConfig } from 'vite'
import react from '@vitejs/plugin-react'
import tailwindcss from '@tailwindcss/vite'
import { pwaBuild } from './pwa-build.ts'

// Tests run in a zone with daylight saving time, so the session list's day
// buckets are tested across a 23 h and a 25 h day (lib/time.test.ts). The
// test workers inherit it from this process.
process.env.TZ = 'Europe/Warsaw'

export default defineConfig({
  plugins: [react(), tailwindcss(), pwaBuild()],
  build: { outDir: 'dist', target: 'es2022' },
  test: {
    environment: 'jsdom',
    globals: true,
    setupFiles: './src/test-setup.ts',
    include: ['src/**/*.test.{ts,tsx}'],
    // A loaded machine (CI's 2–4 vCPUs, or parallel runs) takes seconds to
    // import a test's module graph and render a whole app: the 5 s default
    // timed out tests that pass. A real hang still fails, only later.
````

Create `web/worker.js`:

````javascript
import { installShell, shellResponse, SHELL_PREFIX } from './src/pwa/shell'

self.addEventListener('install', (event) => {
  event.waitUntil(installShell(caches, fetch, __PRECACHE__, __BUILD_ID__, crypto.randomUUID()))
})
self.addEventListener('activate', (event) => {
  // Natural activation only, once the old controlled windows have closed.
  // Never claim an existing document or call skipWaiting.
  event.waitUntil((async () => {
    for (const name of await caches.keys()) {
      if (name.startsWith(SHELL_PREFIX) && !name.startsWith(`${SHELL_PREFIX}${__BUILD_ID__}-`)) await caches.delete(name)
    }
  })())
})
self.addEventListener('fetch', (event) => {
  const r = event.request, url = new URL(r.url)
  if (r.method !== 'GET' || url.origin !== location.origin) return
  event.respondWith((async () => {
    const cached = await shellResponse(caches, __BUILD_ID__, url.pathname + url.search, r.mode === 'navigate', __PRECACHE__)
    return cached ?? fetch(r)
  })())
})
````

````typescript
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
import { Journal, type PushRecord, type PushStore } from './journal'
import type { PushSubscribeRequest } from '../generated/protocol'

// Persistent transactional fake: a new Journal has no JS globals to recover.
function store(): PushStore {
  let row: PushRecord = { generation: 0, enabled: false }
  let queue = Promise.resolve()
  return { transact<T>(fn: (value: PushRecord) => T): Promise<T> {
    const result = queue.then(() => { const copy = structuredClone(row); const value = fn(copy); row = copy; return value })
    queue = result.then(() => {}, () => {})
    return result
  } }
}
const subscription = (endpoint: string): PushSubscribeRequest => ({ endpoint, keys: { p256dh: 'key', auth: 'auth' } })
async function enabled(s = store()) {
  const journal = new Journal(s)
  const lease = await journal.beginEnable(0)
  expect(await journal.finishEnable(lease, 'row1', 'https://push.example/a', 'vapid')).toBe(true)
  return { journal, s }
}
const ok = () => new Response(JSON.stringify({ id: 'row1' }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })

describe('origin-local registration journal', () => {
  it('requires an explicit enable, and fences simultaneous enables and stale completions', async () => {
    const s = store(), a = new Journal(s), b = new Journal(s)
    expect(await a.queueRotation(subscription('https://push.example/b'))).toBe(false)
    const lease = await a.beginEnable(0)
    await expect(b.beginEnable(1)).rejects.toThrow('busy')
    await b.disable()
    expect(await a.finishEnable(lease, 'late-id', 'endpoint', 'key')).toBe(false)
    expect(await a.read()).toMatchObject({ enabled: false, id: 'late-id' })
    expect((await a.read()).endpoint).toBeUndefined()
  })
  it('keeps the first server anchor and only the newest replacement across restarts', async () => {
    const { journal, s } = await enabled()
    await journal.queueRotation(subscription('https://push.example/b'), 10)
    await new Journal(s).queueRotation(subscription('https://push.example/c'), 11)
    const calls: string[] = []
    expect(await new Journal(s).attempt(async (_url, init) => { calls.push(String(init?.body)); return ok() }, 12)).toBe('success')
    expect(JSON.parse(calls[0])).toEqual({ old_endpoint: 'https://push.example/a', subscription: subscription('https://push.example/c') })
    expect(await journal.read()).toMatchObject({ endpoint: 'https://push.example/c', enabled: true })
    expect((await journal.read()).rotation).toBeUndefined()
  })
  it('allows one HTTP attempt, advances its anchor while keeping a replacement received in flight', async () => {
    const { journal, s } = await enabled()
    await journal.queueRotation(subscription('https://push.example/b'), 1)
    let resolve!: (r: Response) => void
    const pending = journal.attempt(() => new Promise<Response>((r) => { resolve = r }), 2)
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(resolve).toBeTypeOf('function'))
    const other = new Journal(s)
    expect(await other.attempt(async () => ok(), 3)).toBe('idle')
    await other.queueRotation(subscription('https://push.example/c'), 4)
    resolve(ok())
    expect(await pending).toBe('success')
    expect(await other.read()).toMatchObject({ endpoint: 'https://push.example/b', rotation: { oldEndpoint: 'https://push.example/b', subscription: subscription('https://push.example/c') } })
    expect(await other.attempt(async (_url, init) => { expect(JSON.parse(String(init?.body)).old_endpoint).toBe('https://push.example/b'); return ok() }, 5)).toBe('success')
  })
  it('disable cancels deferred work and cannot be undone by an HTTP success', async () => {
    const { journal } = await enabled()
    await journal.queueRotation(subscription('https://push.example/b'), 1)
    let resolve!: (r: Response) => void
    const pending = journal.attempt(() => new Promise<Response>((r) => { resolve = r }), 2)
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(resolve).toBeTypeOf('function'))
    await journal.disable()
    resolve(ok())
    expect(await pending).toBe('stale')
    expect(await journal.read()).toMatchObject({ enabled: false, generation: 2 })
    expect((await journal.read()).rotation).toBeUndefined()
    expect((await journal.read()).key).toBeUndefined()
    expect(await journal.queueRotation(subscription('https://push.example/c'))).toBe(false)
    expect(await journal.attempt(async () => ok(), 20000)).toBe('idle')
  })
  it.each([401, 404, 400, 403, 409])('keeps 401 recoverable, stops automatic repair on %s otherwise', async (status) => {
    const { journal, s } = await enabled()
    await journal.queueRotation(subscription('https://push.example/b'), 1)
    expect(await journal.attempt(async () => new Response(null, { status }), 2)).toBe(status === 401 ? 'sign_in' : 'reenable')
    const row = await new Journal(s).read()
    expect(row.repair).toBe(status === 401 ? 'sign_in' : 'reenable')
    expect(row.enabled).toBe(status === 401)
    expect(!!row.rotation).toBe(status === 401)
  })
  it.each([429, 500, 'network', 'bad-response'])('retains bounded retry work on %s, honoring Retry-After', async (status) => {
    const { journal, s } = await enabled()
    await journal.queueRotation(subscription('https://push.example/b'), 1)
    const fetcher = async () => {
      if (status === 'network') throw new TypeError('offline')
      if (status === 'bad-response') return new Response('{}', { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })
      return new Response(null, { status: Number(status), headers: { 'Retry-After': '20' } })
    }
    expect(await journal.attempt(fetcher, 2)).toBe('retry')
    const row = await new Journal(s).read()
    expect(row.rotation?.attempts).toBe(1)
    expect(row.rotation?.nextAt).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1002)
    if (status === 429 || status === 500) expect(row.rotation?.nextAt).toBe(20002)
    expect(await new Journal(s).attempt(async () => ok(), 3)).toBe('idle')
    expect(await new Journal(s).attempt(async () => ok(), 70000)).toBe('success')
  })
  it('requires repair on ambiguous lost success/404 rather than recreating a row', async () => {
    const { journal } = await enabled()
    await journal.queueRotation(subscription('https://push.example/b'), 1)
    const fetcher = vi.fn().mockRejectedValueOnce(new TypeError('lost response')).mockResolvedValueOnce(new Response(null, { status: 404 }))
    expect(await journal.attempt(fetcher, 2)).toBe('retry')
    expect(await journal.attempt(fetcher, 2000)).toBe('reenable')
    expect(fetcher.mock.calls.every(([url]) => url === '/api/push/subscriptions/rotate')).toBe(true)
  })
  it('a dead attempt lease expires, and its stale result cannot overwrite a newer success', async () => {
    const { journal, s } = await enabled()
    await journal.queueRotation(subscription('https://push.example/b'), 1)
    let resolve!: (r: Response) => void
    const first = journal.attempt(() => new Promise<Response>((r) => { resolve = r }), 2)
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(resolve).toBeTypeOf('function'))
    expect(await new Journal(s).attempt(async () => ok(), 13000)).toBe('success')
    resolve(ok())
    expect(await first).toBe('stale')
    expect((await journal.read()).endpoint).toBe('https://push.example/b')
  })
  it('scrubs capability material after disable, preserving only the removal id on partial failure', async () => {
    const { journal } = await enabled()
    expect(await journal.disable()).toMatchObject({ id: 'row1', endpoint: 'https://push.example/a' })
    await journal.finishDisable(false, false)
    expect(await journal.read()).toEqual({ generation: 2, enabled: false, id: 'row1', repair: 'remove_server_and_browser' })
    await journal.finishDisable(true, false)
    expect((await journal.read()).repair).toBe('remove_browser')
    await journal.finishDisable(true, true)
    expect(await journal.read()).toEqual({ generation: 2, enabled: false })
  })
})
````

````typescript
import { decodePush, notificationTarget } from './notification'

describe('bounded Rust PushPayload decoder', () => {

    expect(decodePush(JSON.stringify({ title: 'Needs your answer', body: 'Agent text', url: '/sessions/s-1', tag: 's-1', future: true }))).toEqual({ title: 'Needs your answer', body: 'Agent text', url: '/sessions/s-1', tag: 's-1' })
    expect(decodePush(JSON.stringify({ title: 'MCP', body: 'Needs authentication', url: '/mcp', tag: 'mcp-c1' })).url).toBe('/mcp')
  })
  it.each([null, '', '{', '[]', '{}', '{"title":1}', JSON.stringify({ title: 'x'.repeat(257), body: '', url: '/mcp', tag: 'a' }), JSON.stringify({ title: 't', body: 'x'.repeat(2049), url: '/mcp', tag: 'a' }), JSON.stringify({ title: 't', body: '', url: '/mcp', tag: 'x'.repeat(257) }), 'x'.repeat(8193)])('malformed data displays neutral attention without throwing: %s', (text) => {
    expect(decodePush(text)).toEqual({ title: 'hennery needs your attention', body: 'Open hennery to check the current state.', url: '/sessions', tag: 'hennery-attention' })
  })
  it.each(['https://evil.example/sessions/a', '//evil.example/sessions/a', '/sessions/a\\b', '/sessions/%2f', '/sessions/%252f', '/sessions/a#x', '/sessions/a?pending=p&pending=q', '/sessions/a?other=x', '/mcp/mount', '/login', '/sessions/../mcp', '/sessions/a?pending=%2f', '/sessions/a?%70ending=p', '/sessions/a?pending=p&', '/sessions/a?pending=', '/sessions/a?pending=p#f'])('rejects ambiguous target %s', (target) => expect(notificationTarget(target)).toBe('/sessions'))
  it.each(['/sessions', '/sessions/s-1', '/mcp', '/sessions/s-1?pending=p_1'])('accepts rooted target %s', (target) => expect(notificationTarget(target)).toBe(target))
})
````

````typescript
    await journal.finishDisable(false, false, 2)
    expect(await journal.read()).toEqual({ generation: 2, enabled: false, id: 'row1', repair: 'remove_server_and_browser' })
    await journal.finishDisable(true, false, 2)
    expect((await journal.read()).repair).toBe('remove_browser')
    await journal.finishDisable(true, true, 2)
    expect(await journal.read()).toEqual({ generation: 2, enabled: false })
  })
})
````

Create `web/src/pwa/journal.ts`:

````typescript
import type { PushRotateRequest, PushSubscribeRequest } from '../generated/protocol'

interface Rotation {
  oldEndpoint: string; subscription: PushSubscribeRequest; sequence: number
  attempts: number; nextAt: number
}
export interface Lease { token: string; generation: number; until: number }
export interface PushRecord {
  generation: number; enabled: boolean; id?: string; endpoint?: string; key?: string
  rotation?: Rotation; lease?: Lease; repair?: string
}
// Callback MUST be synchronous. The production adapter commits it atomically.
export interface PushStore { transact<T>(fn: (row: PushRecord) => T): Promise<T> }
export type RotationResult = 'idle' | 'success' | 'retry' | 'sign_in' | 'reenable' | 'stale'
const lease = (generation: number, now: number): Lease => ({ generation, token: crypto.randomUUID(), until: now + 12000 })
const same = (a: Lease | undefined, b: Lease) => a?.token === b.token

export class Journal {
  constructor(private readonly store: PushStore) {}
  read(): Promise<PushRecord> { return this.store.transact((r) => structuredClone(r)) }

  beginEnable(now = Date.now()): Promise<Lease> {
    return this.store.transact((r) => {
      if (r.lease && r.lease.until > now) throw new Error('Notification operation busy; retry shortly.')
      const next = lease(r.generation + 1, now)
      Object.keys(r).forEach((k) => delete r[k as keyof PushRecord])
      Object.assign(r, { generation: next.generation, enabled: false, lease: next })
      return next
    })
  }
  finishEnable(attempt: Lease, id: string, endpoint: string, key: string): Promise<boolean> {
    return this.store.transact((r) => {
      if (r.generation !== attempt.generation || !same(r.lease, attempt)) {
        // Disable won. Preserve a scrubbed id so server cleanup can be retried.
        if (!r.enabled && !r.id) r.id = id
        return false
      }
      Object.assign(r, { enabled: true, id, endpoint, key })
      delete r.lease; delete r.repair
      return true
    })
  }
  abortEnable(attempt: Lease): Promise<void> {
    return this.store.transact((r) => {
      if (!same(r.lease, attempt)) return
      delete r.lease
      if (r.generation === attempt.generation) r.repair = 'reenable'
    })
  }
  disable(): Promise<PushRecord> {
    return this.store.transact((r) => {
      const previous = structuredClone(r)
      Object.keys(r).forEach((k) => delete r[k as keyof PushRecord])
      Object.assign(r, { generation: previous.generation + 1, enabled: false, ...(previous.id ? { id: previous.id } : {}) })
      return previous
    })
  }
  finishDisable(server: boolean, browser: boolean, generation: number): Promise<void> {
    return this.store.transact((r) => {
      if (r.enabled || r.generation !== generation) return
      if (server) delete r.id
      if (server && browser) delete r.repair
      else r.repair = server ? 'remove_browser' : browser ? 'remove_server' : 'remove_server_and_browser'
    })
  }
  needsRepair(generation: number): Promise<void> {
    return this.store.transact((r) => {
      if (r.generation !== generation) return
      r.enabled = false; r.repair = 'reenable'; delete r.rotation; delete r.key; delete r.lease
    })
  }
  queueRotation(subscription: PushSubscribeRequest, now = Date.now(), generation?: number): Promise<boolean> {
    return this.store.transact((r) => {
      if (!r.enabled || !r.id || !r.endpoint || (generation !== undefined && r.generation !== generation)) return false
      const previous = r.rotation
      r.rotation = { oldEndpoint: previous?.oldEndpoint ?? r.endpoint, subscription,
        sequence: (previous?.sequence ?? 0) + 1, attempts: previous?.attempts ?? 0, nextAt: previous?.nextAt ?? now }
      return true
    })
  }

  async attempt(fetcher: typeof fetch = fetch, now = Date.now()): Promise<RotationResult> {
    const owned = await this.store.transact((r) => {
      if (!r.enabled || !r.rotation || r.rotation.nextAt > now || (r.lease && r.lease.until > now)) return null
      const attempt = lease(r.generation, now)
      r.lease = attempt
      return { attempt, id: r.id, rotation: structuredClone(r.rotation) }
    })
    if (!owned) return 'idle'
    const { attempt, rotation } = owned
    const body: PushRotateRequest = { old_endpoint: rotation.oldEndpoint, subscription: rotation.subscription }
    let status = 0, retryAfter = 0
    const controller = new AbortController(), timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 10000)
    try {
      const response = await fetcher('/api/push/subscriptions/rotate', {
        method: 'POST', credentials: 'include', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', Accept: 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify(body), signal: controller.signal,
      })
      status = response.status
      const header = response.headers.get('Retry-After')
      retryAfter = header && /^\d+$/.test(header) ? Number(header) * 1000 : header ? Math.max(0, Date.parse(header) - now) : 0
      if (response.ok) {
        const item: unknown = await response.json()
        if (!item || typeof item !== 'object' || !('id' in item) || item.id !== owned.id) status = 0
      }
    } catch { status = 0 } finally { clearTimeout(timer) }
    return this.store.transact((r) => {
      if (!r.enabled || r.generation !== attempt.generation || !same(r.lease, attempt) || !r.rotation) return 'stale'
      delete r.lease
      if (status >= 200 && status < 300) {
        r.endpoint = rotation.subscription.endpoint
        if (r.rotation.sequence === rotation.sequence) delete r.rotation
        else { r.rotation.oldEndpoint = r.endpoint; r.rotation.attempts = 0; r.rotation.nextAt = now }
        delete r.repair
        return 'success'
      }
      if (status === 401) { r.repair = 'sign_in'; r.rotation.nextAt = now + 1000; return 'sign_in' }
      if (status && status !== 429 && status < 500) {
        r.enabled = false; r.repair = 'reenable'; delete r.rotation; delete r.key
        return 'reenable'
      }
      r.rotation.attempts = Math.min(16, r.rotation.attempts + 1)
      r.rotation.nextAt = now + Math.max(Math.min(3600000, retryAfter || 0), Math.min(60000, 1000 * 2 ** (r.rotation.attempts - 1)))
      r.repair = 'retry'
      return 'retry'
    })
  }
}

export class IndexedPushStore implements PushStore {
  async transact<T>(fn: (row: PushRecord) => T): Promise<T> {
    const db = await new Promise<IDBDatabase>((resolve, reject) => {
      const open = indexedDB.open('hennery-push', 1)
      open.onupgradeneeded = () => open.result.createObjectStore('registration')
      open.onsuccess = () => resolve(open.result)
      open.onerror = () => reject(new Error('Notification storage unavailable.'))
      open.onblocked = () => reject(new Error('Notification storage blocked; close other hennery windows.'))
    })
    try {
      return await new Promise<T>((resolve, reject) => {
        const tx = db.transaction('registration', 'readwrite'), store = tx.objectStore('registration')
        let result: T
        const read = store.get('current')
        read.onsuccess = () => {
          try {
            const row: PushRecord = read.result ?? { generation: 0, enabled: false }
            // Unknown schema or corrupt data never authorizes automatic renewal.
            if (!Number.isSafeInteger(row.generation) || typeof row.enabled !== 'boolean') throw new Error('Notification storage needs repair.')
            result = fn(row)
            store.put(row, 'current')
          } catch (err) { tx.abort(); reject(err) }
        }
        tx.oncomplete = () => resolve(result)
        tx.onabort = tx.onerror = () => reject(new Error('Notification storage write failed.'))
      })
    } finally { db.close() }
  }
}

export const pushJournal = () => new Journal(new IndexedPushStore())
````

Create `web/src/pwa/notification.ts`:

````typescript
import type { PushPayload } from '../generated/protocol'
const neutral: PushPayload = { title: 'hennery needs your attention', body: 'Open hennery to check the current state.', url: '/sessions', tag: 'hennery-attention' }

// Canonical literal paths only. P2 owns card targeting and window navigation.
export function notificationTarget(value: unknown): string {
  if (typeof value !== 'string' || value.length > 512 || /[\\#\s]/.test(value)) return '/sessions'
  if (value === '/mcp' || value === '/sessions') return value
  return /^\/sessions\/[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,128}(\?pending=[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,128})?$/.test(value) ? value : '/sessions'
}
export function decodePush(text: string | null): PushPayload {
  if (!text || text.length > 8192) return { ...neutral }
  try {
    const p: unknown = JSON.parse(text)
    if (!p || typeof p !== 'object') return { ...neutral }
    const fields = p as Record<string, unknown>
    const bounded = (v: unknown, max: number) => typeof v === 'string' && v.length <= max
    if (!bounded(fields.title, 256) || !bounded(fields.body, 2048) || !bounded(fields.tag, 256)) return { ...neutral }
    return { title: fields.title as string, body: fields.body as string, tag: fields.tag as string, url: notificationTarget(fields.url) }
  } catch { return { ...neutral } }
}
````

Create `web/src/pwa/subscription.ts`:

````typescript
import type { PushSubscribeRequest } from '../generated/protocol'

export function subscriptionBody(subscription: PushSubscription, label?: string): PushSubscribeRequest {
  const json = subscription.toJSON()
  if (!json.endpoint || !json.keys?.p256dh || !json.keys.auth) throw new Error('Browser subscription has no encryption keys.')
  return { endpoint: json.endpoint, expirationTime: json.expirationTime, keys: { p256dh: json.keys.p256dh, auth: json.keys.auth }, ...(label ? { device_label: label } : {}) }
}
export function vapidBytes(key: string): Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer> {
  const binary = atob(key.replace(/-/g, '+').replace(/_/g, '/'))
  const bytes = Uint8Array.from(binary, (c) => c.charCodeAt(0))
  if (bytes.length !== 65 || bytes[0] !== 4) throw new Error('Collector VAPID key is invalid.')
  return bytes
}
export function matchesKey(subscription: PushSubscription, key: string): boolean {
  const actual = subscription.options.applicationServerKey
  const expected = vapidBytes(key)
  return !!actual && actual.byteLength === expected.length && new Uint8Array(actual).every((b, i) => b === expected[i])
}
````

Replace the whole of `web/worker.js`:

````javascript
import { installShell, shellResponse, SHELL_PREFIX } from './src/pwa/shell'
import { pushJournal } from './src/pwa/journal'
import { decodePush } from './src/pwa/notification'
import { subscriptionBody } from './src/pwa/subscription'

self.addEventListener('install', (event) => {
  event.waitUntil(installShell(caches, fetch, __PRECACHE__, __BUILD_ID__, crypto.randomUUID()))
})
self.addEventListener('activate', (event) => {
  // Natural activation only, once the old controlled windows have closed.
  // Never claim an existing document or call skipWaiting.
  event.waitUntil((async () => {
    for (const name of await caches.keys()) {
      if (name.startsWith(SHELL_PREFIX) && !name.startsWith(`${SHELL_PREFIX}${__BUILD_ID__}-`)) await caches.delete(name)
    }
  })())
})
self.addEventListener('fetch', (event) => {
  const r = event.request, url = new URL(r.url)
  if (r.method !== 'GET' || url.origin !== location.origin) return
  event.respondWith((async () => {
    const cached = await shellResponse(caches, __BUILD_ID__, url.pathname + url.search, r.mode === 'navigate', __PRECACHE__)
    return cached ?? fetch(r)
  })())
})
self.addEventListener('push', (event) => {
  event.waitUntil((async () => {
    let text = null
    try { text = event.data?.text() ?? null } catch { /* Neutral attention. */ }
    const payload = decodePush(text)
    await self.registration.showNotification(payload.title, {
      body: payload.body, tag: payload.tag, icon: '/icon-192.png', badge: '/icon-192.png',
      data: { url: payload.url, tag: payload.tag },
    })
  })())
})
self.addEventListener('pushsubscriptionchange', (event) => {
  event.waitUntil((async () => {
    const journal = pushJournal(), row = await journal.read()
    if (!row.enabled || !row.id || !row.endpoint) return
    let replacement = event.newSubscription
    if (!replacement) {
      if (!event.oldSubscription?.options?.applicationServerKey || await self.registration.pushManager.permissionState({ userVisibleOnly: true }) !== 'granted') {
        await journal.needsRepair(row.generation); return
      }
      try { replacement = await self.registration.pushManager.subscribe(event.oldSubscription.options) }
      catch { await journal.needsRepair(row.generation); return }
    }
    await journal.queueRotation(subscriptionBody(replacement), Date.now(), row.generation)
    await journal.attempt()
  })())
})
````
