````typescript
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
import { deferred } from '../test-locks'
import { Client, type ClientDeps } from './client'
import { ApiFailure, StepUpCancelled, Unauthenticated } from './errors'

afterEach(() => vi.useRealTimers())

function json(status: number, body: unknown, headers: Record<string, string> = {}): Response {
  return new Response(body === undefined ? null : JSON.stringify(body), {
    status,
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', ...headers },
  })
}

const STEP_UP = { code: 'step_up_required', message: 'confirm' }

/** A client over a stub server that answers each request with the next of
 *  `answers`, recording what was sent. */
function stub(answers: Response[], deps: Partial<ClientDeps> = {}) {
  const sent: { method: string; path: string; body?: string; headers: Record<string, string> }[] = []
  const fetch = vi.fn(async (path: RequestInfo | URL, init?: RequestInit) => {
    sent.push({
      method: init?.method ?? 'GET',
      path: String(path),
      body: init?.body as string | undefined,
      headers: init?.headers as Record<string, string>,
    })
    const next = answers.shift()
    if (!next) throw new Error('no answer left')
    return next
  })
  const navigate = vi.fn()
  const stepUp = vi.fn(async () => {})
  const client = new Client({
    fetch: fetch as unknown as typeof globalThis.fetch,
    navigate,
    here: () => ({ pathname: '/hosts', search: '?x=1' }),
    stepUp,
    ...deps,
  })
  return { client, sent, navigate, stepUp, fetch }
}

describe('request', () => {
  it('starts fetch synchronously when no ownership hook was requested', async () => {
    const { client, fetch } = stub([json(200, { done: true })])
    const work = client.request('GET', '/api/x')
    expect(fetch).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1)
    await expect(work).resolves.toEqual({ done: true })
  })
  it.each([false, true])('per-attempt deadlines preserve caller abort, already aborted: %s', async (already) => {
    vi.useFakeTimers()
    const outer = new AbortController(), entered = deferred<AbortSignal>()
    const { client } = stub([], { fetch: vi.fn((_path, init) => new Promise<Response>((_resolve, reject) => {
      const signal = init!.signal!
      entered.resolve(signal)
      if (signal.aborted) reject(new Error('Caller abort'))
      else signal.addEventListener('abort', () => reject(new Error('Caller abort')))
    })) })
    if (already) outer.abort()
    const work = client.request('GET', '/api/x', undefined, { timeoutMs: 10000, signal: outer.signal }).catch((err: unknown) => err)
    const signal = await entered.promise
    if (!already) outer.abort()
    expect(signal.aborted).toBe(true)
    expect(await work).toMatchObject({ message: 'Caller abort' })
    expect(vi.getTimerCount()).toBe(0)
  })
  it('successful attempts detach caller cancellation and leave no deadline timer', async () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers()
    const outer = new AbortController(), signals: AbortSignal[] = []
    const { client } = stub([], { fetch: vi.fn(async (_path, init) => {
      signals.push(init!.signal!); return json(200, { done: true })
    }) })
    await client.request('GET', '/api/x', undefined, { timeoutMs: 10000, signal: outer.signal })
    outer.abort()
    expect(signals[0].aborted).toBe(false)
    expect(vi.getTimerCount()).toBe(0)
  })
  it('sends JSON with the cookie, and reads JSON back', async () => {
    const { client, sent } = stub([json(200, { a: 1 })])
    await expect(client.request('POST', '/api/x', { b: 2 })).resolves.toEqual({ a: 1 })
    expect(sent[0]).toMatchObject({ method: 'POST', path: '/api/x', body: '{"b":2}' })
    expect(sent[0].headers['Content-Type']).toBe('application/json')
  })

  it('sends no body and no content type without a body, and reads 204 as nothing', async () => {
    const { client, sent } = stub([new Response(null, { status: 204 })])
    await expect(client.request('POST', '/api/auth/logout')).resolves.toBeUndefined()
    expect(sent[0].body).toBeUndefined()
    expect(sent[0].headers['Content-Type']).toBeUndefined()
  })

  it('leaves fetch in its default cors mode, so a POST carries Origin', async () => {
    const { client, fetch } = stub([json(200, {})])
    await client.request('POST', '/api/x', {})
    const init = fetch.mock.calls[0][1] as RequestInit
    expect(init.mode).toBeUndefined()
    expect(init.credentials).toBe('same-origin')
  })

  it('answers a refusal with its code and a plain message', async () => {
    const { client } = stub([json(409, { code: 'host_offline', message: 'server text' })])
    const err = await client.request('POST', '/api/x').catch((e: unknown) => e)
    expect(err).toBeInstanceOf(ApiFailure)
    expect(err).toMatchObject({ status: 409, code: 'host_offline', message: 'The host is offline.' })
  })

  // Every code the UI explains, each with its own message: a code dropped
  // from the table would fall back to the server's text.
  it.each([
    ['hat_ambiguous', 'This path matches more than one hat: pick the path exactly as its rule names it.'],
    ['cwd_moved', 'The session’s directory has moved since it last ran.'],
    ['hat_mismatch', 'This directory now belongs to another hat than the session’s.'],
    ['presumed_parked', 'Its host has been away and may still run it: close the session first.'],
    ['images_unsupported', 'This agent takes no images.'],
    ['host_offline', 'The host is offline.'],
    ['host_installing', 'The host is still installing its agents.'],
    ['already_set_up', 'hennery is set up already: sign in instead.'],
    ['invalid_setup_token', 'This setup link is used or expired. Run `hennery admin setup-url` on the collector for a new one.'],
    ['origin_mismatch', 'The public URL must be the address this page is open at.'],
    ['setup_required', 'hennery is not set up yet. Run `hennery admin setup-url` on the collector for the setup link.'],
    ['invalid_password', 'Wrong password.'],
    ['rate_limited', 'Too many attempts. Wait, then try again.'],
    ['no_passkeys', 'No passkey is registered.'],
    ['passkeys_unavailable', 'Passkeys need a public URL with a host name, not an IP address.'],
    ['passkey_refused', 'The passkey was not accepted.'],
    ['invalid_ceremony', 'The passkey prompt expired. Try again.'],
    ['already_registered', 'This authenticator holds a passkey for hennery already.'],
    ['step_up_required', 'Confirm it is you, then try again.'],
    ['unauthenticated', 'Sign in first.'],
    ['too_many_codes', 'Sixteen pairing codes are live already: wait for one to expire.'],
    ['name_taken', 'Another hat has this name.'],
    ['hat_purging', 'This hat is being purged.'],
    ['hat_is_default', 'This hat is the default for new hosts, or a host’s default hat: make another hat that default first.'],
    ['resolve_unsupported', 'This host cannot resolve paths yet: update hennery on it.'],
    ['no_answer', 'The host did not answer in time. Try again.'],
    ['busy', 'The host is busy. Try again.'],
    ['too_many_subscriptions', 'Remove a device before adding another: 32 at most.'],
    ['endpoint_taken', 'This browser receives notifications for another account.'],
    ['slug_taken', 'This slug is taken: pick another.'],
    ['too_many_connections', 'There are 256 connections already: delete one first.'],
    ['unsupported_cred_kind', 'This collector cannot sign in to a server this way yet.'],
    ['wrong_cred_kind', 'Only a connection that authenticates with a token takes one.'],
  ])('explains %s in its own words', async (code, message) => {
    const { client } = stub([json(409, { code, message: 'server text' })])
    await expect(client.request('POST', '/api/x')).rejects.toMatchObject({ code, message })
  })

  it('names the status of a refusal with no JSON body', async () => {
    const { client } = stub([new Response('upstream broke', { status: 502 })])
    await expect(client.request('GET', '/api/x')).rejects.toMatchObject({

    await expect(client.request('POST', '/api/x')).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'brand_new', message: 'server text' })
  })

  it.each(['toString', 'constructor', '__proto__', 'hasOwnProperty'])(

    },

  it('keeps the session a refused start still names', async () => {

    const { client } = stub([json(503, body), json(409, { code: 'x', message: 'm', session_id: 7 })])

    await expect(client.request('POST', '/api/auth/login', {})).rejects.toMatchObject({ retryAfter: 42 })

})

describe('403 step_up_required', () => {

    expect(stepUp).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1)

  })

  it('sends nothing again when the dialog is dismissed', async () => {
    const { client, sent } = stub([json(403, STEP_UP)], {
      stepUp: async () => {
        throw new StepUpCancelled()
      },
    })
    await expect(client.request('DELETE', '/api/hosts/h-1')).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(StepUpCancelled)
    expect(sent).toHaveLength(1)
  })

  it('opens one dialog for requests refused together', async () => {
    let release!: () => void
    const stepUp = vi.fn(() => new Promise<void>((resolve) => (release = resolve)))
    const { client, sent } = stub([json(403, STEP_UP), json(403, STEP_UP), json(204, undefined), json(204, undefined)], {
      stepUp,
    })
    const both = Promise.all([client.request('DELETE', '/api/a'), client.request('DELETE', '/api/b')])
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(stepUp).toHaveBeenCalled())
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 0))
    release()
    await both
    expect(stepUp).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1)
    expect(sent).toHaveLength(4)
  })

  it('is a plain error on the step-up routes themselves', async () => {
    const { client, stepUp, sent } = stub([json(403, STEP_UP)])
    await expect(
      client.request('POST', '/api/auth/step-up/password', { password: 'x' }, { stepUp: false }),
    ).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'step_up_required' })
    expect(stepUp).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
    expect(sent).toHaveLength(1)
  })

  it('is not opened by another 403', async () => {
    const { client, stepUp } = stub([json(403, { code: 'origin_mismatch', message: 'm' })])
    await expect(client.request('POST', '/api/x')).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'origin_mismatch' })
    expect(stepUp).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
  })
})
````

````typescript
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
import { BrowserPush, classifyPush } from './device'
import { Journal, type PushRecord, type PushStore } from './journal'
import { Client } from '../api/client'
import type { PushSubscriptionItem } from '../generated/protocol'
import { deferred, testLocks } from '../test-locks'

beforeEach(() => Object.defineProperty(navigator, 'locks', { configurable: true, value: testLocks() }))
afterEach(() => { vi.useRealTimers(); vi.unstubAllGlobals(); vi.restoreAllMocks() })

const key = btoa(String.fromCharCode(4, ...Array(64).fill(1)))
const item: PushSubscriptionItem = { id: 'id', endpoint_host: 'push.example', device_label: 'Phone', created_at: '2026-10-14', this_device: true, signed_out: false }
function setup() {
  let record: PushRecord = { generation: 0, enabled: false }
  const store: PushStore = { async transact(fn) { return fn(record) } }
  const journal = new Journal(store)
  let subscription: PushSubscription | null = null
  const unsubscribe = vi.fn(async () => { subscription = null; return true })
  const make = (endpoint = 'https://push.example/a') => ({ endpoint, expirationTime: null,
    options: { applicationServerKey: Uint8Array.from([4, ...Array(64).fill(1)]).buffer }, unsubscribe,
    toJSON: () => ({ endpoint, keys: { p256dh: 'p', auth: 'a' }, expirationTime: null }) }) as unknown as PushSubscription
  const subscribe = vi.fn(async () => { subscription = make(); return subscription })
  const close = vi.fn()
  const reg = { pushManager: { getSubscription: vi.fn(async () => subscription), subscribe }, getNotifications: vi.fn(async () => [{ close }]) } as unknown as ServiceWorkerRegistration
  const fetcher = vi.fn<typeof fetch>(async () => new Response(JSON.stringify(item), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }))
  const stepUp = vi.fn(async () => {})
  const client = new Client({ fetch: fetcher, here: () => ({ pathname: '/settings', search: '' }), navigate: vi.fn(), stepUp })
  const device = new BrowserPush(client, reg, journal, key)
  return { journal, device, subscribe, unsubscribe, fetcher, close, make, reg, stepUp, client,
    setSubscription: (next: PushSubscription | null) => { subscription = next },
    setRecord: (next: PushRecord) => { record = next } }
}
describe('explicit browser push intent', () => {
  it('an enable queued before disable cannot start when that disable lease expires', async () => {
    const { device, journal, subscribe, fetcher } = setup()
    vi.useFakeTimers()
    const occupied = deferred(), release = deferred()
    const occupying = navigator.locks.request('hennery-push-effects', async () => { occupied.resolve(); await release.promise })
    await occupied.promise

    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0)
    const disabling = device.disable(true)
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(13000)

    expect(await enabling).toBeInstanceOf(Error)
    expect((await disabling).browser).toBe(true)

  })
  it('missing origin locks refuse enable before touching the browser or journal', async () => {

  })
  it('disable during browser inspection prevents removal of a mismatched subscription', async () => {

  })
  it('disable between a key-change unsubscribe and subscribe prevents new browser work', async () => {

    const removing = deferred(), removed = deferred<boolean>()

    await journal.disable(); removed.resolve(true)

  })
  it('superseded disable checks ownership after DELETE before browser cleanup', async () => {
    const { device, journal, fetcher, unsubscribe, close } = setup()

    const owner = await journal.beginEnable()

    expect((await journal.read()).enabled).toBe(true)

  it('superseded disable checks ownership after notification inspection before closing', async () => {

    await device.enable()

    vi.spyOn(reg, 'getNotifications').mockImplementationOnce(() => { inspecting.resolve(); return inspected.promise })
    const disabling = device.disable(true)

    const owner = await journal.beginEnable()
    await journal.finishEnable(owner, item.id, 'https://push.example/a', key)
    inspected.resolve([{ close } as unknown as Notification])
    await disabling
    expect(close).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
    expect((await journal.read()).enabled).toBe(true)
  })
  it('the retried HTTP attempt still aborts after its own ten seconds', async () => {
    const { device, fetcher, stepUp, journal } = setup()
    vi.useFakeTimers()
    const confirming = deferred(), confirmation = deferred(), retrying = deferred()
    stepUp.mockImplementationOnce(() => { confirming.resolve(); return confirmation.promise })
    fetcher.mockResolvedValueOnce(new Response(JSON.stringify({ code: 'step_up_required' }), { status: 403, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }))
    fetcher.mockImplementationOnce((_url, init) => new Promise<Response>((_resolve, reject) => {
      retrying.resolve(); init!.signal!.addEventListener('abort', () => reject(new Error('Network deadline')))
    }))
    let done = false
    const enabling = device.enable().catch((err: unknown) => err).then((result) => { done = true; return result })
    await confirming.promise; await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(15000)
    confirmation.resolve(); await retrying.promise
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(9999); expect(done).toBe(false)

    expect(done).toBe(true)
    expect(await enabling).toMatchObject({ message: 'Network deadline' })

    expect(vi.getTimerCount()).toBe(0)
  })
  it('keeps a second window enable behind a DELETE that outlives the journal lease', async () => {
    const { device, journal, fetcher, reg } = setup()
    await device.enable()

    let serverExists = true
    fetcher.mockImplementation(async (_url, init) => {
      if (init?.method === 'DELETE') {
        deleting.resolve(); await releaseDelete.promise; serverExists = false
        return new Response(null, { status: 204 })
      }
      serverExists = true
      return new Response(JSON.stringify(item), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })
    })
    const disabling = device.disable()

    expect(enabled).toBe(false)
    releaseDelete.resolve()

    expect(serverExists).toBe(true)
    expect(await reg.pushManager.getSubscription()).not.toBeNull()
    expect((await journal.read()).enabled).toBe(true)

  it('a superseded disable checks its owner after inspection before any destructive effect', async () => {
    const { device, journal, reg, fetcher, unsubscribe, close, make, setSubscription } = setup()
    await device.enable()

    const disabling = device.disable()
    await inspecting.promise
    // Controlled takeover models a surviving generation after the old owner
    // expires; production effects must be fenced even under the origin lock.

    const next = make('https://push.example/new')

    expect(unsubscribe).not.toHaveBeenCalled()

  it('a second window reconciliation cannot cancel the unsubscribe/subscribe renewal gap', async () => {

  })

    const { device, subscribe, fetcher, journal } = setup()
    fetcher.mockRejectedValueOnce(new TypeError('lost response'))

    expect((await journal.read()).enabled).toBe(false)
  })

    const { device, make, setSubscription, fetcher, journal } = setup()
    await device.enable()

    await device.reconcile([{ ...item, signed_out: true }], fetcher)
    expect(fetcher.mock.calls[0][0]).toBe('/api/push/subscriptions/rotate')
    expect(JSON.parse(fetcher.mock.calls[0][1]!.body as string).old_endpoint).toBe('https://push.example/a')
    expect((await journal.read()).endpoint).toBe('https://push.example/b')
  })
  it.each(['server', 'browser', 'both', 'none'])('disable reports separate cleanup outcomes: %s', async (fault) => {
    const { device, fetcher, unsubscribe, journal, close } = setup()
    await device.enable()
    fetcher.mockImplementationOnce(async () => {
      expect((await journal.read()).enabled).toBe(false)
      if (fault === 'server' || fault === 'both') throw new TypeError('offline')
      return new Response(null, { status: 204 })
    })
    if (fault === 'browser' || fault === 'both') unsubscribe.mockResolvedValueOnce(false)
    const result = await device.disable()
    expect(result.server).toBe(fault !== 'server' && fault !== 'both')
    expect(result.browser).toBe(fault !== 'browser' && fault !== 'both')
    expect(unsubscribe).toHaveBeenCalled()
    expect(close).toHaveBeenCalled()
    expect((await journal.read()).enabled).toBe(false)
    expect((await journal.read()).id).toBe(result.server ? undefined : 'id')
  })
  it.each(['server', 'browser', 'both', 'none'])('coalesced disables report the same completed cleanup outcomes: %s', async (fault) => {
    const { device, reg, fetcher, unsubscribe } = setup()
    await device.enable()
    vi.useFakeTimers()
    const inspecting = deferred(), inspected = deferred<PushSubscription | null>()
    const old = await reg.pushManager.getSubscription()
    vi.spyOn(reg.pushManager, 'getSubscription').mockImplementationOnce(() => { inspecting.resolve(); return inspected.promise })
    if (fault === 'server' || fault === 'both') fetcher.mockRejectedValueOnce(new Error('offline'))
    else fetcher.mockResolvedValueOnce(new Response(null, { status: 204 }))
    if (fault === 'browser' || fault === 'both') unsubscribe.mockResolvedValueOnce(false)
    const a = device.disable()
    await inspecting.promise
    const b = device.disable()
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0)
    inspected.resolve(old)
    const first = await a
    expect(await b).toEqual(first)
    expect(first).toEqual({ server: fault !== 'server' && fault !== 'both', browser: fault !== 'browser' && fault !== 'both' })
  })
  it('404 removal is success and missing local id removes by known endpoint', async () => {
    const { device, make, setSubscription, fetcher } = setup()
    setSubscription(make())
    fetcher.mockResolvedValueOnce(new Response(JSON.stringify({ code: 'not_found' }), { status: 404, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }))
    expect(await device.disable()).toEqual({ server: true, browser: true })
    expect(fetcher.mock.calls[0][0]).toBe('/api/push/subscriptions')
    expect(fetcher.mock.calls[0][1]!.method).toBe('DELETE')
  })
  it('renewal replaces the browser subscription and rotates without POST subscribe or step-up', async () => {
    const { device, make, setSubscription, subscribe, unsubscribe, fetcher, journal } = setup()
    await device.enable()
    subscribe.mockImplementationOnce(async () => { const next = make('https://push.example/b'); setSubscription(next); return next })
    fetcher.mockClear(); vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetcher)
    try {
      await device.renew([item])
      expect(unsubscribe).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1)
      expect(fetcher.mock.calls[0][0]).toBe('/api/push/subscriptions/rotate')
      expect((await journal.read()).endpoint).toBe('https://push.example/b')
    } finally { vi.unstubAllGlobals() }
  })
  it('fails closed when browser inspection or durable disable fails, while still trying cleanup', async () => {
    const { device, reg, journal, unsubscribe, close } = setup()
    await device.enable()
    vi.spyOn(journal, 'disable').mockRejectedValueOnce(new Error('storage'))
    expect((await device.disable(true)).browser).toBe(false)
    expect(unsubscribe).toHaveBeenCalled(); expect(close).toHaveBeenCalled()
    vi.spyOn(reg.pushManager, 'getSubscription').mockRejectedValueOnce(new Error('browser'))
    expect((await device.disable(true)).browser).toBe(false)
  })
})
describe('registration classifier', () => {
  const record: PushRecord = { generation: 1, enabled: true, id: 'id', endpoint: 'endpoint', key }
  const base = { supported: true, secure: true, permission: 'granted' as NotificationPermission, subscribed: true, matches: true, record, row: item }
  it.each([
    [{ ...base, supported: false }, 'unsupported'], [{ ...base, secure: false }, 'insecure'],
    [{ ...base, permission: 'denied' }, 'blocked'], [{ ...base, permission: 'default' }, 'off'],
    [{ ...base, subscribed: false }, 'off'], [{ ...base, record: { generation: 0, enabled: false } }, 'unconfirmed'],
    [{ ...base, row: undefined }, 'reenable'], [{ ...base, matches: false }, 'reenable'],
    [{ ...base, record: { ...record, rotation: { oldEndpoint: 'endpoint', subscription: { endpoint: 'replacement', keys: { p256dh: 'p', auth: 'a' } }, sequence: 1, attempts: 1, nextAt: 1000 } } }, 'unconfirmed'],
    [{ ...base, row: { ...item, signed_out: true } }, 'signed_out'], [base, 'enabled'],
  ] as const)('reports %s as %s', (input, verdict) => expect(classifyPush(input)).toBe(verdict))
})
````

Replace the whole of `web/src/pwa/journal.test.ts`:

````typescript
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
import { Journal, type PushRecord, type PushStore } from './journal'
import type { PushSubscribeRequest } from '../generated/protocol'

// Persistent transactional fake: a new Journal has no JS globals to recover.
function store(): PushStore {
  let row: PushRecord = { generation: 0, enabled: false }
  let queue = Promise.resolve()
  return { transact<T>(fn: (value: PushRecord) => T): Promise<T> {
    const result = queue.then(() => { const copy = structuredClone(row); const value = fn(copy); row = copy; return value })
    queue = result.then(() => {}, () => {})
    return result
  } }
}
const subscription = (endpoint: string): PushSubscribeRequest => ({ endpoint, keys: { p256dh: 'key', auth: 'auth' } })
async function enabled(s = store()) {
  const journal = new Journal(s)
  const lease = await journal.beginEnable(0)
  expect(await journal.finishEnable(lease, 'row1', 'https://push.example/a', 'vapid')).toBe(true)
  return { journal, s }
}
const ok = () => new Response(JSON.stringify({ id: 'row1' }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })

describe('origin-local registration journal', () => {
  it('overlapping disables reserve one cleanup owner instead of invalidating each other', async () => {
    const { journal } = await enabled()
    await journal.disable()
    const owner = (await journal.read()).lease
    await journal.disable()
    expect((await journal.read()).generation).toBe(2)
    expect((await journal.read()).lease).toEqual(owner)
  })
  it('cleanup completion also checks the owner token within the same generation', async () => {
    const { journal } = await enabled()
    const disabled = await journal.disable()
    const owner = { ...disabled.cleanup, token: 'wrong-owner' }
    await journal.finishDisable(true, true, owner.generation, owner)
    expect((await journal.read()).lease).toEqual(disabled.cleanup)
    expect((await journal.read()).id).toBe('row1')
    await journal.finishDisable(true, true, disabled.cleanup.generation, disabled.cleanup)
    expect((await journal.read()).lease).toBeUndefined()
  })
  it('reserves disable browser cleanup and renewal against another window enabling', async () => {
    const { journal } = await enabled()
    const now = Date.now()
    const owned = await journal.beginRenew(now)
    await expect(journal.beginEnable(now + 1)).rejects.toThrow('busy')
    await journal.release(owned)
    await journal.disable(now + 2)
    await expect(journal.beginEnable(now + 3)).rejects.toThrow('busy')
    await journal.finishDisable(true, true, 2)
    const next = await journal.beginEnable(now + 4)
    expect(next.generation).toBe(3)
  })
  it('records only collector-confirmed identity and aborts a failed explicit enable', async () => {
    const { journal } = await enabled()
    expect(await journal.read()).toEqual({ generation: 1, enabled: true, id: 'row1', endpoint: 'https://push.example/a', key: 'vapid' })
    await journal.queueRotation(subscription('https://push.example/b'), 1)
    const next = await journal.beginEnable(20000)
    expect((await journal.read()).rotation).toBeUndefined()
    await journal.abortEnable(next)
    expect(await journal.read()).toEqual({ generation: 2, enabled: false, repair: 'reenable' })
  })
  it('stale cleanup and subscription events cannot change a new explicit enable', async () => {
    const { journal } = await enabled()
    const previous = await journal.disable()
    await journal.finishDisable(true, true, previous.generation + 1)
    const next = await journal.beginEnable(100)
    await journal.finishEnable(next, 'new-row', 'new-endpoint', 'new-key')
    await journal.finishDisable(true, true, previous.generation + 1)
    await journal.needsRepair(previous.generation)
    expect(await journal.queueRotation(subscription('stale'), 200, previous.generation)).toBe(false)
    expect(await journal.read()).toEqual({ generation: 3, enabled: true, id: 'new-row', endpoint: 'new-endpoint', key: 'new-key' })
  })
  it('a retained removal identity never authorizes repair after a missing-row verdict', async () => {
    const { journal } = await enabled()
    await journal.needsRepair(1)
    expect(await journal.queueRotation(subscription('https://push.example/b'), 2)).toBe(false)
    expect(await journal.read()).toMatchObject({ enabled: false, repair: 'reenable' })
  })
  it('requires an explicit enable, and fences simultaneous enables and stale completions', async () => {
    const s = store(), a = new Journal(s), b = new Journal(s)
    expect(await a.queueRotation(subscription('https://push.example/b'))).toBe(false)
    const lease = await a.beginEnable(0)
    await expect(b.beginEnable(1)).rejects.toThrow('busy')
    await b.disable()
    expect(await a.finishEnable(lease, 'late-id', 'endpoint', 'key')).toBe(false)
    expect(await a.read()).toMatchObject({ enabled: false, id: 'late-id' })
    expect((await a.read()).endpoint).toBeUndefined()
  })
  it('keeps the first server anchor and only the newest replacement across restarts', async () => {
    const { journal, s } = await enabled()
    await journal.queueRotation(subscription('https://push.example/b'), 10)
    await new Journal(s).queueRotation(subscription('https://push.example/c'), 11)
    const calls: string[] = []
    expect(await new Journal(s).attempt(async (_url, init) => { calls.push(String(init?.body)); return ok() }, 12)).toBe('success')
    expect(JSON.parse(calls[0])).toEqual({ old_endpoint: 'https://push.example/a', subscription: subscription('https://push.example/c') })
    expect(await journal.read()).toMatchObject({ endpoint: 'https://push.example/c', enabled: true })
    expect((await journal.read()).rotation).toBeUndefined()
  })
  it('allows one HTTP attempt, advances its anchor while keeping a replacement received in flight', async () => {
    const { journal, s } = await enabled()
    await journal.queueRotation(subscription('https://push.example/b'), 1)
    let resolve!: (r: Response) => void
    const pending = journal.attempt(() => new Promise<Response>((r) => { resolve = r }), 2)
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(resolve).toBeTypeOf('function'))
    const other = new Journal(s)
    expect(await other.attempt(async () => ok(), 3)).toBe('idle')
    await other.queueRotation(subscription('https://push.example/c'), 4)
    resolve(ok())
    expect(await pending).toBe('success')
    expect(await other.read()).toMatchObject({ endpoint: 'https://push.example/b', rotation: { oldEndpoint: 'https://push.example/b', subscription: subscription('https://push.example/c') } })
    expect(await other.attempt(async (_url, init) => { expect(JSON.parse(String(init?.body)).old_endpoint).toBe('https://push.example/b'); return ok() }, 5)).toBe('success')
  })
  it('failure backoff is bounded, and a newer replacement keeps its sequence', async () => {
    const { journal } = await enabled()
    await journal.queueRotation(subscription('https://push.example/b'), 1)
    let now = 1
    for (let i = 0; i < 20; i++) {
      expect(await journal.attempt(async () => new Response(null, { status: 500, headers: { 'Retry-After': '999999' } }), now)).toBe('retry')
      const row = await journal.read()
      expect(row.rotation!.nextAt).toBe(now + 3600000)
      now = row.rotation!.nextAt
    }
    expect((await journal.read()).rotation!.attempts).toBe(16)
  })
  it('uses credentials, JSON and a cancellable ten-second deadline for an attempt', async () => {
    const { journal } = await enabled()
    await journal.queueRotation(subscription('https://push.example/b'), 1)
    vi.useFakeTimers()
    try {
      const call = vi.fn((_url: string | URL | Request, init?: RequestInit) => new Promise<Response>((_resolve, reject) => {
        init!.signal!.addEventListener('abort', () => reject(new Error('timeout')))
      }))
      const pending = journal.attempt(call, 2)
      await Promise.resolve(); await Promise.resolve(); await Promise.resolve()
      expect(call).toHaveBeenCalledWith('/api/push/subscriptions/rotate', expect.objectContaining({ credentials: 'include', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', Accept: 'application/json' } }))
      await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(10001)
      expect(await pending).toBe('retry')
      expect((await journal.read()).repair).toBe('retry')
    } finally { vi.useRealTimers() }
  })
  it('disable cancels deferred work and cannot be undone by an HTTP success', async () => {
    const { journal } = await enabled()
    await journal.queueRotation(subscription('https://push.example/b'), 1)
    let resolve!: (r: Response) => void
    const pending = journal.attempt(() => new Promise<Response>((r) => { resolve = r }), 2)
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(resolve).toBeTypeOf('function'))
    await journal.disable()
    resolve(ok())
    expect(await pending).toBe('stale')
    expect(await journal.read()).toMatchObject({ enabled: false, generation: 2 })
    expect((await journal.read()).rotation).toBeUndefined()
    expect((await journal.read()).key).toBeUndefined()
    expect(await journal.queueRotation(subscription('https://push.example/c'))).toBe(false)
    expect(await journal.attempt(async () => ok(), 20000)).toBe('idle')
  })
  it.each([401, 404, 400, 403, 409])('keeps 401 recoverable, stops automatic repair on %s otherwise', async (status) => {
    const { journal, s } = await enabled()
    await journal.queueRotation(subscription('https://push.example/b'), 1)
    expect(await journal.attempt(async () => new Response(null, { status }), 2)).toBe(status === 401 ? 'sign_in' : 'reenable')
    const row = await new Journal(s).read()
    expect(row.repair).toBe(status === 401 ? 'sign_in' : 'reenable')
    expect(row.enabled).toBe(status === 401)
    expect(!!row.rotation).toBe(status === 401)
  })
  it.each([429, 500, 'network', 'bad-response'])('retains bounded retry work on %s, honoring Retry-After', async (status) => {
    const { journal, s } = await enabled()
    await journal.queueRotation(subscription('https://push.example/b'), 1)
    const fetcher = async () => {
      if (status === 'network') throw new TypeError('offline')
      if (status === 'bad-response') return new Response('{}', { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })
      return new Response(null, { status: Number(status), headers: { 'Retry-After': '20' } })
    }
    expect(await journal.attempt(fetcher, 2)).toBe('retry')
    const row = await new Journal(s).read()
    expect(row.rotation?.attempts).toBe(1)
    expect(row.rotation?.nextAt).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1002)
    if (status === 429 || status === 500) expect(row.rotation?.nextAt).toBe(20002)
    expect(await new Journal(s).attempt(async () => ok(), 3)).toBe('idle')
    expect(await new Journal(s).attempt(async () => ok(), 70000)).toBe('success')
  })
  it('requires repair on ambiguous lost success/404 rather than recreating a row', async () => {
    const { journal } = await enabled()
    await journal.queueRotation(subscription('https://push.example/b'), 1)
    const fetcher = vi.fn().mockRejectedValueOnce(new TypeError('lost response')).mockResolvedValueOnce(new Response(null, { status: 404 }))
    expect(await journal.attempt(fetcher, 2)).toBe('retry')
    expect(await journal.attempt(fetcher, 2000)).toBe('reenable')
    expect(fetcher.mock.calls.every(([url]) => url === '/api/push/subscriptions/rotate')).toBe(true)
  })
  it('a dead attempt lease expires, and its stale result cannot overwrite a newer success', async () => {
    const { journal, s } = await enabled()
    await journal.queueRotation(subscription('https://push.example/b'), 1)
    let resolve!: (r: Response) => void
    const first = journal.attempt(() => new Promise<Response>((r) => { resolve = r }), 2)
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(resolve).toBeTypeOf('function'))
    expect(await new Journal(s).attempt(async () => ok(), 13000)).toBe('success')
    await new Journal(s).queueRotation(subscription('https://push.example/c'), 13001)
    resolve(ok())
    expect(await first).toBe('stale')
    expect((await journal.read()).endpoint).toBe('https://push.example/b')
    expect((await journal.read()).rotation?.subscription.endpoint).toBe('https://push.example/c')
  })
  it('a success for a different collector id remains unconfirmed', async () => {
    const { journal } = await enabled()
    await journal.queueRotation(subscription('https://push.example/b'), 1)
    expect(await journal.attempt(async () => new Response(JSON.stringify({ id: 'foreign-row' })), 2)).toBe('retry')
    expect((await journal.read()).endpoint).toBe('https://push.example/a')
  })
  it('scrubs capability material after disable, preserving only the removal id on partial failure', async () => {
    const { journal } = await enabled()
    expect(await journal.disable()).toMatchObject({ id: 'row1', endpoint: 'https://push.example/a' })
    await journal.finishDisable(false, false, 2)
    expect(await journal.read()).toEqual({ generation: 2, enabled: false, id: 'row1', repair: 'remove_server_and_browser' })
    await journal.finishDisable(true, false, 2)
    expect((await journal.read()).repair).toBe('remove_browser')
    await journal.finishDisable(true, true, 2)
    expect(await journal.read()).toEqual({ generation: 2, enabled: false })
  })
})
````

````typescript
import { afterEach, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
import { Client } from '../api/client'
import { completeLogout } from './logout'
import { hold, heldFor, forgetAllAttachments } from '../lib/attachments'
import * as journals from './journal'
import * as lifecycle from './lifecycle'
import { Journal, type PushRecord, type PushStore } from './journal'
import { deferred, testLocks } from '../test-locks'

afterEach(() => { vi.useRealTimers(); vi.unstubAllGlobals(); vi.restoreAllMocks(); sessionStorage.clear(); forgetAllAttachments(); history.replaceState(null, '', '/') })
const client = () => new Client({ fetch: vi.fn(), here: () => ({ pathname: '/settings', search: '' }), navigate: vi.fn(), stepUp: vi.fn() })
it('a successful cleanup wins over a delayed outer timeout report', async () => {
  Object.defineProperty(navigator, 'locks', { configurable: true, value: testLocks() })
  const row: PushRecord = { generation: 1, enabled: true, id: 'id' }
  const store: PushStore = { async transact(fn) { return fn(row) } }
  vi.spyOn(journals, 'pushJournal').mockImplementation(() => new Journal(store))
  const registration = { pushManager: { getSubscription: async () => null }, getNotifications: async () => [] }
  Object.defineProperty(navigator, 'serviceWorker', { configurable: true, value: { getRegistration: async () => registration } })
  vi.spyOn(lifecycle, 'bounded').mockImplementationOnce((work) => work)
    .mockImplementationOnce((work) => work.then(() => { throw new Error('Delayed timeout report') }))
  await completeLogout(client(), false)
  expect(sessionStorage.getItem('hennery.logout-cleanup')).toBeNull()
})
it('cleanup finishing after the outer deadline clears the unconfirmed warning', async () => {
  vi.useFakeTimers()
  Object.defineProperty(navigator, 'locks', { configurable: true, value: testLocks() })
  const row: PushRecord = { generation: 1, enabled: true, id: 'id' }
  const store: PushStore = { async transact(fn) { return fn(row) } }
  vi.spyOn(journals, 'pushJournal').mockImplementation(() => new Journal(store))
  const cleaning = deferred(), cleaned = deferred<Notification[]>()
  const registration = { pushManager: { getSubscription: async () => null }, getNotifications: () => { cleaning.resolve(); return cleaned.promise } }
  Object.defineProperty(navigator, 'serviceWorker', { configurable: true, value: { getRegistration: async () => registration } })
  const logout = completeLogout(client(), false)
  await cleaning.promise
  await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(10001)
  await logout
  expect(sessionStorage.getItem('hennery.logout-cleanup')).toContain('unconfirmed')
  cleaned.resolve([])
  await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0)
  expect(sessionStorage.getItem('hennery.logout-cleanup')).toBeNull()
})
it('overlapping confirmed logouts leave no warning after both local cleanups finish', async () => {
  vi.useFakeTimers()
  Object.defineProperty(navigator, 'locks', { configurable: true, value: testLocks() })
  const row: PushRecord = { generation: 1, enabled: true, id: 'id', endpoint: 'https://push.example/a' }
  const store: PushStore = { async transact(fn) { return fn(row) } }
  vi.spyOn(journals, 'pushJournal').mockImplementation(() => new Journal(store))
  const registration = { pushManager: { getSubscription: async () => null }, getNotifications: async () => [] }
  Object.defineProperty(navigator, 'serviceWorker', { configurable: true, value: { getRegistration: async () => registration } })
  const a = completeLogout(client(), false), b = completeLogout(client(), false)
  await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(10001)
  await a; await b
  await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(2000)
  expect(sessionStorage.getItem('hennery.logout-cleanup')).toBeNull()
})
it('confirmed logout clears drafts and images, broadcasts and goes to login even if worker cleanup fails', async () => {
  sessionStorage.setItem('hennery.draft.s1', 'draft')
  hold('s1', { attachments: [{ n: 1, file: new File(['a'], 'a.png', { type: 'image/png' }) }], nextN: 2 })
  const postMessage = vi.fn(), close = vi.fn()
  vi.stubGlobal('BroadcastChannel', class { postMessage = postMessage; close = close })
  Object.defineProperty(navigator, 'serviceWorker', { configurable: true, value: { getRegistration: vi.fn().mockRejectedValue(new Error('storage')) } })
  await completeLogout(client())
  expect(location.pathname).toBe('/login')
  expect(sessionStorage.getItem('hennery.draft.s1')).toBeNull()
  expect(heldFor('s1').attachments).toEqual([])
  expect(postMessage).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ version: 1, kind: 'logout' })
  expect(close).toHaveBeenCalled()
  expect(sessionStorage.getItem('hennery.logout-cleanup')).toContain('Signed out.')
})
it('a received logout never broadcasts again, and successful local cleanup clears its warning', async () => {
  sessionStorage.setItem('hennery.logout-cleanup', 'old warning')
  const postMessage = vi.fn()
  vi.stubGlobal('BroadcastChannel', class { postMessage = postMessage; close = vi.fn() })
  Object.defineProperty(navigator, 'serviceWorker', { configurable: true, value: { getRegistration: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(undefined) } })
  await completeLogout(client(), false)
  expect(postMessage).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
  expect(sessionStorage.getItem('hennery.logout-cleanup')).toBeNull()
})
````

````tsx
    'PATCH /api/settings': json(200, { public_url: 'https://app.example', contact: 'mailto:new@example.invalid' }),

}

    expect(screen.queryByRole('heading', { name: 'Notification privacy by hat' })).not.toBeInTheDocument()

  })
  it('sends only contact and uses read-back, with no public_url field', async () => {

    await userEvent.clear(contact); await userEvent.type(contact, 'mailto:new@example.invalid')
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Save contact' }))
    await waitFor(() => expect(server.sent.filter((c) => c.method === 'PATCH')).toHaveLength(1))
    expect(server.sent.find((c) => c.method === 'PATCH')?.body).toEqual({ contact: 'mailto:new@example.invalid' })
  })
  it('keeps three independent hat controls, renders User-Agent as isolated text', async () => {
    const { server } = page()
    const card = await screen.findByRole('group', { name: 'Personal' })
    await userEvent.click(within(card).getByLabelText('Mute', { exact: true }))
    await userEvent.click(within(card).getByLabelText('Include question details', { exact: true }))
    await userEvent.click(within(card).getByRole('button', { name: 'Save policy' }))
    await waitFor(() => expect(within(card).getByLabelText('Mute', { exact: true })).not.toBeChecked())
    expect(within(card).getByLabelText('Use a generic title', { exact: true })).toBeChecked()
    expect(server.sent.find((r) => r.method === 'PUT')?.body).toEqual({ muted: true, details: true, generic_title: false })
    const agent = screen.getByText('<script>bad</script>', { exact: true })
    expect(agent.tagName).toBe('BDI')
    expect(document.querySelector('script')).toBeNull()
  })
})
````

Create `web/src/test-locks.ts`:

````typescript
// FIFO origin lock dependency for deterministic page/worker interleavings.
export function testLocks() {
  const queues = new Map<string, Promise<void>>()
  return { request<T>(name: string, work: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
    const result = (queues.get(name) ?? Promise.resolve()).then(work)
    queues.set(name, result.then(() => {}, () => {}))
    return result
  } }
}

export function deferred<T = void>() {
  let resolve!: (value: T) => void
  const promise = new Promise<T>((done) => { resolve = done })
  return { promise, resolve }
}
````

````tsx
// The app: the route decides between setup, login and the signed-in shell;
// the step-up dialog opens over any of them when a request needs it.
import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import { StepUpCancelled } from './api/errors'
import { ClientContext, browserClient } from './app-client'
import Shell from './components/Shell'
import StepUpDialog from './components/StepUpDialog'
import { match, navigate, useLocation } from './router'
import Login from './screens/Login'
import Setup from './screens/Setup'
import PwaStatus from './components/PwaStatus'
import { completeLogout } from './pwa/logout'

interface Waiting {
  resolve: () => void
  reject: (err: Error) => void
}

export default function App({ fetchImpl }: { fetchImpl?: typeof fetch }) {
  const [waiting, setWaiting] = useState<Waiting | null>(null)
  const open = useRef<() => Promise<void>>(() => Promise.reject(new StepUpCancelled()))
  open.current = () => new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => setWaiting({ resolve, reject }))
  const client = useMemo(() => browserClient(() => open.current(), fetchImpl), [fetchImpl])
  const { pathname } = useLocation()
  const route = match(pathname)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (pathname === '/') navigate('/sessions', { replace: true })
  }, [pathname])
  useEffect(() => {
    if (typeof BroadcastChannel === 'undefined') return
    const channel = new BroadcastChannel('hennery-auth')
    channel.onmessage = (event: MessageEvent<unknown>) => {
      const data = event.data
      if (data && typeof data === 'object' && 'version' in data && data.version === 1 && 'kind' in data && data.kind === 'logout') void completeLogout(client, false)
    }
    return () => channel.close()
  }, [client])

  const close = (outcome: 'done' | 'cancelled') => {
    if (!waiting) return
    if (outcome === 'done') waiting.resolve()
    else waiting.reject(new StepUpCancelled())
    setWaiting(null)
  }

  return (
    <ClientContext.Provider value={client}>
      <div className="application-root">
      <PwaStatus />
      {/* While the step-up dialog is open, nothing under it can be focused
          or clicked. */}
      <div className="page" inert={waiting !== null} style={{ display: 'contents' }}>
        {route.name === 'setup' ? <Setup /> : route.name === 'login' ? <Login /> : <Shell route={route} />}
      </div>
      {waiting && <StepUpDialog onDone={() => close('done')} onCancel={() => close('cancelled')} />}
      </div>
    </ClientContext.Provider>
  )
}
````

Replace the whole of `web/src/api/client.ts`:

````typescript
// The typed transport (client view spec §6, frontend spec §3): every API
// call goes through `request`, which answers the three refusals the same
// way everywhere.
//
// - 401 `unauthenticated`: to the login screen, which returns here.
// - 403 `step_up_required`: the step-up dialog, then the request once more.
//   Safe to send again: every step-up check runs before its handler does
//   anything (`require_step_up` is a route or handler layer; the handlers
//   that check for themselves check before they read or write), so the
//   refused attempt changed nothing. Never a third time, never another
//   dialog.
// - Anything else not 2xx: an `ApiFailure` with the server's code.
//
// `mode` is left at fetch's default, `cors`: with it a same-origin `POST`
// carries `Origin`, which the server requires, under the page's
// `Referrer-Policy: no-referrer`.
import { ApiFailure, Unauthenticated, retryAfterSeconds } from './errors'
import { loginHref } from './next'

export interface ClientDeps {
  fetch: typeof fetch
  /** Navigate inside the app (`history.pushState`), never to another origin. */
  navigate: (to: string) => void
  /** The current path and query, without the fragment. */
  here: () => { pathname: string; search: string }
  /** Open the step-up dialog: resolves once stepped up, rejects with
   *  `StepUpCancelled` when dismissed. */
  stepUp: () => Promise<void>
}

export interface RequestOptions {
  /** `false` on the step-up routes themselves: a 403 there is an error. */
  stepUp?: boolean
  signal?: AbortSignal
  /** Each HTTP attempt gets its own deadline; human confirmation has none. */
  timeoutMs?: number
  /** Recheck operation ownership before the initial attempt and its retry. */
  beforeSend?: () => Promise<void>
  /** Same-origin response metadata, separate from the JSON wire body. */
  onResponse?: (response: Response) => void
}

/** Screens whose own 401s are answers, not a reason to go and sign in. */
const SIGN_IN_PAGES = ['/login', '/setup']

export class Client {
  private readonly deps: ClientDeps
  private steppingUp: Promise<void> | null = null

  constructor(deps: ClientDeps) {
    this.deps = deps
  }

  /** A fresh confirmation without resending a request (OAuth needs a new click). */
  async confirm(): Promise<void> {
    await this.stepUpOnce()
  }

  /** `method path` with a JSON body, answered as JSON (`undefined` for an
   *  empty answer). */
  async request<T>(method: string, path: string, body?: unknown, options: RequestOptions = {}): Promise<T> {
    const first = await this.networkAttempt<{ retry: true } | { retry: false; value: T }>(method, path, body, options, async (response) => {
      if (options.stepUp !== false && await isStepUpRequired(response)) return { retry: true }
      options.onResponse?.(response)
      return { retry: false, value: await this.answer<T>(response) }
    })
    if (!first.retry) return first.value
    await this.stepUpOnce()
    return this.networkAttempt(method, path, body, options, async (response) => {
      options.onResponse?.(response)
      return this.answer<T>(response)
    })
  }

  private async networkAttempt<T>(method: string, path: string, body: unknown, options: RequestOptions, read: (response: Response) => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
    if (options.beforeSend) await options.beforeSend()
    if (options.timeoutMs === undefined) return read(await this.send(method, path, body, options.signal))
    const controller = new AbortController()
    const abort = () => controller.abort(options.signal?.reason)
    if (options.signal?.aborted) abort()
    else options.signal?.addEventListener('abort', abort, { once: true })
    const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), options.timeoutMs)
    try { return await read(await this.send(method, path, body, controller.signal)) }
    finally { clearTimeout(timer); options.signal?.removeEventListener('abort', abort) }
  }

  /** A `GET` whose answer the caller reads itself (the SSE helper): the 401
   *  rule applies; any other status is the caller's. */
  async open(path: string, headers: Record<string, string>, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<Response> {
    const response = await this.deps.fetch(path, { method: 'GET', headers, credentials: 'same-origin', signal })
    if (response.status === 401) await this.unauthenticated(response)
    return response
  }

  private send(method: string, path: string, body: unknown, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<Response> {
    const headers: Record<string, string> = { Accept: 'application/json' }
    const init: RequestInit = { method, headers, credentials: 'same-origin', signal }
    if (body !== undefined) {
      headers['Content-Type'] = 'application/json'
      init.body = JSON.stringify(body)
    }
    return this.deps.fetch(path, init)
  }

  /** One dialog for every request refused while it is open. */
  private stepUpOnce(): Promise<void> {
    if (!this.steppingUp) {
      this.steppingUp = this.deps.stepUp().finally(() => {
        this.steppingUp = null
      })
    }
    return this.steppingUp
  }

  private async answer<T>(response: Response): Promise<T> {
    if (response.ok) {
      if (response.status === 204 || response.status === 205) return undefined as T
      const type = response.headers.get('content-type') ?? ''
      return (type.includes('application/json') ? await response.json() : await response.text()) as T
    }
    if (response.status === 401) await this.unauthenticated(response)
    const retryAfter = response.status === 429 ? retryAfterSeconds(response.headers.get('retry-after')) : undefined
    throw new ApiFailure(response.status, await errorBody(response), retryAfter)
  }

  /** A 401 `unauthenticated` sends the browser to sign in, and back here
   *  afterwards; on the sign-in screens it is only an error. */
  private async unauthenticated(response: Response): Promise<void> {
    const body = await errorBody(response.clone())
    const { pathname, search } = this.deps.here()
    if (body?.code !== 'unauthenticated' || SIGN_IN_PAGES.includes(pathname)) return
    this.deps.navigate(loginHref(pathname + search))
    throw new Unauthenticated()
  }
}

async function errorBody(
  response: Response,
): Promise<{ code?: string; message?: string; session_id?: string } | undefined> {
  try {
    const body: unknown = await response.json()
    if (body && typeof body === 'object') {
      const { code, message, session_id } = body as Record<string, unknown>
      return {
        code: typeof code === 'string' ? code : undefined,
        message: typeof message === 'string' ? message : undefined,
        // A start whose delivery is unknown names the session it created.
        session_id: typeof session_id === 'string' ? session_id : undefined,
      }
    }
  } catch {
    // Not JSON: the status speaks for itself.
  }
  return undefined
}

async function isStepUpRequired(response: Response): Promise<boolean> {
  if (response.status !== 403) return false
  return (await errorBody(response.clone()))?.code === 'step_up_required'
}
````

Create `web/src/api/device.ts`:

````typescript
import type { AuthSessionItem, PushPolicyItem, PushPolicyRequest, PushSubscribeRequest, PushSubscriptionItem, SettingsUpdateResponse, VapidKeyResponse } from '../generated/protocol'
import type { Client } from './client'
import { ApiFailure } from './errors'
export const pushDevices = (c: Client): Promise<PushSubscriptionItem[]> => c.request('GET', '/api/push/subscriptions')
export const vapid = (c: Client): Promise<VapidKeyResponse> => c.request('GET', '/api/push/vapid')
export const policies = (c: Client): Promise<PushPolicyItem[]> => c.request('GET', '/api/push/policies')
export const savePolicy = (c: Client, id: string, body: PushPolicyRequest): Promise<PushPolicyItem> => c.request('PUT', `/api/push/policies/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`, body)
export const saveContact = (c: Client, contact: string): Promise<SettingsUpdateResponse> => c.request('PATCH', '/api/settings', { contact })
export const authDevices = (c: Client): Promise<AuthSessionItem[]> => c.request('GET', '/api/auth/sessions')
export const revokeDevice = (c: Client, id: string): Promise<void> => c.request('DELETE', `/api/auth/sessions/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`)
export const subscribeDevice = (c: Client, body: PushSubscribeRequest, beforeSend: () => Promise<void>): Promise<PushSubscriptionItem> => c.request('POST', '/api/push/subscriptions', body, { timeoutMs: 10000, beforeSend })
export async function removePush(c: Client, identity: { id?: string; endpoint?: string }): Promise<void> {
  if (!identity.id && !identity.endpoint) return
  try {
    await c.request('DELETE', identity.id ? `/api/push/subscriptions/${encodeURIComponent(identity.id)}` : '/api/push/subscriptions', identity.id ? undefined : { endpoint: identity.endpoint })
  } catch (err) { if (!(err instanceof ApiFailure && err.status === 404)) throw err }
}
````

Create `web/src/components/PushForeground.tsx`:

````tsx
import { useEffect } from 'react'
import { pushDevices, vapid } from '../api/device'
import { useClient } from '../app-client'
import { BrowserPush } from '../pwa/device'
import { pushJournal } from '../pwa/journal'
import { usePwa } from '../pwa/lifecycle'

// Best-effort foreground renewal. Every attempt first reads the server row;
// no missing row can be recreated. One timer only while this page is visible.
export default function PushForeground() {
  const client = useClient(), { registration } = usePwa()
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!registration || typeof Notification === 'undefined') return
    let live = true, running = false, timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined
    const reconcile = async () => {
      if (!live || running || document.visibilityState === 'hidden') return
      running = true; clearTimeout(timer)
      try {
        const [rows, key] = await Promise.all([pushDevices(client), vapid(client)])
        if (!live) return
        const device = new BrowserPush(client, registration, pushJournal(), key.public_key)
        const inspected = await device.reconcile(rows)
        if (live && inspected.record.rotation && inspected.record.repair !== 'sign_in') {
          timer = setTimeout(() => void reconcile(), Math.max(1000, inspected.record.rotation.nextAt - Date.now()))
        }
      } catch { /* Settings shows repair/connection errors. Focus retries. */ }
      finally { running = false }
    }
    const wake = () => void reconcile()
    window.addEventListener('focus', wake); window.addEventListener('online', wake); document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', wake)
    wake()
    return () => { live = false; clearTimeout(timer); window.removeEventListener('focus', wake); window.removeEventListener('online', wake); document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', wake) }
  }, [client, registration])
  return null
}
````

Create `web/src/components/PwaStatus.tsx`:

````tsx
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { usePwa } from '../pwa/lifecycle'
export default function PwaStatus() {
  const [offline, setOffline] = useState(!navigator.onLine)
  const { waiting, cleanupWarning } = usePwa()
  useEffect(() => {
    const update = () => setOffline(!navigator.onLine)
    window.addEventListener('online', update); window.addEventListener('offline', update)
    return () => { window.removeEventListener('online', update); window.removeEventListener('offline', update) }
  }, [])
  return <>{offline && <p className="pwa-banner" role="status">Offline — reconnect to load sessions and answer</p>}
    {waiting && <p className="pwa-banner" role="status">Update available. Close all hennery windows and reopen to update.</p>}
    {cleanupWarning && <p className="pwa-banner" role="alert">{cleanupWarning}</p>}</>
}
````

Replace the whole of `web/src/components/Shell.tsx`:

````tsx
// The signed-in app's frame (frontend spec §2): one component tree for every
// width (F-3). From 768 px a rail with the views beside the content; under
// it a top bar and a bottom tab bar. Which views exist comes from
// `GET /api/capabilities`, which also tells a signed-out browser to sign in.
import { lazy, Suspense, useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { capabilities } from '../api/auth'
import { messageOf } from '../api/errors'
import { useClient } from '../app-client'
import type { CapabilitiesResponse } from '../generated/protocol'
import { LABEL, PATH, tabsOf, viewOf, viewsOf, type View } from '../lib/views'
import { Icon } from '../lib/ui'
import { Link, navigate, type Route } from '../router'
import Hats from '../screens/Hats'
import Hosts from '../screens/Hosts'
import NewSession from '../screens/NewSession'
import Mcp from '../screens/Mcp'
import Placeholder from '../screens/Placeholder'
import PushForeground from './PushForeground'
import SignOut from './SignOut'
import SessionView from '../screens/Session'
import { useMediaQuery } from '../hooks/useMediaQuery'
import SessionList from './SessionList'
import SessionScope, { WaitingBadge, useSessionScope } from './SessionScope'
import McpScope, { McpBadge } from './McpScope'

const Settings = lazy(() => import('../screens/Settings'))

/** The rail's width and up (frontend spec §2). */
export const DESKTOP = '(min-width: 768px)'

const ICON: Record<View, (p: { size?: number }) => React.JSX.Element> = {
  sessions: Icon.List,
  new: Icon.Plus,
  hosts: Icon.Cpu,
  mcp: Icon.Wrench,
  hats: Icon.Sparkle,
  settings: Icon.Shield,
}

/** `/sessions/:id`: the session, its header fed by the list store's summary
 *  (kept current by the list stream) when the list holds it. A session the
 *  view finds gone leaves the list. While the
 *  list's first page is on its way the header waits for it; only a session
 *  the list does not hold fetches its detail. The address is the selection
 *  (F-11, F-19): shown whatever the hat, and whether or not the list holds it. */
function SessionRoute({ id }: { id: string }) {
  const list = useSessionScope()?.list
  return (
    <SessionView
      id={id}
      summary={list?.all.get(id)}
      awaitSummary={!!list && list.loading && !list.error}
      onRemoved={list?.remove}
    />
  )
}

export default function Shell({ route }: { route: Route }) {
  const client = useClient()
  const [caps, setCaps] = useState<CapabilitiesResponse | null>(null)
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [attempt, setAttempt] = useState(0)
  const desktop = useMediaQuery(DESKTOP)

  useEffect(() => {
    let live = true
    setError(null)
    capabilities(client).then(
      (c) => live && setCaps(c),
      (err) => {
        // A 401 has already sent the browser to sign in.
        if (live && !(err instanceof Error && err.name === 'Unauthenticated')) setError(messageOf(err))
      },
    )
    return () => {
      live = false
    }
  }, [client, attempt])
  useEffect(() => {
    if (caps?.mode === 'gateway' && route.name === 'sessions') navigate('/mcp', { replace: true })
  }, [caps?.mode, route.name])
  useEffect(() => {
    const online = () => { if (!caps) setAttempt((n) => n + 1) }
    window.addEventListener('online', online)
    return () => window.removeEventListener('online', online)
  }, [caps])

  if (!caps) {
    return (
      <main className="auth">
        <div className="auth-card" role="status">
          {error ? (
            <>
              <p className="form-error">
                <bdi>{error}</bdi>
              </p>
              <button type="button" className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={() => setAttempt((n) => n + 1)}>
                Try again
              </button>
            </>
          ) : (
            'Loading…'
          )}
        </div>
      </main>
    )
  }

  const views = viewsOf(caps.mode)
  const current = viewOf(route.name)
  const shown = current !== null && views.includes(current)
  const title = route.name === 'not_found' ? 'Not found' : current ? LABEL[current] : 'hennery'

  const frame = (
    <div className="app">
      {caps.features.includes('web_push') && <PushForeground />}
      <aside className="rail" aria-label="Views">
        <div className="rail-head">
          <span className="brand-name">hennery</span>
          <WaitingBadge />
        </div>
        {views.includes('new') && (
          <Link to={PATH.new} className="new-btn">
            <Icon.Plus size={17} /> New session
          </Link>
        )}
        <nav className="rail-nav">
          {views
            .filter((v) => v !== 'new')
            .map((v) => {
              const Glyph = ICON[v]
              return (
                <Link
                  key={v}
                  to={PATH[v]}
                  className={'nav-item' + (v === current ? ' on' : '')}
                  aria-current={v === current ? 'page' : undefined}
                >
                  <Glyph size={17} /> {LABEL[v]}{v === 'mcp' && <McpBadge />}
                </Link>
              )
            })}
        </nav>
        <div className="rail-scroll">{desktop && <SessionList />}</div>
        <div className="rail-foot">
          {route.name !== 'settings' && <SignOut />}
        </div>
      </aside>
      <div className="shell">
        <header className="mtopbar">
          <div className="mtb-mid">
            <div className="mtb-eyebrow">hennery</div>
            <div className="mtb-title">{title}</div>
          </div>
          <WaitingBadge />
          <McpBadge link />
        </header>
        <main className="main">
          {route.name === 'not_found' ? (
            <Placeholder title="Not found" text="No page lives at this address." />
          ) : !shown ? (
            <Placeholder title={title} text="This view is not part of this deployment." />
          ) : route.name === 'hosts' ? (
            <Hosts />
          ) : route.name === 'hats' ? (
            <Hats />
          ) : route.name === 'settings' ? (
            <Suspense fallback={<p role="status">Loading Settings…</p>}><Settings push={caps.features.includes('web_push')} sessions={caps.mode === 'full'} /></Suspense>
          ) : route.name === 'sessions' && !desktop ? (
            <SessionList screen />
          ) : route.name === 'new' ? (
            <NewSession />
          ) : route.name === 'mcp' ? (
            // The gateway's screen exists only where its API is served
            // (4b: a screen behind a feature checks `features`).
            caps.features.includes('mcp_connections') ? (
              <Mcp stdio={caps.features.includes('mcp_stdio')} oauth={caps.features.includes('mcp_oauth')} />
            ) : (
              <Placeholder title={title} text="The MCP gateway is not part of this deployment." />
            )
          ) : route.name === 'session' ? (
            <SessionRoute key={route.id} id={route.id ?? ''} />
          ) : route.name === 'sessions' ? (
            // A desktop at /sessions with no row to open: an empty hat, a
            // search with no match, or before the first page has come.
            <Placeholder title={title} text="Pick a session from the list, or start a new one." />
          ) : (
            <Placeholder title={title} text="This screen arrives in a later part of the web UI." />
          )}
        </main>
        <nav className="tabbar" aria-label="Tabs">
          {tabsOf(caps.mode).map((v) => {
            const Glyph = ICON[v]
            return (
              <Link
                key={v}
                to={PATH[v]}
                className={'tab' + (v === current ? ' on' : '')}
                aria-current={v === current ? 'page' : undefined}
              >
                <Glyph size={20} />
                <span>{v === 'new' ? 'New' : LABEL[v]}</span>{v === 'mcp' && <McpBadge />}
              </Link>
            )
          })}
        </nav>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
  const scoped = views.includes('sessions') ? (
    <SessionScope route={route} desktop={desktop}>
      {frame}
    </SessionScope>
  ) : (
    frame
  )
  return caps.features.includes('mcp_connections') && caps.features.includes('mcp_oauth') ? <McpScope active={route.name === 'mcp'}>{scoped}</McpScope> : scoped
}
````

Replace the whole of `web/src/components/SignOut.tsx`:

````tsx
// Sign out (kernel spec §3.2): `POST /api/auth/logout` ends this browser's
// session, and only once it has does the browser go to the login screen. A
// sign-out that failed (no network, a refusal) says so and stays: the
// cookie is still good, and showing the login screen would claim
// otherwise.
import { useState } from 'react'
import { logOut } from '../api/auth'
import { messageOf } from '../api/errors'
import { Text } from '../lib/text'
import { useClient } from '../app-client'
import { completeLogout } from '../pwa/logout'

export default function SignOut({ className = 'btn btn-ghost btn-sm' }: { className?: string }) {
  const client = useClient()
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)

  const signOut = async () => {
    setBusy(true)
    setError(null)
    try {
      await logOut(client)
    } catch (err) {
      setError(messageOf(err))
      setBusy(false)
      return
    }
    // Signed out: the drafts go with the session, their text in
    // sessionStorage and their images in memory, and a send still in flight
    // is let go of, so no composer waits on it. Only here: a failed
    // sign-out keeps them (still signed in), and so does a 401's redirect to
    // the login screen, so work survives an expired session.
    await completeLogout(client)
  }

  return (
    <>
      <button type="button" className={className} onClick={signOut} disabled={busy}>
        Sign out
      </button>
      {error && (
        <p className="form-error sign-out-error" role="alert">
          Not signed out: this browser is still signed in. <Text>{error}</Text>
        </p>
      )}
    </>
  )
}
````

````css
   before the first paint); these are the defaults. */
````
