Create `web/src/pwa/logout.ts`:

````typescript
import type { Client } from '../api/client'
import { forgetAllAttachments } from '../lib/attachments'
import { forgetAllDrafts } from '../lib/drafts'
import { forgetAllSends } from '../lib/sending'
import { navigate } from '../router'
import { BrowserPush } from './device'
import { pushJournal } from './journal'
import { bounded, setCleanupWarning } from './lifecycle'

// Called only after confirmed logout/current-login revocation, or its
// same-origin broadcast. Expiry's 401 deliberately does not call this.
export async function completeLogout(client: Client, broadcast = true): Promise<void> {
  forgetAllDrafts(); forgetAllAttachments(); forgetAllSends()
  navigate('/login') // Unmount all authenticated stores and question inputs.
  if (broadcast && typeof BroadcastChannel !== 'undefined') {
    const channel = new BroadcastChannel('hennery-auth')
    channel.postMessage({ version: 1, kind: 'logout' }); channel.close()
  }
  let cleanupSucceeded = false
  try {
    if ('serviceWorker' in navigator) {
      const registration = await bounded(navigator.serviceWorker.getRegistration('/'))
      if (registration) {
        const cleanup = new BrowserPush(client, registration, pushJournal(), '').disable(true)
        // bounded() reports uncertainty; it does not cancel cleanup. Correct
        // that report when the real operation subsequently succeeds.
        void cleanup.then((result) => { if (result.browser) { cleanupSucceeded = true; setCleanupWarning(null) } }, () => {})
        const result = await bounded(cleanup)
        if (!result.browser) throw new Error('Browser unsubscribe or notification cleanup failed.')
      } else if (typeof indexedDB !== 'undefined') {
        await bounded(pushJournal().disable())
      }
    }
    setCleanupWarning(null)
  } catch {
    if (!cleanupSucceeded) setCleanupWarning('Signed out. Local notification cleanup is unconfirmed; retry cleanup in Settings after signing in.')
  }
}
````

Create `web/src/pwa/ownership.ts`:

````typescript
// Held across external effects, not an IndexedDB transaction or an expiring
// journal lease. Windows and workers share this origin-wide resource. Never
// steal it: a suspended DELETE/unsubscribe may still have effects on resume.
export function withPushOwnership<T>(work: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
  if (!globalThis.navigator?.locks) return Promise.reject(new Error('Safe notification coordination is unavailable in this browser.'))
  return navigator.locks.request('hennery-push-effects', work)
}
````

Create `web/src/screens/Settings.tsx`:

````tsx
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import { authDevices, policies as listPolicies, pushDevices, removePush, revokeDevice, saveContact, savePolicy, vapid } from '../api/device'
import { hats as listHats, settings } from '../api/manage'
import { ApiFailure, messageOf } from '../api/errors'
import { useClient } from '../app-client'
import SignOut from '../components/SignOut'
import type { AuthSessionItem, HatItem, PushPolicyItem, PushSubscriptionItem } from '../generated/protocol'
import { Text } from '../lib/text'
import { BrowserPush, classifyPush, type PushState } from '../pwa/device'
import { pushJournal, type PushRecord } from '../pwa/journal'
import { registerWorker, usePwa } from '../pwa/lifecycle'
import { completeLogout } from '../pwa/logout'
import { vapidBytes } from '../pwa/subscription'
import '../manage.css'

const COPY: Record<PushState, string> = {
  unsupported: 'This browser does not support notifications. Install the app where supported.',
  insecure: 'Notifications require a secure HTTPS connection.',
  blocked: 'Notifications are blocked by browser or OS settings. Change those settings to enable them.',
  off: 'Notifications are off on this browser.',
  unconfirmed: 'Browser registration is unconfirmed. Enable to register or repair it.',
  reenable: 'Enable again: the device was removed, its subscription changed, or collector keys changed.',
  signed_out: 'This push registration belongs to an expired login. Explicitly enable to bind it to this login.',
  enabled: 'Notifications are enabled on this browser.',
}
const supported = () => 'serviceWorker' in navigator && !!navigator.locks && typeof PushManager !== 'undefined' && typeof Notification !== 'undefined'
const hintDismissed = () => { try { return !!sessionStorage.getItem('hennery.install-hint-dismissed') } catch { return false } }

export default function Settings({ push, sessions }: { push: boolean; sessions: boolean }) {
  const client = useClient(), pwa = usePwa()
  const [devices, setDevices] = useState<PushSubscriptionItem[]>([])
  const [logins, setLogins] = useState<AuthSessionItem[]>([])
  const [hats, setHats] = useState<HatItem[]>([]), [policies, setPolicies] = useState<PushPolicyItem[]>([])
  const [contact, setContact] = useState<string | null>(null), [key, setKey] = useState('')
  const [inspection, setInspection] = useState<{ subscription: PushSubscription | null; record: PushRecord; matches: boolean } | null>(null)
  const [checked, setChecked] = useState<number | null>(null), [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState<string | null>(null), [busy, setBusy] = useState(false), [prepared, setPrepared] = useState(false)
  const [label, setLabel] = useState(''), [hint, setHint] = useState(!hintDismissed())
  const device = useMemo(() => pwa.registration && key ? new BrowserPush(client, pwa.registration, pushJournal(), key) : null, [client, pwa.registration, key])

  const refresh = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const loginRows = await authDevices(client)
      setLogins(loginRows)
      if (push) {
        const observed = pwa.registration && supported() ? await pushJournal().read() : null
        const [rows, loadedKey, config] = await Promise.all([pushDevices(client), vapid(client), settings(client)])
        vapidBytes(loadedKey.public_key)
        if (pwa.registration && supported()) {
          const inspected = await new BrowserPush(client, pwa.registration, pushJournal(), loadedKey.public_key).reconcile(rows, fetch, observed?.generation)
          if (inspected.stale) throw new Error('Another window changed notification settings; refresh to check them.')
          setInspection(inspected)
        }
        setDevices(rows); setKey(loadedKey.public_key); setContact((old) => old ?? config.contact ?? '')
        if (sessions) {
          const [hatRows, policyRows] = await Promise.all([listHats(client), listPolicies(client)])
          setHats(hatRows); setPolicies(policyRows)
        }
      }
      setChecked(Date.now()); setError(null)
    } catch (err) { setError('Unable to check device settings: ' + messageOf(err)) }
  }, [client, push, sessions, pwa.registration])
  useEffect(() => { void refresh() }, [refresh])

  const run = async (action: () => Promise<void>) => {
    setBusy(true); setError(null); setNotice(null)
    try { await action() } catch (err) { setError(messageOf(err)) } finally { setBusy(false) }
  }
  const state = classifyPush({ supported: supported(), secure: globalThis.isSecureContext === true,
    permission: typeof Notification === 'undefined' ? 'default' : Notification.permission,
    subscribed: !!inspection?.subscription, matches: inspection?.matches ?? true,
    record: inspection?.record ?? { generation: 0, enabled: false }, row: devices.find((r) => r.id === inspection?.record.id) })
  const canEnable = !['unsupported', 'insecure', 'blocked'].includes(state) && !!device
  const iosTab = /iPad|iPhone|iPod/.test(navigator.userAgent) && !window.matchMedia('(display-mode: standalone)').matches && !(navigator as Navigator & { standalone?: boolean }).standalone
  const expires = inspection?.subscription?.expirationTime
  const renewable = state === 'enabled' && expires != null && expires <= Date.now() + 86400000

  return <div className="manage device-settings">
    <header className="manage-head"><h1>Settings</h1></header>
    {error && <p role="alert" className="form-error"><Text>{error}</Text>{checked && <> Last checked {new Date(checked).toLocaleString()}.</>}</p>}
    {notice && <p role="status">{notice}</p>}
    <button className="btn btn-ghost" disabled={busy} onClick={() => void run(refresh)}>Refresh settings</button>
    {push && <section className="card" aria-labelledby="browser-push-heading">
      <h2 id="browser-push-heading">Notifications on this browser</h2>
      {hint && <div><p>Install hennery for convenient access. Enable notifications only when you want them.</p>
        <button className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={() => { try { sessionStorage.setItem('hennery.install-hint-dismissed', '1') } catch { /* Nonessential preference. */ } setHint(false) }}>Dismiss install hint</button></div>}
      {iosTab && <p>Use your browser’s Share or menu control, Add to Home Screen, then launch that app and sign in there if needed before enabling notifications.</p>}
      <p role="status">{COPY[state]}</p>
      {!pwa.registration && !pwa.error && supported() && globalThis.isSecureContext === true && <p role="status">Preparing the offline shell…</p>}
      {pwa.error && <p role="alert">{pwa.error} <button className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={() => void registerWorker()}>Retry worker setup</button></p>}
      <label className="field">Device label (optional)<input className="input" value={label} maxLength={64} onChange={(e) => setLabel(e.target.value)} /></label>
      <div className="card-actions">
        {canEnable && (prepared ? <button className="btn btn-primary" disabled={busy} onClick={() => {
          // The permission API is invoked in this tap before ANY await.
          const permission = Notification.permission === 'granted' ? Promise.resolve('granted') : Notification.requestPermission()
          void run(async () => {
            if (await permission !== 'granted') { setPrepared(false); setNotice('Notifications were not enabled. Check browser or OS settings.'); return }
            await device!.enable(label.trim() || undefined); setPrepared(false); await refresh()
          })
        }}>Enable notifications</button> : <button className="btn btn-primary" disabled={busy} onClick={() => void run(async () => { await client.confirm(); setPrepared(true) })}>Prepare notifications</button>)}
        <button className="btn btn-ghost" disabled={busy || !device} onClick={() => void run(async () => {
          const result = await device!.disable(); setPrepared(false)
          setNotice(result.server && result.browser ? 'Notifications disabled.' : !result.server && !result.browser ? 'Collector removal and browser cleanup are unconfirmed. Retry Disable.' : !result.server ? 'Browser unsubscribed; collector removal is unconfirmed. Retry Disable.' : 'Collector registration removed; browser cleanup is unconfirmed. Retry Disable.')
          await refresh()
        })}>Disable notifications</button>
        {renewable && <button className="btn btn-ghost" disabled={busy} onClick={() => void run(async () => { await device!.renew(devices); await refresh() })}>Renew subscription</button>}
      </div>
      <p className="hint">The icon does not guarantee offline storage. An offline first visit needs a successful installation first. Receiving a push does not make the collector reachable.</p>
    </section>}
    {push && <section className="card"><h2>Push devices</h2><p className="hint">These are registrations, not unique physical devices. Removing one stops delivery; it does not sign that browser out.</p>
      <ul className="cards">{devices.map((row) => <li className="card" key={row.id}>
        <h3><Text>{row.device_label}</Text>{row.id === inspection?.record.id && ' (this registration)'}</h3>
        <dl className="facts"><dt>Endpoint host</dt><dd><Text>{row.endpoint_host}</Text></dd><dt>Created</dt><dd>{row.created_at}</dd>
          <dt>Last service acceptance</dt><dd>{row.last_success_at ?? 'None reported'}</dd><dt>Delivery error</dt><dd><Text>{row.last_error ?? 'None reported'}</Text></dd></dl>
        {row.signed_out && <p>Signed out or expired; this registration can still receive push.</p>}
        <p className="hint">Service acceptance does not prove the OS displayed or you read a notification.</p>
        <button className="btn btn-ghost" disabled={busy} onClick={() => void run(async () => {
          if (row.id === inspection?.record.id && device) {
            const result = await device.disable()
            if (!result.server || !result.browser) setNotice('Removal or browser cleanup is unconfirmed. Retry Disable.')
          } else await removePush(client, { id: row.id })
          await refresh()
        })}>Remove <Text>{row.device_label}</Text></button>
      </li>)}</ul>
    </section>}
    <section className="card"><h2>Signed-in devices</h2><p className="hint">Each row is a login. Revoke also removes its push registrations.</p>
      <ul className="cards">{logins.map((row) => <li key={row.id} className="card"><p><Text>{row.user_agent}</Text>{row.current && ' (current login)'}</p>
        <dl className="facts"><dt>Created</dt><dd>{row.created_at}</dd><dt>Last seen</dt><dd>{row.last_seen_at}</dd><dt>Expires</dt><dd>{row.expires_at}</dd></dl>
        <button className="btn btn-ghost" disabled={busy} onClick={() => void run(async () => { await revokeDevice(client, row.id); if (row.current) await completeLogout(client); else await refresh() })}>Revoke {row.current ? 'current login' : 'login'}</button>
      </li>)}</ul>
    </section>
    {push && sessions && <section className="card"><h2>Notification privacy by hat</h2><p>These settings apply to every push device for this hat. Question details are agent text and may contain secrets. A generic title with details still reveals that text.</p>
      {policies.map((policy) => <Policy key={policy.hat_id} policy={policy} name={hats.find((h) => h.id === policy.hat_id)?.name ?? policy.hat_id} onRemove={() => setPolicies((rows) => rows.filter((r) => r.hat_id !== policy.hat_id))} />)}
    </section>}
    {push && contact !== null && <section className="card"><h2>Push contact</h2><p className="hint">The VAPID contact lets a push service contact the collector operator. Saving it changes only the contact.</p>
      <label className="field">Push contact<input className="input" value={contact} onChange={(e) => setContact(e.target.value)} /></label>
      <button className="btn btn-ghost" disabled={busy} onClick={() => void run(async () => { const stored = await saveContact(client, contact); setContact(stored.contact ?? ''); setNotice('Contact saved.') })}>Save contact</button>
    </section>}
    <section className="card"><h2>This login</h2><SignOut className="btn btn-ghost" /></section>
  </div>
}

function Policy({ policy, name, onRemove }: { policy: PushPolicyItem; name: string; onRemove: () => void }) {
  const client = useClient(), [value, setValue] = useState(policy), [busy, setBusy] = useState(false), [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)
  return <fieldset className="card" disabled={busy}><legend><Text>{name}</Text></legend>
    {(['muted', 'generic_title', 'details'] as const).map((key, i) => <label className="policy-control" key={key}>
      <input type="checkbox" checked={value[key]} onChange={(e) => setValue({ ...value, [key]: e.target.checked })} />{['Mute', 'Use a generic title', 'Include question details'][i]}
    </label>)}
    <p>Preview: {value.muted ? 'No notification' : value.generic_title ? 'hennery needs your answer' : 'Example project needs your answer'}{!value.muted && value.details && ' — Example question text (may contain secrets)'}</p>
    {error && <p role="alert"><Text>{error}</Text></p>}
    <button className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={() => {
      setBusy(true); setError(null)
      const { muted, details, generic_title } = value
      void savePolicy(client, policy.hat_id, { muted, details, generic_title }).then(setValue, (err: unknown) => {
        if (err instanceof ApiFailure && err.status === 404) onRemove()
        else setError(messageOf(err))
      }).finally(() => setBusy(false))
    }}>Save policy</button>
  </fieldset>
}
````

### Task 4: Worker-enabled browser acceptance and concurrency hardening

Create `web/e2e/push-fixture.ts`:

````typescript
// Two explicit test boundaries: the page's PushManager service, and an OS
// event harness evaluating the *emitted* production worker. No production
// message endpoint or external vendor transport is involved.
import type { BrowserContext } from '@playwright/test'
import { runInNewContext } from 'node:vm'
import { webcrypto } from 'node:crypto'
import type { PushRecord } from '../src/pwa/journal'
import { testLocks } from '../src/test-locks'

export async function mockPush(context: BrowserContext) {
  await context.addInitScript(() => {
    const from64 = (text: string) => Uint8Array.from(atob(text.replace(/-/g, '+').replace(/_/g, '/')), (c) => c.charCodeAt(0)).buffer
    const to64 = (bytes: ArrayBuffer) => btoa(String.fromCharCode(...new Uint8Array(bytes))).replace(/=/g, '').replace(/\+/g, '-').replace(/\//g, '_')
    let permission: NotificationPermission = localStorage.getItem('mock-permission') as NotificationPermission ?? 'default'
    Object.defineProperty(Notification, 'permission', { get: () => permission })
    Notification.requestPermission = async () => {
      localStorage.setItem('mock-permission-calls', String(Number(localStorage.getItem('mock-permission-calls') ?? '0') + 1))
      permission = localStorage.getItem('mock-denied') ? 'denied' : 'granted'
      localStorage.setItem('mock-permission', permission)
      return permission
    }
    const current = () => {
      const text = localStorage.getItem('mock-subscription')
      if (!text) return null
      const json = JSON.parse(text) as { endpoint: string; key: string; expirationTime: number | null }
      return { endpoint: json.endpoint, expirationTime: json.expirationTime,
        options: { userVisibleOnly: true, applicationServerKey: from64(json.key) },
        toJSON: () => ({ endpoint: json.endpoint, expirationTime: json.expirationTime, keys: { p256dh: json.key, auth: 'AAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAA' } }),
        unsubscribe: async () => { localStorage.removeItem('mock-subscription'); return true },
      }
    }
    Object.defineProperty(PushManager.prototype, 'getSubscription', { value: async () => current() })
    Object.defineProperty(PushManager.prototype, 'subscribe', { value: async (options: PushSubscriptionOptionsInit) => {
      localStorage.setItem('mock-subscription', JSON.stringify({ endpoint: `https://push.example.com/${crypto.randomUUID()}`, key: to64(options.applicationServerKey as ArrayBuffer), expirationTime: null }))
      return current()
    } })
  })
}

export function workerHarness(code: string, persistence = { row: { generation: 0, enabled: false } as PushRecord }, dependencies: { pushManager?: PushManager; locks?: ReturnType<typeof testLocks>; fetch?: typeof fetch; onWrite?: (row: PushRecord) => void } = {}) {
  type Event = { data?: { text(): string }; oldSubscription?: PushSubscription | null; newSubscription?: PushSubscription | null; request?: Request; respondWith?(promise: Promise<Response>): void; waitUntil(promise: Promise<unknown>): void }
  const handlers = new Map<string, (event: Event) => void>()
  const notifications: { title: string; options: unknown }[] = [], fetches: string[] = []
  const attempts: RequestInit[] = []
  // Model persistence beyond worker termination; each transaction reads a
  // copy and commits via put. Production IndexedPushStore is used unchanged.
  const indexedDB = { open: () => {
    const request: { result?: object; onsuccess?: () => void; onupgradeneeded?: () => void } = {}
    request.result = { createObjectStore() {}, close() {}, transaction: () => {
      const tx: { oncomplete?: () => void; onabort?: () => void; objectStore?: () => object; abort?: () => void } = {}
      let aborted = false
      tx.abort = () => { aborted = true; tx.onabort?.() }
      tx.objectStore = () => ({ get: () => {
        const read: { result: PushRecord; onsuccess?: () => void } = { result: structuredClone(persistence.row) }
        queueMicrotask(() => { read.onsuccess?.(); queueMicrotask(() => { if (!aborted) tx.oncomplete?.() }) })
        return read
      }, put: (row: PushRecord) => { persistence.row = structuredClone(row); dependencies.onWrite?.(persistence.row) } })
      return tx
    } }
    queueMicrotask(() => { request.onupgradeneeded?.(); request.onsuccess?.() })
    return request
  } }
  runInNewContext(code, { self: { addEventListener: (name: string, handler: typeof handlers extends Map<string, infer V> ? V : never) => handlers.set(name, handler),
    registration: { pushManager: dependencies.pushManager, showNotification: async (title: string, options: unknown) => { notifications.push({ title, options }) } } },
    navigator: { locks: dependencies.locks ?? testLocks() },
    location: { origin: 'https://app.example' }, URL, Response, Request, crypto: webcrypto, AbortController, setTimeout, clearTimeout, indexedDB, structuredClone, caches: { keys: async () => [] },
    fetch: async (url: string, init: RequestInit) => { fetches.push(url); attempts.push(init); return dependencies.fetch ? dependencies.fetch(url, init) : new Response(JSON.stringify({ id: persistence.row.id })) },
  })
  const dispatch = async (name: string, event: Omit<Event, 'waitUntil'>) => {
    let work: Promise<unknown> | undefined
    handlers.get(name)!({ ...event, waitUntil: (promise) => { work = promise } })
    if (!work) throw new Error(`${name} handler failed to extend event lifetime`)
    await work
  }
  return { notifications, fetches, attempts,
    push: (text: string | null) => dispatch('push', text === null ? {} : { data: { text: () => text } }),
    change: (oldSubscription: PushSubscription | null, newSubscription: PushSubscription | null) => dispatch('pushsubscriptionchange', { oldSubscription, newSubscription }),
    handles(request: Request) {
      let handled = false
      handlers.get('fetch')!({ request, waitUntil() {}, respondWith: (promise) => { handled = true; void promise.catch(() => {}) } })
      return handled
    },
  }
}
````

````typescript
import { expect, test, type BrowserContext, type Page } from '@playwright/test'
import { startCollector, type Collector } from './collector'
import { mockPush, workerHarness } from './push-fixture'
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs'
import { resolve } from 'node:path'

const PASSWORD = 'correct horse battery staple'
let collector: Collector
test.beforeAll(async ({ browser }) => {
  collector = await startCollector()
  const context = await browser.newContext()
  const token = new URL(collector.setupLink).hash.slice(1)
  const response = await context.request.post(`${collector.origin}/api/setup`, { headers: { Origin: collector.origin }, data: { token, password: PASSWORD, public_url: collector.origin, default_hat_name: 'Personal' } })
  expect(response.ok()).toBe(true)
  await context.close()
})
test.afterAll(async () => { await collector?.stop() })
async function login(context: BrowserContext) {
  expect((await context.request.post(`${collector.origin}/api/auth/login`, { headers: { Origin: collector.origin }, data: { password: PASSWORD } })).status()).toBe(204)
}
async function controlled(page: Page) {
  await page.goto(`${collector.origin}/settings`)
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Settings', exact: true })).toBeVisible()
  await page.evaluate(async () => { await navigator.serviceWorker.ready })
  await page.reload()
  await expect.poll(() => page.evaluate(() => !!navigator.serviceWorker.controller)).toBe(true)
}

for (const width of [1280, 390]) test(`embedded PWA and gesture-safe device lifecycle at ${width}px`, async ({ browser }) => {
  const context = await browser.newContext({ viewport: { width, height: 844 }, serviceWorkers: 'allow' })
  try {
    await login(context); await mockPush(context)
    const page = await context.newPage()
    await page.addInitScript(() => { document.addEventListener('securitypolicyviolation', (event) => { ((window as unknown as { csp?: string[] }).csp ??= []).push(event.violatedDirective) }) })
    await controlled(page)
    for (const [path, mime] of [['/sw.js', 'text/javascript'], ['/manifest.webmanifest', 'application/manifest+json']]) {
      const response = await context.request.get(collector.origin + path)
      expect(response.status()).toBe(200)
      expect(response.headers()['content-type']).toContain(mime)
      expect(response.headers()['cache-control']).toBe('no-cache')
      expect(response.headers().etag).toBeTruthy()
      if (path === '/sw.js') expect(response.headers()['content-security-policy']).toBe("default-src 'none'; script-src 'self'; connect-src 'self'")
    }
    const manifest = await (await context.request.get(`${collector.origin}/manifest.webmanifest`)).json()
    expect(manifest).toMatchObject({ id: '/', scope: '/', start_url: '/sessions', display: 'standalone' })
    expect(manifest.icons).toHaveLength(3)
    for (const path of ['/icon-192.png', '/icon-512.png', '/icon-maskable.png', '/icon-180.png']) expect((await context.request.get(collector.origin + path)).headers()['content-type']).toBe('image/png')
    const permissionCalls = () => page.evaluate(() => Number(localStorage.getItem('mock-permission-calls') ?? '0'))
    expect(await permissionCalls()).toBe(0)
    await page.getByLabel('Device label (optional)', { exact: true }).fill(`Phone ${width}`)
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Prepare notifications', exact: true }).click()
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Use password instead', exact: true }).click()
    await page.getByLabel('Password', { exact: true }).fill(PASSWORD)
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Confirm', exact: true }).click()
    expect(await permissionCalls()).toBe(0)
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Enable notifications', exact: true }).click()
    await expect(page.getByText('Notifications are enabled on this browser.', { exact: true })).toBeVisible()
    expect(await permissionCalls()).toBe(1)
    expect(await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Disable notifications', exact: true }).evaluate((el) => el.getBoundingClientRect().height)).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(44)
    const keys = await page.evaluate(async () => (await Promise.all((await caches.keys()).map(async (name) => (await (await caches.open(name)).keys()).map((r) => new URL(r.url).pathname)))).flat().sort())
    expect(keys).toContain('/index.html'); expect(keys).toContain('/theme.js'); expect(keys).toContain('/manifest.webmanifest')
    expect(keys.some((k) => k.includes('Settings-') && k.endsWith('.js'))).toBe(true)
    expect(keys.some((k) => k.startsWith('/api/') || k.startsWith('/mcp/') || k.includes('attachment') || k.includes('logo'))).toBe(false)
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Disable notifications', exact: true }).click()
    await expect(page.getByText('Notifications disabled.', { exact: true })).toBeVisible()
    expect((await context.request.get(`${collector.origin}/api/push/subscriptions`)).ok()).toBe(true)
    await context.setOffline(true); await page.reload()
    await expect(page.getByText('Offline — reconnect to load sessions and answer', { exact: true })).toBeVisible()
    expect(await page.evaluate(() => caches.keys())).toHaveLength(1)
    await context.setOffline(false)
    await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Settings', exact: true })).toBeVisible()
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Sign out', exact: true }).click()
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(`${collector.origin}/login`)
    expect(await page.evaluate(() => (window as unknown as { csp?: string[] }).csp ?? [])).toEqual([])
  } finally { await context.close() }
})
````

Create `web/src/pwa/worker.test.ts`:

````typescript
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs'
import { afterEach, beforeAll, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
import { build } from 'vite'
import { workerHarness } from '../../e2e/push-fixture'
import { deferred, testLocks } from '../test-locks'
import { Journal, type PushRecord, type PushStore } from './journal'
import { BrowserPush } from './device'
import { Client } from '../api/client'

beforeAll(async () => { await build() })
afterEach(() => { vi.useRealTimers(); vi.restoreAllMocks() })

function renewalSetup() {
  const persistence = { row: { generation: 1, enabled: true, id: 'id', endpoint: 'https://push.example/a', key: 'key' } as PushRecord }
  const store: PushStore = { async transact(fn) { return fn(persistence.row) } }
  const journal = new Journal(store), locks = testLocks()
  Object.defineProperty(navigator, 'locks', { configurable: true, value: locks })
  let current: PushSubscription | null = null
  const key = btoa(String.fromCharCode(4, ...Array(64).fill(0)))
  const make = (endpoint: string) => ({ endpoint, options: { applicationServerKey: Uint8Array.from([4, ...Array(64).fill(0)]).buffer },
    toJSON: () => ({ endpoint, keys: { p256dh: 'p', auth: 'a' } }),
    unsubscribe: vi.fn(async () => { if (current?.endpoint === endpoint) current = null; return true }),
  }) as unknown as PushSubscription
  current = make(persistence.row.endpoint!)
  const old = current
  const pushManager = { getSubscription: vi.fn(async () => current), permissionState: vi.fn(async () => 'granted' as PermissionState),
    subscribe: vi.fn(async () => { current = make('https://push.example/b'); return current }),
  } as unknown as PushManager
  const client = new Client({ fetch: vi.fn(async () => new Response(JSON.stringify({ id: 'new-id' }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })), here: () => ({ pathname: '/settings', search: '' }), navigate: vi.fn(), stepUp: vi.fn() })
  const device = new BrowserPush(client, { pushManager, getNotifications: async () => [] } as unknown as ServiceWorkerRegistration, journal, key)
  return { persistence, journal, old, make, pushManager, device, locks, setSubscription: (next: PushSubscription) => { current = next } }
}

it.each(['disabled', 'unknown', 'stale-endpoint'])('worker does not inspect permission for an unowned event: %s', async (state) => {
  const { persistence, pushManager, old, locks } = renewalSetup()
  if (state === 'disabled') persistence.row.enabled = false
  if (state === 'unknown') delete persistence.row.id
  if (state === 'stale-endpoint') persistence.row.endpoint = 'https://push.example/newer'
  const harness = workerHarness(readFileSync('dist/sw.js', 'utf8'), persistence, { pushManager, locks })
  await harness.change(old, null)
  expect(pushManager.permissionState).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
  expect(pushManager.subscribe).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
  expect(harness.fetches).toEqual([])
})

it('worker rotation holds external-effect ownership across an HTTP attempt longer than its lease', async () => {
  const { persistence, pushManager, device, locks, make, setSubscription, journal } = renewalSetup()
  vi.useFakeTimers()
  const posting = deferred(), posted = deferred<Response>()
  const next = make('https://push.example/b'); setSubscription(next)
  const harness = workerHarness(readFileSync('dist/sw.js', 'utf8'), persistence, { pushManager, locks,
    fetch: vi.fn(() => { posting.resolve(); return posted.promise }),
  })
  const change = harness.change(null, next)
  await posting.promise
  await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(13000)
  let enabled = false
  const enabling = device.enable().then(() => { enabled = true })
  await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0)
  expect(enabled).toBe(false)
  posted.resolve(new Response(JSON.stringify({ id: 'id' })))
  await change; await enabling
  expect(await journal.read()).toMatchObject({ enabled: true, id: 'new-id', endpoint: next.endpoint })
})

it('worker renewal releases its operation lease even if replacement serialization throws', async () => {
  const { persistence, pushManager, locks, journal } = renewalSetup()
  const invalid = { endpoint: 'https://push.example/b', toJSON: () => ({ endpoint: 'https://push.example/b' }) } as unknown as PushSubscription
  const harness = workerHarness(readFileSync('dist/sw.js', 'utf8'), persistence, { pushManager, locks })
  await expect(harness.change(null, invalid)).rejects.toThrow('encryption keys')
  expect((await journal.read()).lease).toBeUndefined()
})

it('worker renewal never subscribes after denied permission and records repair', async () => {
  const { persistence, pushManager, old, locks, journal } = renewalSetup()
  vi.mocked(pushManager.permissionState).mockResolvedValueOnce('denied')
  const harness = workerHarness(readFileSync('dist/sw.js', 'utf8'), persistence, { pushManager, locks })
  await harness.change(old, null)
  expect(pushManager.subscribe).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
  expect(await journal.read()).toMatchObject({ enabled: false, repair: 'reenable' })
})

it('a worker browser failure records repair and releases ownership', async () => {
  const { persistence, pushManager, old, locks, journal } = renewalSetup()
  vi.mocked(pushManager.subscribe).mockRejectedValueOnce(new Error('Browser failure'))
  const harness = workerHarness(readFileSync('dist/sw.js', 'utf8'), persistence, { pushManager, locks })
  await harness.change(old, null)
  expect(await journal.read()).toMatchObject({ enabled: false, repair: 'reenable' })
  expect((await journal.read()).lease).toBeUndefined()
})

it('a late worker browser failure cannot clear a newer operation token in the same generation', async () => {
  const { persistence, pushManager, old, locks, journal } = renewalSetup()
  vi.useFakeTimers()
  const subscribing = deferred(), rejected = deferred()
  vi.mocked(pushManager.subscribe).mockImplementationOnce(async () => { subscribing.resolve(); await rejected.promise; throw new Error('Browser failure') })
  const harness = workerHarness(readFileSync('dist/sw.js', 'utf8'), persistence, { pushManager, locks })
  const change = harness.change(old, null)
  await subscribing.promise; await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(13000)
  const newer = await journal.beginRenew()
  rejected.resolve(); await change
  expect((await journal.read()).enabled).toBe(true)
  expect((await journal.read()).lease).toEqual(newer)
})

it('worker renewal rechecks the old endpoint after waiting on permission', async () => {

  const checking = deferred(), permission = deferred<PermissionState>()

})

it('a worker paused on permission cannot subscribe after completed disable', async () => {
  const { persistence, pushManager, old, device, locks } = renewalSetup()
  const checking = deferred(), permission = deferred<PermissionState>()
  vi.mocked(pushManager.permissionState).mockImplementationOnce(() => { checking.resolve(); return permission.promise })

it('a worker permission result cannot replace a newer explicit enable on the same endpoint', async () => {
  const { persistence, pushManager, old, device, locks, make, setSubscription } = renewalSetup()
  const checking = deferred(), permission = deferred<PermissionState>()
  vi.mocked(pushManager.permissionState).mockImplementationOnce(() => { checking.resolve(); return permission.promise })

it('disable between worker lease reservation and subscribe prevents creation', async () => {
  const { persistence, pushManager, old, locks, journal } = renewalSetup()
  let disabled: ReturnType<Journal['disable']> | undefined

})
it('the shipped handler fences identity/generation, persists a supplied replacement and stops without old options', async () => {
  const code = readFileSync('dist/sw.js', 'utf8')
  const replacement = { endpoint: 'https://push.example.com/b', toJSON: () => ({ endpoint: 'https://push.example.com/b', keys: { p256dh: 'key', auth: 'auth' } }) } as unknown as PushSubscription
  const persisted = { row: { generation: 1, enabled: true, id: 'id', endpoint: 'https://push.example.com/a', key: 'key' } }
  const first = workerHarness(code, persisted)
  await first.change(null, replacement)
  expect(first.fetches).toEqual(['/api/push/subscriptions/rotate'])
  expect(persisted.row.endpoint).toBe(replacement.endpoint)
  persisted.row.enabled = false
  const second = workerHarness(code, persisted)
  await second.change(null, replacement)
  expect(second.fetches).toEqual([])
  const missing = workerHarness(code)
  await missing.change(null, replacement); expect(missing.fetches).toEqual([])
  persisted.row.enabled = true
  await workerHarness(code, persisted).change(null, null)
  expect(persisted.row.enabled).toBe(false)
  expect(persisted.row).toHaveProperty('repair', 'reenable')
})
````

Replace the whole of `crates/hennery-kernel/src/csp.rs`:

````rust
//! Kernel spec §7.2: every HTML response carries the
//! `Content-Security-Policy`. One layer over the whole router sets it, so a
//! page cannot be added without it.

use axum::http::{HeaderValue, header};
use axum::response::Response;

/// Kernel spec §7.2's policy. No page has an inline script, so it names no
/// hash: the web UI's theme bootstrap is a file of its own (`/theme.js`).
/// Everything else comes from this origin: scripts, styles, fonts, API
/// calls and form targets (plan 4b).
pub const POLICY: &str = "script-src 'self'; worker-src 'self'; manifest-src 'self'; default-src 'self'; connect-src 'self'; img-src 'self' data: blob:; \
     object-src 'none'; frame-ancestors 'none'; base-uri 'none'; form-action 'self'";

/// The policy of a hat's logo (plan 4d-B2; the security review's A9): it
/// is served from this origin, so opened as a page it must still run
/// nothing, load nothing and be framed nowhere, whatever it holds.
pub const LOGO_POLICY: &str = "default-src 'none'; sandbox; frame-ancestors 'none'";

/// The worker can execute and fetch only from its own origin.
pub const WORKER_POLICY: &str = "default-src 'none'; script-src 'self'; connect-src 'self'; img-src 'self'";

/// Set `POLICY` on a `text/html` response. Use as
/// `axum::middleware::map_response(csp::on_html)`, outermost.
pub async fn on_html(mut response: Response) -> Response {
    let is_html = response
        .headers()
        .get(header::CONTENT_TYPE)
        .and_then(|t| t.to_str().ok())
        .is_some_and(|t| t.trim_start().to_ascii_lowercase().starts_with("text/html"));
    if is_html {
        response
            .headers_mut()
            .insert(header::CONTENT_SECURITY_POLICY, HeaderValue::from_static(POLICY));
    }
    response
}
````

Replace the whole of `crates/hennery-kernel/src/web.rs`:

````rust
//! The web UI, embedded at build time (`build.rs`; kernel spec §7): the
//! router's fallback, so it answers only paths no route claims.
//!
//! - `/api` and `/api/…`, and `/mcp/…` (the gateway's proxy, umbrella §8):
//!   a JSON 404, whatever the method. Never the app.
//! - Any method but `GET` and `HEAD`: a JSON 404.
//! - A file of the build: the file. Hashed files (`/assets/…`) are cached
//!   for good; the rest revalidate with their ETag.
//! - A missing file under `/assets/`: a plain 404, so a stale chunk fails
//!   rather than running the page as a script.
//! - Anything else: `index.html`, whose router shows the route.
//!
//! The page holds no data, so the fallback sits outside the browser rules
//! and the cookie: a link opened from another site loads the app, and the
//! app's API calls are where those rules apply. Every answer is
//! `nosniff`; every file and page is `no-referrer`, `/index.html` asked for
//! by name included, and `/setup`'s `no-store` (kernel spec §3.1). The `Content-Security-Policy` comes from
//! `csp::on_html` over the whole router.

use crate::auth_api::error;
use axum::http::{HeaderMap, HeaderValue, Method, StatusCode, Uri, header};
use axum::response::{IntoResponse, Response};

/// One file of the build, as `build.rs` embeds it.
struct Asset {
    path: &'static str,
    body: &'static [u8],
    content_type: &'static str,
    etag: &'static str,
}

include!(concat!(env!("OUT_DIR"), "/web_assets.rs"));

/// How long a hashed file may be cached: its name changes with its bytes.
const IMMUTABLE: &str = "public, max-age=31536000, immutable";

/// The router's fallback.
pub async fn serve(method: Method, uri: Uri, headers: HeaderMap) -> Response {
    serve_assets(method, uri, headers, FILES)
}

fn serve_assets(method: Method, uri: Uri, headers: HeaderMap, assets: &'static [Asset]) -> Response {
    let find = |path: &str| assets.iter().find(|a| a.path == path);
    let path = uri.path();
    if is_reserved(path) || (method != Method::GET && method != Method::HEAD) {
        let mut response = error(StatusCode::NOT_FOUND, "not_found", "no such route");
        response
            .headers_mut()
            .insert(header::X_CONTENT_TYPE_OPTIONS, HeaderValue::from_static("nosniff"));
        return response;
    }
    if let Some(asset) = find(path) {
        let cache = if path.starts_with("/assets/") {
            IMMUTABLE
        } else {
            "no-cache"
        };
        return file(asset, cache, &headers);
    }
    if path.starts_with("/assets/") || matches!(path, "/sw.js" | "/manifest.webmanifest") {
        return (
            StatusCode::NOT_FOUND,
            [(header::X_CONTENT_TYPE_OPTIONS, "nosniff")],
            "not found",
        )
            .into_response();
    }
    let index = find("/index.html").expect("the build has an index.html");
    if is_setup(path) {
        file(index, "no-store", &HeaderMap::new())
    } else {
        file(index, "no-cache", &headers)
    }
}

/// Paths the app never answers: the API, and the gateway's proxy.
fn is_reserved(path: &str) -> bool {
    path == "/api" || path.starts_with("/api/") || path.starts_with("/mcp/")
}

/// The page a setup link opens: never cached (kernel spec §3.1).
fn is_setup(path: &str) -> bool {
    path == "/setup" || path.starts_with("/setup/")
}

#[cfg(test)]
fn find(path: &str) -> Option<&'static Asset> {
    FILES.iter().find(|a| a.path == path)
}

fn file(asset: &'static Asset, cache: &'static str, request: &HeaderMap) -> Response {
    let fresh = cache == "no-cache"
        && request
            .get(header::IF_NONE_MATCH)
            .and_then(|v| v.to_str().ok())
            .is_some_and(|tags| tags.split(',').any(|t| t.trim() == asset.etag || t.trim() == "*"));
    // A 304 names no type: the cached copy keeps its own.
    let mut response = if fresh {
        StatusCode::NOT_MODIFIED.into_response()
    } else {
        ([(header::CONTENT_TYPE, asset.content_type)], asset.body).into_response()
    };
    let headers = response.headers_mut();
    headers.insert(header::CACHE_CONTROL, HeaderValue::from_static(cache));
    headers.insert(header::X_CONTENT_TYPE_OPTIONS, HeaderValue::from_static("nosniff"));
    headers.insert(header::REFERRER_POLICY, HeaderValue::from_static("no-referrer"));
    if asset.path == "/sw.js" {
        headers.insert(
            header::CONTENT_SECURITY_POLICY,
            HeaderValue::from_static(crate::csp::WORKER_POLICY),
        );
    }
    if cache != "no-store" {
        headers.insert(header::ETAG, HeaderValue::from_static(asset.etag));
    }
    response
}

/// Whether the real UI is embedded, not the placeholder.
pub fn ui_embedded() -> bool {
    EMBEDDED
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn the_api_and_the_gateways_proxy_are_reserved() {
        for path in ["/api", "/api/", "/api/nope", "/mcp/slug", "/mcp/"] {
            assert!(is_reserved(path), "{path}");
        }
        for path in ["/", "/apis", "/mcp", "/sessions/s-1", "/setup"] {
            assert!(!is_reserved(path), "{path}");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_build_has_an_index() {
        let index = find("/index.html").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(index.content_type, "text/html; charset=utf-8");
        assert!(index.etag.starts_with('"') && index.etag.len() == 18, "{}", index.etag);
    }

    #[test]
    fn missing_worker_and_manifest_do_not_fall_back_to_html() {
        static INDEX_ONLY: &[Asset] = &[Asset {
            path: "/index.html",
            body: b"index",
            content_type: "text/html; charset=utf-8",
            etag: "\"index\"",
        }];
        for path in ["/sw.js", "/manifest.webmanifest"] {
            let response = serve_assets(Method::GET, path.parse().unwrap(), HeaderMap::new(), INDEX_ONLY);
            assert_eq!(response.status(), StatusCode::NOT_FOUND);
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_worker_response_has_its_own_policy() {
        static WORKER: Asset = Asset {
            path: "/sw.js",
            body: b"worker",
            content_type: "text/javascript; charset=utf-8",
            etag: "\"worker\"",
        };
        let response = file(&WORKER, "no-cache", &HeaderMap::new());
        assert_eq!(
            response.headers()[header::CONTENT_SECURITY_POLICY],
            crate::csp::WORKER_POLICY
        );
        assert_eq!(response.headers()[header::CACHE_CONTROL], "no-cache");
        assert_eq!(response.headers()[header::ETAG], "\"worker\"");
        assert!(crate::csp::WORKER_POLICY.contains("img-src 'self'"));
        assert!(crate::csp::POLICY.contains("worker-src 'self'") && crate::csp::POLICY.contains("manifest-src 'self'"));
    }
}
````

````rust
//! `GET /api/capabilities` over HTTP (kernel spec §8; plan 4b). Its cookie
//! and browser rules are pinned by `auth.rs`'s route table.

use hennery_kernel::hosts::Hosts;
use hennery_kernel::operator::Operator;
use hennery_proto::rest::{CapabilitiesResponse, DeploymentMode};
use hennery_sessions::AppState;
use hennery_sessions::store::Store;

/// The signed-in owner learns the mode and the features switched on, and
/// nothing else: today the whole cockpit, with the gateway's connections.
#[tokio::test]
async fn the_owner_learns_the_mode_and_the_features() {
    let listener = tokio::net::TcpListener::bind("127.0.0.1:0").await.unwrap();
    let addr = listener.local_addr().unwrap();
    let state = AppState::new(
        Store::open_in_memory().unwrap(),
        Hosts::open_in_memory().unwrap(),
        Operator::open_in_memory().unwrap(),
    );
    let client = hennery_testkit::operator_client(&state.operator);
    tokio::spawn(hennery_sessions::serve(listener, state));
    let resp = client
        .get(format!("http://{addr}/api/capabilities"))
        .send()
        .await
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(resp.status(), 200);
    let body: serde_json::Value = resp.json().await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        body,
        serde_json::json!({"mode": "full", "features": ["mcp_connections", "mcp_stdio", "mcp_oauth", "web_push"]})
    );
    let typed: CapabilitiesResponse = serde_json::from_value(body).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(typed.mode, DeploymentMode::Full);
    for path in [
        "/api/push/vapid",
        "/api/push/subscriptions",
        "/api/push/policies",
        "/api/settings",
    ] {
        let response = client.get(format!("http://{addr}{path}")).send().await.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(response.status(), 200, "advertised web_push: {path}");
    }
}
````

````javascript
import { cpSync, mkdtempSync, readFileSync, readdirSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs'
import { resolve } from 'node:path'
import { pwaBuild } from '../pwa-build.ts'

const root = resolve('web'), out = mkdtempSync(resolve('target/pwa-variant-'))
cpSync(resolve(root, 'dist'), out, { recursive: true })
// A real second embedded shell: every JS chunk and both fixed-name files
// change with status 200 and the expected MIME. Digest rejection, not 404,
// must reject a mid-install mixture.
for (const file of ['index.html', 'theme.js', ...readdirSync(resolve(out, 'assets')).filter((f) => f.endsWith('.js')).map((f) => `assets/${f}`)]) {
  writeFileSync(resolve(out, file), readFileSync(resolve(out, file), 'utf8') + (file.endsWith('.html') ? '\n<!-- pwa test build B -->\n' : '\n/* pwa test build B */\n'))
}
const plugin = pwaBuild()
plugin.configResolved({ root, build: { outDir: out } })
await plugin.closeBundle()
writeFileSync(resolve('target/pwa-variant.json'), JSON.stringify({ dist: out }))
````

Replace the whole of `web/e2e/collector.ts`:

````typescript
// A real collector for the browser checks: the built `hennery` binary
// (`HENNERY_BIN`, else the workspace's debug build), on a fresh data
// directory and a port of its own choosing, read from the setup link it
// writes. Stopped by its own process id. It starts, as every binary the
// browser checks start, through `spawnHennery` (`spawn.ts`).
import { mkdtempSync, readFileSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs'
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os'
import { join } from 'node:path'
import { spawnHennery, type ChildProcess } from './spawn'

export { BIN, scratchEnv } from './spawn'

export interface Collector {
  /** `http://localhost:<port>`. */
  origin: string
  /** The one-time setup link, `<origin>/setup#<token>`. */
  setupLink: string
  stop(): Promise<void>
}

export async function startCollector(binary?: string): Promise<Collector> {
  const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'hennery-e2e-'))
  const child: ChildProcess = spawnHennery(['collector', '--data-dir', dir, '--listen', '127.0.0.1:0'], dir, {
    RUST_LOG: 'warn',
  }, undefined, binary)
  let stderr = ''
  child.stderr?.on('data', (chunk: Buffer) => (stderr += chunk.toString()))
  // A binary that cannot start fails the test that asked for it, rather
  // than the worker.
  child.once('error', (err) => (stderr += `${err}`))
  // Stopped and removed on every path, a failed start included: the
  // directory holds a live setup token.
  const stop = async () => {
    await end(child)
    rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true })
  }
  let setupLink: string
  try {
    setupLink = await waitFor(() => {
      if (!running(child)) {
        throw new Error(`the collector exited (${child.exitCode ?? child.signalCode}): ${stderr}`)
      }
      try {
        return readFileSync(join(dir, 'setup-url'), 'utf8').trim() || null
      } catch {
        return null
      }
    })
  } catch (err) {
    await stop()
    throw err
  }
  return { origin: new URL(setupLink).origin, setupLink, stop }
}

// A process killed by a signal keeps `exitCode` null: `signalCode` says so.
function running(child: ChildProcess): boolean {
  return child.exitCode === null && child.signalCode === null
}

// SIGTERM, then SIGKILL if it has not gone within 5 s.
async function end(child: ChildProcess): Promise<void> {
  if (!running(child)) return
  const gone = new Promise((done) => child.once('exit', done))
  child.kill('SIGTERM')
  const timer = setTimeout(() => child.kill('SIGKILL'), 5000)
  await gone
  clearTimeout(timer)
}

async function waitFor<T>(probe: () => T | null): Promise<T> {
  for (let i = 0; i < 300; i++) {
    const value = probe()
    if (value !== null) return value
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 100))
  }
  throw new Error('the collector wrote no setup link within 30 s')
}
````

Create `web/e2e/pwa-proxy.ts`:

````typescript
// Same-origin deploy fixture. Static bytes come from two *embedded* builds;
// API/auth stays at the first collector. The forwarder is never a push egress.
import { createServer } from 'node:http'
import { type AddressInfo } from 'node:net'
import type { Collector } from './collector'
export async function deployProxy(a: Collector, b: Collector) {
  let variant: 'a' | 'b' | 'mixed' = 'a'
  const reads: { path: string; build: string }[] = []
  const server = createServer(async (request, response) => {
    try {
      const path = new URL(request.url!, 'http://localhost').pathname
      const staticFile = path === '/sw.js' || path === '/index.html' || path === '/theme.js' || path.startsWith('/assets/') || path.endsWith('.png') || path.endsWith('.webmanifest') || path.endsWith('.svg')
      const target = staticFile && (variant === 'b' || variant === 'mixed' && path === '/sw.js') ? b : a
      if (staticFile) reads.push({ path, build: target === b ? 'b' : 'a' })
      const chunks: Buffer[] = []
      for await (const chunk of request) chunks.push(Buffer.from(chunk))
      const body = Buffer.concat(chunks)
      const headers = new Headers()
      for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(request.headers)) if (key !== 'host' && value) headers.set(key, Array.isArray(value) ? value.join(', ') : value)
      const upstream = await fetch(target.origin + request.url, { method: request.method, headers, ...(body.length ? { body } : {}), redirect: 'manual' })
      response.writeHead(upstream.status, Object.fromEntries([...upstream.headers].filter(([key]) => key !== 'transfer-encoding' && key !== 'content-encoding' && key !== 'content-length')))
      // Streaming remains live; no buffering SSE.
      if (upstream.body) for await (const chunk of upstream.body) { if (!response.write(chunk)) await new Promise<void>((resolve) => response.once('drain', resolve)) }
      response.end()
    } catch { if (!response.headersSent) response.writeHead(502); response.end() }
  })
  await new Promise<void>((resolve) => server.listen(0, '127.0.0.1', resolve))
  return { origin: `http://localhost:${(server.address() as AddressInfo).port}`, reads,
    use(next: typeof variant) { variant = next },
    async stop() { server.closeAllConnections(); await new Promise<void>((resolve) => server.close(() => resolve())) },
  }
}
````

````typescript
import { expect, test, type BrowserContext, type Page } from '@playwright/test'
import { startCollector, type Collector } from './collector'
import { mockPush, workerHarness } from './push-fixture'
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs'
import { resolve } from 'node:path'

const PASSWORD = 'correct horse battery staple'
let collector: Collector
let host: TestHost
test.beforeAll(async ({ browser }) => {
  collector = await startCollector()
  const context = await browser.newContext()
  const token = new URL(collector.setupLink).hash.slice(1)
  const response = await context.request.post(`${collector.origin}/api/setup`, { headers: { Origin: collector.origin }, data: { token, password: PASSWORD, public_url: collector.origin, default_hat_name: 'Personal' } })
  expect(response.ok()).toBe(true)
  host = testHost(fakeAgent('claude', { asks: [], chunks: ['PWA acceptance agent'], chunk_delay_ms: 0 }))
  const pairing = await context.request.post(`${collector.origin}/api/hosts/pairing-codes`, { headers: { Origin: collector.origin } })
  expect(pairing.ok()).toBe(true)
  expect(await host.join(`hennery host join ${collector.origin} ${(await pairing.json()).code}`, ['--name', 'pwa fake host', '--no-runtime'])).toBe(0)
  host.run()
  await context.close()
})
test.afterAll(async () => { try { await host?.stop() } finally { await collector?.stop() } })
async function login(context: BrowserContext) {
  expect((await context.request.post(`${collector.origin}/api/auth/login`, { headers: { Origin: collector.origin }, data: { password: PASSWORD } })).status()).toBe(204)
}
async function controlled(page: Page) {
  await page.goto(`${collector.origin}/settings`)
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Settings', exact: true })).toBeVisible()
  await page.evaluate(async () => { await navigator.serviceWorker.ready })
  await page.reload()
  await expect.poll(() => page.evaluate(() => !!navigator.serviceWorker.controller)).toBe(true)
}

    expect((await context.request.get(`${collector.origin}/api/push/subscriptions`)).ok()).toBe(true)
    await context.setOffline(true); await page.reload()
    await expect(page.getByText('Offline — reconnect to load sessions and answer', { exact: true })).toBeVisible()
    expect(await page.evaluate(() => caches.keys())).toHaveLength(1)
    await context.setOffline(false)
    await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Settings', exact: true })).toBeVisible()
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Sign out', exact: true }).click()
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(`${collector.origin}/login`)
    expect(await page.evaluate(() => (window as unknown as { csp?: string[] }).csp ?? [])).toEqual([])
  } finally { await context.close() }
})

  const harness = workerHarness(readFileSync(resolve('dist/sw.js'), 'utf8'))
  await harness.push(JSON.stringify({ title: 'Needs your answer', body: 'Example question', tag: 's-1', url: '/sessions/s-1' }))

  expect(harness.handles(new Request('https://app.example/index.html?secret=1'))).toBe(false)

  await harness.change(null, replacement)
  expect(harness.fetches).toEqual([])
  const persisted = { row: { generation: 1, enabled: true, id: 'id', endpoint: 'https://push.example.com/a', key: 'key' } }
  const restarted = workerHarness(readFileSync(resolve('dist/sw.js'), 'utf8'), persisted)
  await restarted.change(null, replacement)
  expect(restarted.fetches).toEqual(['/api/push/subscriptions/rotate'])

  expect(persisted.row.endpoint).toBe('https://push.example.com/b')
  persisted.row.enabled = false
  await workerHarness(readFileSync(resolve('dist/sw.js'), 'utf8'), persisted).change(null, replacement)
  expect(persisted.row.enabled).toBe(false)
})

test('mixed digest install fails, preserving the old offline shell; a complete new build waits with two dirty windows', async ({ browser }) => {
  const a = await startCollector(resolve('../target/hennery-pwa-base'))
  const b = await startCollector(resolve('../target/hennery-pwa-update'))
  const proxy = await deployProxy(a, b)
  const context = await browser.newContext({ serviceWorkers: 'allow' })
  try {
    expect((await context.request.post(`${proxy.origin}/api/setup`, { headers: { Origin: proxy.origin }, data: { token: new URL(a.setupLink).hash.slice(1), password: PASSWORD, public_url: proxy.origin, default_hat_name: 'Personal' } })).ok()).toBe(true)
    const page = await context.newPage()
    await page.goto(`${proxy.origin}/settings`)
    await page.evaluate(async () => { await navigator.serviceWorker.ready })
    await page.reload()
    expect(await page.evaluate(() => !!navigator.serviceWorker.controller)).toBe(true)
    const oldCaches = await page.evaluate(() => caches.keys())
    expect(oldCaches).toHaveLength(1)
    const other = await context.newPage(); await other.goto(`${proxy.origin}/settings`)
    expect(await other.evaluate(() => !!navigator.serviceWorker.controller)).toBe(true)
    await page.getByLabel('Push contact', { exact: true }).fill('mailto:unsent@example.invalid')
    await other.getByLabel('Push contact', { exact: true }).fill('mailto:other-unsent@example.invalid')
    proxy.use('mixed')
    // Actual expected-MIME 200s from A under B's expected digests.
    const failed = await page.evaluate(async () => {
      const registration = (await navigator.serviceWorker.getRegistration('/'))!
      return new Promise<string>((resolve) => {
        registration.addEventListener('updatefound', () => {
          const installing = registration.installing!
          installing.addEventListener('statechange', () => { if (installing.state === 'redundant') resolve('rejected') })
        }, { once: true })
        void registration.update()
      })
    })
    expect(failed).toBe('rejected')
    expect(proxy.reads.some((r) => r.path === '/sw.js' && r.build === 'b')).toBe(true)
    expect(proxy.reads.some((r) => r.path === '/index.html' && r.build === 'a')).toBe(true)
    expect(await page.evaluate(() => caches.keys())).toEqual(oldCaches)
    await context.setOffline(true); await page.reload()
    await expect(page.getByText('Offline — reconnect to load sessions and answer', { exact: true })).toBeVisible()
    await context.setOffline(false)
    await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Settings', exact: true })).toBeVisible()
    await page.getByLabel('Push contact', { exact: true }).fill('mailto:unsent@example.invalid')
    proxy.use('b')
    expect(await page.evaluate(() => !!navigator.serviceWorker.controller)).toBe(true)
    expect(await other.evaluate(() => !!navigator.serviceWorker.controller)).toBe(true)
    await page.evaluate(async () => { await (await navigator.serviceWorker.getRegistration('/'))!.update() })
    await expect.poll(() => page.evaluate(async () => !!(await navigator.serviceWorker.getRegistration('/'))?.waiting)).toBe(true)
    await expect(page.getByText('Update available. Close all hennery windows and reopen to update.', { exact: true })).toBeVisible()
    await expect(page.getByLabel('Push contact', { exact: true })).toHaveValue('mailto:unsent@example.invalid')
    await expect(other.getByLabel('Push contact', { exact: true })).toHaveValue('mailto:other-unsent@example.invalid')
    expect(await page.evaluate(async () => (await navigator.serviceWorker.getRegistration('/'))!.active!.state)).toBe('activated')
    expect(await page.evaluate(() => caches.keys())).toHaveLength(2)
    await page.close(); await other.close()
    const next = await context.newPage(); await next.goto(`${proxy.origin}/settings`)
    await expect.poll(() => next.evaluate(async () => (await caches.keys()).length)).toBe(1)
    expect(await next.evaluate(async () => (await fetch('/index.html')).text())).toContain('pwa test build B')
  } finally { await context.close(); await proxy.stop(); await a.stop(); await b.stop() }
})
````

Replace the whole of `web/e2e/spawn.ts`:

````typescript
// The one place the browser checks start a process (a guard in
// `src/security.test.ts` holds every other file of `e2e/` to that): the
// built `hennery` binary, always in `scratchEnv`, so nothing of the
// runner's own hennery setup, home or XDG directories reaches it.
import { spawn, type ChildProcess, type StdioOptions } from 'node:child_process'
import { join, resolve } from 'node:path'

export type { ChildProcess }

export const BIN = process.env.HENNERY_BIN ?? resolve(process.cwd(), '../target/debug/hennery')

/** What a child may take from the runner's environment: where programs
 *  are, the locale and time zone, and the temporary directory. */
export const INHERITED = ['PATH', 'LANG', 'LC_ALL', 'LC_CTYPE', 'TZ', 'TMPDIR'] as const

/** A child's environment, built from an allowlist: `INHERITED` from the
 *  runner, and its home and XDG directories under `dir`. Nothing else of the
 *  runner's reaches it: no `HENNERY_*` variable (a data or log directory, a
 *  service flag), no `CODEX_HOME`, no token. A test adds what it needs
 *  through `extra`. */
export function scratchEnv(dir: string): NodeJS.ProcessEnv {
  const env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv = {}
  for (const key of INHERITED) if (process.env[key] !== undefined) env[key] = process.env[key]
  return {
    ...env,
    HOME: dir,
    XDG_DATA_HOME: join(dir, 'xdg-data'),
    XDG_CONFIG_HOME: join(dir, 'xdg-config'),
    XDG_CACHE_HOME: join(dir, 'xdg-cache'),
    XDG_STATE_HOME: join(dir, 'xdg-state'),
  }
}

/** `hennery <args>` in `scratchEnv(dir)`, plus `extra` (which may not
 *  name a `HENNERY_*` variable the test did not choose itself). */
export function spawnHennery(
  args: string[],
  dir: string,
  extra: NodeJS.ProcessEnv,
  stdio: StdioOptions = ['ignore', 'ignore', 'pipe'],
  binary = BIN,
): ChildProcess {
  return spawn(binary, args, { stdio, env: { ...scratchEnv(dir), ...extra } })
}
````

Replace the whole of `web/pwa-build.ts`:

````typescript
// Runs after Vite has emitted every chunk. The worker isn't part of its own
// precache. No network-generated URL or query parameter enters this list.
import { createHash } from 'node:crypto'
import { readFileSync, readdirSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs'
import { resolve } from 'node:path'
import { deflateSync } from 'node:zlib'
import { build, type Plugin } from 'vite'

const hash = (bytes: string | Uint8Array) => createHash('sha256').update(bytes).digest('hex')
function png(size: number): Buffer {
  const crc = (bytes: Buffer) => {
    let c = 0xffffffff
    for (const b of bytes) { c ^= b; for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) c = (c >>> 1) ^ ((c & 1) ? 0xedb88320 : 0) }
    return (c ^ 0xffffffff) >>> 0
  }
  const chunk = (kind: string, data: Buffer) => {
    const body = Buffer.concat([Buffer.from(kind), data]), length = Buffer.alloc(4), check = Buffer.alloc(4)
    length.writeUInt32BE(data.length); check.writeUInt32BE(crc(body))
    return Buffer.concat([length, body, check])
  }
  const header = Buffer.alloc(13)
  header.writeUInt32BE(size); header.writeUInt32BE(size, 4); header[8] = 8; header[9] = 6
  const pixels = Buffer.alloc((size * 4 + 1) * size)
  // A simple h inside the central 60%, safe within a maskable icon's circle.
  for (let y = 0; y < size; y++) for (let x = 0; x < size; x++) {
    const u = x / size, v = y / size
    const mark = (u > .30 && u < .40 && v > .25 && v < .75)
      || (u > .40 && u < .65 && v > .46 && v < .56)
      || (u > .55 && u < .65 && v > .50 && v < .75)
    const offset = y * (size * 4 + 1) + 1 + x * 4
    pixels.set(mark ? [245, 245, 240, 255] : [30, 39, 35, 255], offset)
  }
  return Buffer.concat([Buffer.from([137,80,78,71,13,10,26,10]), chunk('IHDR', header), chunk('IDAT', deflateSync(pixels)), chunk('IEND', Buffer.alloc(0))])
}

export function pwaBuild(): Plugin {
  let root = '', out = ''
  return {
    name: 'hennery-pwa', apply: 'build',
    configResolved(config) { root = config.root; out = resolve(root, config.build.outDir) },
    async closeBundle() {
      for (const size of [180, 192, 512]) writeFileSync(resolve(out, `icon-${size}.png`), png(size))
      writeFileSync(resolve(out, 'icon-maskable.png'), png(512))
      const mime: Record<string, string> = { html: 'text/html', js: 'text/javascript', css: 'text/css', svg: 'image/svg+xml', png: 'image/png', webmanifest: 'application/manifest+json', woff2: 'font/woff2' }
      const entries: { url: string; mime: string; digest: string }[] = []
      const visit = (dir: string, prefix: string) => {
        for (const file of readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true })) {
          if (file.isDirectory()) visit(resolve(dir, file.name), `${prefix}${file.name}/`)
          else if (mime[file.name.split('.').at(-1)!] && file.name !== 'sw.js') entries.push({ url: `${prefix}${file.name}`, mime: mime[file.name.split('.').at(-1)!], digest: hash(readFileSync(resolve(dir, file.name))) })
        }
      }
      visit(out, '/')
      entries.sort((a, b) => a.url.localeCompare(b.url))
      await build({ configFile: false, publicDir: false, root, define: { __PRECACHE__: JSON.stringify(entries), __BUILD_ID__: JSON.stringify(hash(JSON.stringify(entries))) },
        build: { outDir: out, emptyOutDir: false, target: 'es2022', lib: { entry: resolve(root, 'worker.js'), name: 'henneryWorker', formats: ['iife'], fileName: () => 'sw.js' } } })
    },
  }
}
````

Replace the whole of `web/src/components/PushForeground.tsx`:

````tsx
import { useEffect } from 'react'
import { pushDevices, vapid } from '../api/device'
import { useClient } from '../app-client'
import { BrowserPush } from '../pwa/device'
import { pushJournal } from '../pwa/journal'
import { usePwa } from '../pwa/lifecycle'

// Best-effort foreground renewal. Every attempt first reads the server row;
// no missing row can be recreated. One timer only while this page is visible.
export default function PushForeground() {
  const client = useClient(), { registration } = usePwa()
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!registration || typeof Notification === 'undefined') return
    let live = true, running = false, timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined
    const reconcile = async () => {
      if (!live || running || document.visibilityState === 'hidden') return
      running = true; clearTimeout(timer)
      try {
        const observed = await pushJournal().read()
        const [rows, key] = await Promise.all([pushDevices(client), vapid(client)])
        if (!live) return
        const device = new BrowserPush(client, registration, pushJournal(), key.public_key)
        const inspected = await device.reconcile(rows, fetch, observed.generation)
        if (live && inspected.record.rotation && inspected.record.repair !== 'sign_in') {
          timer = setTimeout(() => void reconcile(), Math.max(1000, inspected.record.rotation.nextAt - Date.now()))
        }
      } catch { /* Settings shows repair/connection errors. Focus retries. */ }
      finally { running = false }
    }
    const wake = () => void reconcile()
    window.addEventListener('focus', wake); window.addEventListener('online', wake); document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', wake)
    wake()
    return () => { live = false; clearTimeout(timer); window.removeEventListener('focus', wake); window.removeEventListener('online', wake); document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', wake) }
  }, [client, registration])
  return null
}
````

````typescript
// The browser checks start processes in one place only, `e2e/spawn.ts`,
// whose one `spawn` runs the binary in `scratchEnv`: a check that started
// its own would hand the binary the runner's home and any `HENNERY_*`
// variable, and with them the operator's own data and log directories.
import { existsSync, readFileSync, readdirSync, statSync } from 'node:fs'
import { join, relative } from 'node:path'
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { INHERITED, scratchEnv } from '../e2e/spawn'

// Vitest runs from `web/`.
const ROOT = process.cwd()
const HELPER = join('e2e', 'spawn.ts')
// A call of any of these, not as a method of something else, or the module.
const STARTS = /(?<![.\w])(spawn|spawnSync|exec|execSync|execFile|execFileSync|fork)\s*\(|child_process/

function files(dir: string): string[] {
  if (!existsSync(dir)) return []
  return readdirSync(dir).flatMap((name) => {
    const path = join(dir, name)
    if (statSync(path).isDirectory()) return files(path)
    return /\.(ts|tsx|js|mjs|cjs)$/.test(name) ? [path] : []
  })
}

describe('the browser checks', () => {
  const all = [...files(join(ROOT, 'e2e')), ...files(join(ROOT, 'scripts'))]

  it('are all read', () => {
    expect(all.map((f) => relative(ROOT, f))).toContain(HELPER)
    expect(all.length).toBeGreaterThan(4)
  })

  it('start a process only through spawn.ts', () => {
    const found = all.filter((f) => relative(ROOT, f) !== HELPER && STARTS.test(readFileSync(f, 'utf8')))
    expect(found.map((f) => relative(ROOT, f))).toEqual([])
  })

  it('start it, there, only in scratchEnv', () => {
    const helper = readFileSync(join(ROOT, HELPER), 'utf8')
    expect(helper.match(/(?<![.\w])spawn\s*\(/g)).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(helper).toContain('spawn(binary, args, { stdio, env: { ...scratchEnv(dir), ...extra } })')
    expect(helper).toContain('binary = BIN')
  })
})

describe('a child of the browser checks', () => {
  const set = (key: string, value: string | undefined) => {
    if (value === undefined) delete process.env[key]
    else process.env[key] = value
  }
  const saved = ['HENNERY_DATA_DIR', 'HENNERY_LOG_DIR', 'CODEX_HOME', 'E2E_UNEXPECTED', 'PATH'].map(
    (key) => [key, process.env[key]] as const,
  )
  afterEach(() => saved.forEach(([key, value]) => set(key, value)))

  it('gets only the allowlisted variables of the runner, and its scratch home', () => {
    set('HENNERY_DATA_DIR', '/runner/data')
    set('HENNERY_LOG_DIR', '/runner/logs')
    set('CODEX_HOME', '/runner/codex')
    set('E2E_UNEXPECTED', 'secret')
    set('PATH', '/usr/bin:/bin')
    const env = scratchEnv('/scratch')
    const scratch = ['HOME', 'XDG_DATA_HOME', 'XDG_CONFIG_HOME', 'XDG_CACHE_HOME', 'XDG_STATE_HOME']
    expect(Object.keys(env).every((key) => (INHERITED as readonly string[]).includes(key) || scratch.includes(key))).toBe(true)
    expect(env.E2E_UNEXPECTED).toBeUndefined()
    expect(env.HENNERY_DATA_DIR).toBeUndefined()
    expect(env.HENNERY_LOG_DIR).toBeUndefined()
    expect(env.CODEX_HOME).toBeUndefined()
    expect(env.PATH).toBe('/usr/bin:/bin')
    expect(env.HOME).toBe('/scratch')
    for (const key of scratch.slice(1)) expect(env[key]?.startsWith('/scratch/')).toBe(true)
  })
})
````

Replace the whole of `web/src/pwa/lifecycle.ts`:

````typescript
import { useSyncExternalStore } from 'react'
interface PwaStatus { registration: ServiceWorkerRegistration | null; error: string | null; waiting: boolean; cleanupWarning: string | null }
const rememberedCleanup = () => { try { return sessionStorage.getItem('hennery.logout-cleanup') } catch { return null } }
let status: PwaStatus = { registration: null, error: null, waiting: false, cleanupWarning: rememberedCleanup() }
const listeners = new Set<() => void>()
const set = (change: Partial<PwaStatus>) => { status = { ...status, ...change }; listeners.forEach((f) => f()) }
let pending: Promise<void> | null = null
export const usePwa = () => useSyncExternalStore((fn) => { listeners.add(fn); return () => { listeners.delete(fn) } }, () => status)
export const setCleanupWarning = (warning: string | null) => {
  try {
    if (warning) sessionStorage.setItem('hennery.logout-cleanup', warning)
    else sessionStorage.removeItem('hennery.logout-cleanup')
  } catch { /* Still show the result when browser storage is unavailable. */ }
  set({ cleanupWarning: warning })
}

export function bounded<T>(work: Promise<T>, ms = 10000): Promise<T> {
  let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>
  return Promise.race([work, new Promise<T>((_resolve, reject) => { timer = setTimeout(() => reject(new Error('Worker setup timed out. Close other windows and retry.')), ms) })]).finally(() => clearTimeout(timer))
}
export function registerWorker(): Promise<void> {
  if (globalThis.isSecureContext !== true || !('serviceWorker' in navigator)) return Promise.resolve()
  if (pending) return pending
  set({ error: null })
  pending = (async () => {
    try {
      const registration = await bounded(navigator.serviceWorker.register('/sw.js', { scope: '/', updateViaCache: 'none' }))
      const check = () => set({ waiting: !!registration.waiting })
      registration.addEventListener('updatefound', () => {
        const worker = registration.installing
        worker?.addEventListener('statechange', () => {
          check()
          if (worker.state === 'redundant') set({ error: 'Offline shell verification or storage failed. Retry worker setup.' })
        })
        check()
      })
      await bounded(new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
        if (registration.active?.state === 'activated') { resolve(); return }
        const worker = registration.installing ?? registration.waiting ?? registration.active
        if (!worker) { reject(new Error('Offline shell installation failed.')); return }
        const change = () => {
          if (worker.state === 'activated') { worker.removeEventListener('statechange', change); resolve() }
          if (worker.state === 'redundant') { worker.removeEventListener('statechange', change); reject(new Error('Offline shell verification or storage failed.')) }
        }
        worker.addEventListener('statechange', change); change()
      }))
      set({ registration, waiting: !!registration.waiting })
      await registration.update()
    } catch (err) { set({ error: err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Worker setup failed.' }) }
    finally { pending = null }
  })()
  return pending
}
````

````typescript
import type { PushPayload } from '../generated/protocol'
const neutral: PushPayload = { title: 'hennery needs your attention', body: 'Open hennery to check the current state.', url: '/sessions', tag: 'hennery-attention' }

// Canonical literal paths only. P2 owns card targeting and window navigation.
export function notificationTarget(value: unknown): string {
  if (typeof value !== 'string' || value.length > 512 || /[\\#\s]/.test(value)) return '/sessions'
  try {
    const parsed = new URL(value, 'https://hennery.invalid')
    if (parsed.origin !== 'https://hennery.invalid' || parsed.pathname + parsed.search !== value) return '/sessions'
  } catch { return '/sessions' }
  if (value === '/mcp' || value === '/sessions') return value
  return /^\/sessions\/[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,128}(\?pending=[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,128})?$/.test(value) ? value : '/sessions'
}
export function decodePush(text: string | null): PushPayload {
  if (!text || text.length > 8192) return { ...neutral }
  try {
    const p: unknown = JSON.parse(text)
    if (!p || typeof p !== 'object') return { ...neutral }
    const fields = p as Record<string, unknown>
    const bounded = (v: unknown, max: number) => typeof v === 'string' && v.length <= max
    if (!bounded(fields.title, 256) || !bounded(fields.body, 2048) || !bounded(fields.tag, 256)) return { ...neutral }
    return { title: fields.title as string, body: fields.body as string, tag: fields.tag as string, url: notificationTarget(fields.url) }
  } catch { return { ...neutral } }
}
````
